/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[gen_bill]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:33:35 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[gen_bill]
    @bill_type INT = 0,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE
        @today INT,
        @bill_id INT,
        @last_bill_id INT = NULL,   -- last run actually created; NULL if none
        @rate FLOAT,
        @parking FLOAT,
        @due_days INT,
        @interest DECIMAL(18,2),
        @msg NVARCHAR(MAX),
        @bill_charges_ids NVARCHAR(100),
        @setting_charges_ids NVARCHAR(100);

    

    -- Society cursor
    DECLARE society_cursor CURSOR FOR
    SELECT 
        society_id,
        CASE 
            WHEN bill_gen_date BETWEEN (DATEPART(DAY, DATEADD(DAY, 1 - DAY(GETDATE()), GETDATE()))) 
                 AND DAY(EOMONTH(GETDATE())) 
            THEN bill_gen_date 
            ELSE DAY(EOMONTH(GETDATE())) 
        END AS today,
        bill_due_period,
        ISNULL(interest_rate, 0)
    FROM account_setting
    WHERE (auto_bill_generation = CASE WHEN @bill_type = 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
      AND (@society_id IS NULL OR society_id = @society_id);

    OPEN society_cursor;
    FETCH NEXT FROM society_cursor INTO @society_id, @today, @due_days, @interest;

    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
        IF NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 
            FROM maintenance_cal 
            WHERE MONTH(gen_date) = MONTH(GETDATE())  AND YEAR(gen_date) = YEAR(GETDATE())
              AND bill_type = 1 
              AND society_id = @society_id
        )
        AND (@today = DATEPART(DAY, GETDATE()) OR @bill_type = 1)
        BEGIN
            -- Held until the insert lands. gen_bill walks every society in one
            -- cursor, so two runs reading MAX at the same moment would both
            -- take the same bill_id and their bills would merge into one run.
            SELECT @bill_id = ISNULL(MAX(bill_id), 0) + 1
            FROM   maintenance_cal WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK);

            DECLARE @flat_count INT, @total_billed DECIMAL(18,2) = 0;
            SELECT @flat_count = COUNT(DISTINCT flat_id)
            FROM flat_master
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id;

            SELECT @rate = rate_per_sqfeet 
            FROM account_setting 
            WHERE society_id = @society_id;

            DECLARE flat_cursor CURSOR FOR
            SELECT flat_id, sq_ft
            FROM owner_search_vw 
            WHERE society_id = @society_id 
              AND type = 'Owner';

            DECLARE @flat_id INT, @sqft FLOAT;

            OPEN flat_cursor;
            FETCH NEXT FROM flat_cursor INTO @flat_id, @sqft;

            WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
            BEGIN
                DECLARE 
                    @total_amt DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
                    @amt_forward DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
                    @tax_interest DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
                    @prev_due DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
					  @prev_tax DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
                    @due DECIMAL(18,2) = 0;

                -- Shared maintenance per flat
                SELECT @total_amt = ISNULL(SUM(amount / NULLIF(@flat_count, 0)), 0)
                FROM maintenance_charges
                WHERE status = 1 AND charges_type = 1 AND society_id = @society_id;

                -- Add sqft and parking
                SELECT @parking = SUM(
                    CASE 
                        WHEN v.vehicle_type = 0 THEN a.two_wheeler_rate
                        WHEN v.vehicle_type = 1 THEN a.four_wheeler_rate
                        ELSE 0 
                    END)
                FROM Vehicle v
                INNER JOIN account_setting a ON v.society_id = a.society_id
                WHERE v.flat_id = @flat_id AND v.society_id = @society_id 
                      AND ISNULL(v.park_place_id, 0) != 0;

                SET @total_amt +=  ISNULL(@parking, 0);

                -- Deduct advance
                --
                -- Not ABS(): that flips the sign, so an advance larger than the
                -- charge came back as money owed. Flat 43 holds 4,439.24 against
                -- a 1,100.00 bill — ABS made that 3,339.24 payable when the
                -- resident is 3,339.24 in credit. Clamped at zero instead; the
                -- surplus stays where it is rather than being billed.
                --
                -- society_id matters too: flat_id is not unique across
                -- societies, so without it another society's advance could be
                -- deducted from this one's bill.
                SELECT @total_amt = @total_amt - ISNULL(SUM(advance), 0)
                FROM   maintenance_cal
                WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id AND society_id = @society_id;

                IF @total_amt < 0 SET @total_amt = 0;
				 
                SET @due = @total_amt;

                -- Previous dues + interest
                --
                -- Scoped to the society for the same reason as the advance
                -- above: flat_id alone would pull another society's arrears
                -- onto this bill the moment two of them share a number.
                SELECT @prev_due = ISNULL(SUM(due), 0)
                FROM   maintenance_cal
                WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id
                  AND  society_id = @society_id
                  AND  due_date < DATEADD(DAY, -1, GETDATE());
			
                IF @prev_due > 0
                BEGIN
                    SET @amt_forward = @prev_due;
                    SET @tax_interest =round( ((@prev_due * @interest) / 100)/365* 31,2);
                    --SET @total_amt += @amt_forward + @tax_interest;
				-- 	set @tax_interest= @prev_tax+@tax_interest;
                END;

                -- Get last audit IDs correctly (no nested aggregates)
                --
                -- Joined to the live table. The audit is a history: every head
                -- keeps its old rows, so selecting from it alone put charges
                -- retired months ago onto the bill. There was no status test
                -- here at all, so a deactivated regular head printed forever.
                -- The live table decides what is billed; the audit only says
                -- which version of it.
                ;WITH ChargesAgg AS (
                    SELECT MAX(a.audit_id) AS max_audit_id
                    FROM maintenance_charges_audit a
                    INNER JOIN maintenance_charges c
                            ON c.charge_id = a.charge_id AND c.society_id = a.society_id
                    WHERE a.charges_type = 1 AND a.society_id = @society_id
                      AND c.status = 1 AND c.charges_type = 1
                    GROUP BY a.charge_id, a.society_id
                )
                SELECT @bill_charges_ids = STRING_AGG(CAST(max_audit_id AS NVARCHAR(10)), ',')
                FROM ChargesAgg;

                ;WITH SettingAgg AS (
                    SELECT MAX(audit_id) AS max_audit_id
                    FROM account_setting_audit
                    WHERE society_id = @society_id
                    GROUP BY acc_set_id, society_id
                )
                SELECT @setting_charges_ids = STRING_AGG(CAST(max_audit_id AS NVARCHAR(10)), ',')
                FROM SettingAgg;

                -- Insert bill
                INSERT INTO maintenance_cal
                (
                    maintenance_id, bill_id, bill_no, gen_date, due_date, 
                    total_amount, due, flat_id, m_date, bill_type, society_id,
                    amt_forward, interest_forward, tax_interest_amt, advance, generate_status, status
                )
                SELECT 
                    ISNULL(MAX(maintenance_id), 0) + 1, @bill_id, 
                    ISNULL(MAX(bill_no), 0) + 1, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE),
                    DATEADD(DAY, @due_days, GETDATE()), 
                    @due, @due, @flat_id, GETDATE(), 1, @society_id,
                    @amt_forward, @interest, @tax_interest, 0, 1,  
                   1-- CASE WHEN @total_amt = @due THEN 1 ELSE 5 END          if advance payment exist then status 5 partially paid 
                FROM maintenance_cal;

                SET @total_billed += @total_amt;

                FETCH NEXT FROM flat_cursor INTO @flat_id, @sqft;
            END;

            CLOSE flat_cursor;
            DEALLOCATE flat_cursor;

            -- Charge heads for THIS society's run, written inside the society
            -- loop. Outside it this ran even when the guard above had skipped
            -- generation, so a second call on the same day appended another
            -- row and the bill printed every charge twice; a third call, three
            -- times. Inside, @society_id and @bill_id both still belong to the
            -- society just billed -- outside, @society_id holds whichever
            -- society the cursor happened to end on and MAX(bill_id) spans the
            -- whole table, so the charges could attach to another society's bill.
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM bill_charges
                           WHERE bill_Id = @bill_id AND Society_Id = @society_id)
                INSERT INTO bill_charges
                       (Bill_Charges_Id, Setting_Charges_Id, Society_Id, Charges_Type, bill_Id)
                VALUES (@bill_charges_ids, @setting_charges_ids, @society_id, @bill_type, @bill_id);

            -- Kept for the notification SELECT after the cursor closes.
            SET @last_bill_id = @bill_id;

            SET @msg = CONCAT('Bill generated for ', @flat_count,
                              ' flats @ rate ', @rate, 
                              ' per sq.ft. Total billed ₹', @total_billed);
print 'Bill  generated Successfully';

           
        END
        ELSE
       
           print 'Bill already exists or generation skipped';
  

        FETCH NEXT FROM society_cursor INTO @society_id, @today, @due_days, @interest;
    END;

    CLOSE society_cursor;
    DEALLOCATE society_cursor;

    -- The bill_charges INSERT that stood here has moved inside the society
    -- loop; MAX(bill_id) over the whole table could not tell which society's
    -- run it belonged to.
    --  SELECT @bill_id = ISNULL(MAX(bill_id), 0)  FROM maintenance_cal;
    --  INSERT INTO bill_charges VALUES (@bill_charges_ids, @setting_charges_ids, @society_id, @bill_type, @bill_id);

    -- Push tokens for the run just created. Nothing was generated when
    -- @last_bill_id is still NULL, so return no rows rather than every owner.
    select bill_id as maintenance_id,token,type,owner_id  from maintenance_cal m inner join userdata o on  m.flat_id=o.flat_id
	where bill_id=@last_bill_id and token is not null and type='owner' and active_status=0 order by o.flat_id ;
END;


 
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[ManageRefreshToken]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:34:57 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ManageRefreshToken]
    @operation       NVARCHAR(20),       -- 'insert', 'get', or 'revoke'
    
    -- Insert fields
    @user_mobile     NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @refresh_token   NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @device_info     NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @ip_address      NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @expires_at      DATETIME2 = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Insert Operation --------------------------------------------
    IF @operation = 'insert'
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO dbo.RefreshTokens (user_mobile, refresh_token, device_info,  expires_at)
        VALUES (@user_mobile, @refresh_token, @device_info, @expires_at);

        SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS id;
        RETURN;
    END


    -- Get Operation ------------------------------------------------
    IF @operation = 'get'
    BEGIN
        SELECT TOP 1 *
        FROM dbo.RefreshTokens
        WHERE refresh_token = @refresh_token
          AND revoked = 0
          AND expires_at >   SYSUTCDATETIME();
        RETURN;
    END


    -- Revoke Operation ---------------------------------------------
    IF @operation = 'revoke'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.RefreshTokens
        SET revoked = 1
        WHERE refresh_token = @refresh_token;

        SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS revoked_count;  -- Optional feedback
        RETURN;
    END
	IF @operation = 'AutoTask'
    BEGIN
	  DELETE FROM RefreshTokens 
      WHERE expires_at < SYSUTCDATETIME();
        DELETE FROM RefreshTokens
      WHERE id IN (
          SELECT id FROM (
              SELECT id,
                     ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
                       PARTITION BY user_mobile ORDER BY created_at DESC
                     ) AS rn
              FROM RefreshTokens
          ) t
          WHERE t.rn > 5
      )
	
    END

    -- Invalid Operation --------------------------------------------
    SELECT 'Invalid operation. Use insert, get, or revoke.' AS error_message;
END

select * from RefreshTokens
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_account_setting]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:35:22 ******/

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_account_setting]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@acc_set_id int =0,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@mem_open_bal int=0,
@mem_charge_btn int=0,
@mem_charge_allocation int=0,
@receipt_btn int=0,
@bill_gen_btn int=0,
@gst_round int=0,
@charge_round int=0,
@payment_voucher int=0,
@debit_note_voucher int=0,
@credit_note_voucher int=0,
@general_voucher int=0,
@receipt_voucher int=0,
@build_wise_payment int=0,
@remainder_email_dues int=0,
@rate_per_sqf decimal = null,
@two_w_rate decimal = null,
@four_w_rate decimal = null,
@auto_bill_generation bit = false,
@bill_gen_date int = 0,
@bill_due_period int  = 0

AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
 	select @acc_set_id=acc_set_id from account_setting where acc_set_id=@acc_set_id
	
	     if @acc_set_id=0 
	       Begin
		   INSERT INTO account_setting(mem_open_bal,mem_charge_btn,mem_charge_allocation,receipt_btn,gst_round,charge_round,payment_voucher,debit_note_voucher,credit_note_voucher,general_voucher,receipt_voucher,build_wise_payment,remainder_email_dues,society_id,rate_per_sqfeet,two_wheeler_rate,four_wheeler_rate)values
		   (@mem_open_bal,@mem_charge_btn,@mem_charge_allocation,@receipt_btn,@gst_round,@charge_round,@payment_voucher,@debit_note_voucher,@credit_note_voucher,@general_voucher,@receipt_voucher,@build_wise_payment,@remainder_email_dues,@society_id,@rate_per_sqf,@two_w_rate,@four_w_rate)
	      End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update account_setting set mem_open_bal=@mem_open_bal,mem_charge_btn=@mem_charge_btn,mem_charge_allocation=@mem_charge_allocation,receipt_btn=@receipt_btn,gst_round=@gst_round,charge_round=@charge_round,payment_voucher=@payment_voucher, rate_per_sqfeet=@rate_per_sqf,two_wheeler_rate=@two_w_rate,four_wheeler_rate=@four_w_rate, debit_note_voucher=@debit_note_voucher,credit_note_voucher=@credit_note_voucher,general_voucher=@general_voucher,receipt_voucher=@receipt_voucher,build_wise_payment=@build_wise_payment,remainder_email_dues=@remainder_email_dues,society_id=@society_id where acc_set_id=@acc_set_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from account_setting where society_id=@society_id
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Delete from account_setting where acc_set_id=@acc_set_id
    	End
	If @operation='getcode'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from account_setting where society_id=@society_id
    	End	


	 IF @operation = 'Insert'
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM account_setting WHERE society_id = @society_id)
    BEGIN
        UPDATE account_setting
        SET 
            rate_per_sqfeet = @rate_per_sqf,
            two_wheeler_rate = @two_w_rate,
            four_wheeler_rate = @four_w_rate,
            auto_bill_generation = @auto_bill_generation,
            bill_gen_date = @bill_gen_date,
            bill_due_period = @bill_due_period
        WHERE society_id = @society_id;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO account_setting
        (
            society_id,
            rate_per_sqfeet,
            two_wheeler_rate,
            four_wheeler_rate,
            auto_bill_generation,
            bill_gen_date,
            bill_due_period,
			interest_rate
        )
        VALUES
        (
            @society_id,
            @rate_per_sqf,
            @two_w_rate,
            @four_w_rate,
            @auto_bill_generation,
            @bill_gen_date,
            @bill_due_period,
			21
        );
    END
END
		
	
END


GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_approvar]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL,
@approver_id int=0,
@user_id int=0,
@bill_id int=0,
@approval_status int= null,
@approval_date Date=null
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @approver_id = 0
                BEGIN
                  INSERT INTO dbo.vendor_bill_approval(approval_id, user_id, bill_id, approval_status)
VALUES (
    (SELECT ISNULL(MAX(approval_id), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.vendor_bill_approval),
    @user_id,
    @bill_id,
    ISNULL(@approval_status, 1)
);                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.vendor_bill_approval
                    SET   user_id=@user_id,
					      bill_id=@bill_id,
						  approval_status=@approval_status,
						  approval_date=@approval_date
                          
                    WHERE  approval_id = @approver_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select' 
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM  vendor_bill_approval
            WHERE approval_id=@approver_id;

			
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
           Update  vendor_bill_approval set approval_status=1
            WHERE  approval_id=@approver_id;
        END
		 --IF @operation = 'update_approval_status'
   --     BEGIN
   --         Update vendor_bill_approval set approval_status=1,approval_date=getdate()
   --         WHERE  bill_id=@bill_id and user_id=@user_id;
   --     END
   -- IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
   --     BEGIN
   --          SELECT approval_id,vendor_bill_approval.user_id,bill_id,vendor_bill_approval.approval_status,approval_date,name,'get' as type
   --         FROM  vendor_bill_approval inner join UserLogin on UserLogin.user_id=vendor_bill_approval.user_id
   --         WHERE bill_id=@bill_id
   --         ORDER BY approval_id DESC;
   --     END
		End
		
	
		
  
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_auditor_question_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:36:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_auditor_question_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL,
@audt_ques_id INT=0,
@question_desc NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL,
@answer_desc NVARCHAR (500)=NULL,
@answer_key INT=0,
@status_id INT=0,
@society_id NVARCHAR(10)=null,
@audt_header_desc NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@audit_header_id INT = 0,
@sequence  INT=0

AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @audt_ques_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = audt_ques_id
                    FROM     dbo.auditor_question_master
                    ORDER BY audt_ques_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.auditor_question_master (audt_ques_id,society_id,question_desc,answer_desc, status_id, audt_header_id)
                    VALUES                      (@new,@society_id,N''+@question_desc,N''+@answer_desc, @status_id, @audit_header_id);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.auditor_question_master
                    SET 
                           society_id    = @society_id,
                           question_desc = @question_desc,
                           answer_desc   = @answer_desc,
						   status_id     = @status_id,
						   audt_header_id= @audit_header_id
                    WHERE  audt_ques_id  = @audt_ques_id;
                END
        END
		
	IF @operation = 'insert_header'
		BEGIN
			IF @audit_header_id = 0
				BEGIN
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = audt_header_id
                    FROM     dbo.audt_header_lkp
                    ORDER BY audt_header_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END

					INSERT INTO audt_header_lkp (audt_header_id, audt_header_desc, status_id,society_id)
					OUTPUT INSERTED.audt_header_id
					Values (@new,@audt_header_desc, @status_id,@society_id);
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
				UPDATE dbo.audt_header_lkp
				SET
					audt_header_desc = @audt_header_desc,
					status_id = @status_id,
					society_id = @society_id where audt_header_id = @audit_header_id;
			END
		END

    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.auditor_question_master
            WHERE  audt_ques_id = @audt_ques_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.auditor_question_master set status_id=1
            WHERE  audt_ques_id = @audt_ques_id;
        END
  	

    IF @operation = 'Grid_Bind_CD'
        BEGIN
		WITH inc AS (
    SELECT 
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY (SELECT NULL)) AS rn,
        prev_year_income,
        description AS income_description,
        current_year_income
    FROM yearwise_income  WHERE  society_id = @society_id
),
exp AS (
    SELECT 
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY (SELECT NULL)) AS rn,
        prev_year_expense,
        description AS expense_description,
        current_year_expense
    FROM yearwise_expense  WHERE  society_id = @society_id
)
SELECT
    COALESCE(inc.prev_year_income, NULL) AS prev_year_income,
    inc.income_description AS INCOME,
    COALESCE(inc.current_year_income, NULL) AS current_year_income,
    COALESCE(exp.prev_year_expense, NULL) AS prev_year_expense,
    exp.expense_description AS EXPENSE,
    COALESCE(exp.current_year_expense, NULL) AS current_year_expense
FROM inc
FULL OUTER JOIN exp
    ON inc.rn = exp.rn
ORDER BY inc.rn;

          
        END



		 IF @operation = 'get_main_points'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.audt_header_lkp where status_id=1 and society_id=@society_id  order by SequenceOrder asc ;
        END
		 IF @operation = 'get_questions'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.auditor_question_master
            WHERE  status_id = 1 and society_id=@society_id order by audt_ques_id asc ;
        END

		IF @operation='Update_Sequence'
		BEGIN
		 
		 UPDATE audt_header_lkp set SequenceOrder=@sequence where audt_header_id=@audit_header_id;
		END

		IF @operation='get_Society_Info' 
		Begin
			select * from society_info where society_id=@society_id
			END

 END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_balancesheet]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:36:28 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_balancesheet]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@bal_sub_id INT=0,
@bal_sub_desc NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@amount decimal(18,2)=0,
@status_id INT=0,
@village_id NVARCHAR(10)=null,
@bal_header_desc NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@bal_head_id INT = 0,
@Seq_order  INT=0,
@comp_id int=0

AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @bal_sub_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = bal_sub_id
                    FROM     dbo.balancesheet_sub_point
                    ORDER BY bal_sub_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.balancesheet_sub_point (bal_sub_id,village_id,bal_sub_desc,amount, status_id, bal_head_id)
                    VALUES                      (@new,@village_id,N''+@bal_sub_desc,@amount, @status_id, @bal_head_id);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.balancesheet_sub_point
                    SET 
                           village_id    = @village_id,
                           bal_sub_desc  = N''+@bal_sub_desc,
                           amount        = @amount,
						   status_id     = @status_id,
						   bal_head_id   = @bal_head_id
                    WHERE  bal_sub_id    = @bal_sub_id;
                END
        END
		
IF @operation = 'insert_header'
BEGIN
    IF @bal_head_id = 0
    BEGIN

        SELECT TOP (1) @new = bal_head_id
        FROM dbo.balancesheet_head_lkp
        ORDER BY bal_head_id DESC;

        SET @new = ISNULL(@new, 0) + 1;

        INSERT INTO balancesheet_head_lkp
        (
            bal_head_id,
            bal_header_desc,
            amount,
            status_id,
            village_id,
            Seq_order,
            comp_id
        )
        OUTPUT INSERTED.bal_head_id
        VALUES
        (
            @new,
            @bal_header_desc,
            @amount,
            @status_id,
            @village_id,
            @Seq_order,
            @comp_id
        );
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.balancesheet_head_lkp
        SET
            bal_header_desc = @bal_header_desc,
            status_id       = @status_id,
            amount          = @amount,
            village_id      = @village_id,
            Seq_order       = @Seq_order,
            comp_id         = @comp_id
        OUTPUT INSERTED.bal_head_id   -- ✅ RETURN ID ON UPDATE
        WHERE bal_head_id = @bal_head_id;
    END
END

    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.balancesheet_sub_point
            WHERE  bal_sub_id = @bal_sub_id;
        END
       IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.balancesheet_sub_point set status_id=1
            WHERE  bal_sub_id = @bal_sub_id;
        END
  	
		 IF @operation = 'get_main_points'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.balancesheet_head_lkp where status_id=1 and village_id=@village_id  order by Seq_order asc ;
        END
		 IF @operation = 'get_sub_point'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.balancesheet_sub_point
            WHERE  status_id = 1 and village_id=@village_id order by bal_sub_id asc ;
        END

		IF @operation='Update_Seq_order'
		BEGIN
		 
		 UPDATE balancesheet_head_lkp set Seq_order=@Seq_order where bal_head_id=@bal_head_id;
		END

 END

GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_building_master]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@build_id int=0,
@name nvarchar(50)=null,
@address1 nvarchar(150)=null,
@address2 nvarchar(150)=null,
@no_of_floore int=0,
@print_name nvarchar(250)=Null,
@registration_no nvarchar(50)=0,
@para nvarchar(100)=Null,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@bank_name nvarchar(50)=null,
@bank_add nvarchar(50)=null,
@branch nvarchar(50)=null,
@ifsc_code nvarchar(50)=null,
@acc_no nvarchar(50)=null, 
@email nvarchar(50)=null
AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @build_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = build_id
                    FROM     dbo.building_master
                    ORDER BY build_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO dbo.building_master(build_id,name,address1,address2,no_of_floore,print_name,registration_no,society_id,bank_name,bank_add,branch,ifsc_code,acc_no,email,active_status)values(@new,dbo.InitCap(@name),dbo.InitCap(@address1),dbo.InitCap(@address2),@no_of_floore,dbo.InitCap(@print_name),@registration_no,@society_id,@bank_name,@bank_add,@branch,@ifsc_code,@acc_no,@email,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.building_master set name=dbo.InitCap(@name),address1=dbo.InitCap(@address1),address2=dbo.InitCap(@address2),no_of_floore=@no_of_floore,print_name=dbo.InitCap(@print_name),registration_no=@registration_no,society_id=@society_id,bank_name=@bank_name,bank_add=@bank_add,branch=@branch,ifsc_code=@ifsc_code,acc_no=@acc_no,email=@email where build_id=@build_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.building_master where build_id=@build_id
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	  Update dbo.building_master set active_status=1 where build_id=@build_id

    	End

	  If @operation='check_no'
	   Begin
	    If @build_id=0
		Select * from dbo.building_master Where registration_no=@registration_no AND active_status=0
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.building_master Where registration_no=@registration_no AND active_status=0 AND build_id=@build_id AND build_id<>@build_id
	   End

	   		  

	  If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	     select * from building_master where society_id= @society_id AND active_status=0 ORDER BY build_id DESC
    	End

		If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	select * from building_master where  active_status=0  And society_id= @society_id and( name like  @search+'%' or print_name like @search+'%' or no_of_floore like try_cast(@search as int) or address1 like @search+'%' or address2 like @search+'%')
		 ORDER BY build_id DESC
    	End

END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_car_polling]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@car_id int=0,
@c_name nvarchar(50)=null,

@vehical_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@seat nvarchar(50)=null,
@time smalldatetime=Null,
@date smalldatetime=Null,
@destination nvarchar(50)=null,
@charges nvarchar(50)=Null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@search nvarchar(200)=null



AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @car_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = car_id
                    FROM     dbo.car_polling
                    ORDER BY car_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO dbo.car_polling(car_id,c_name,vehical_no,seat,time,date,destination,charges,society_id,active_status)values(@new,dbo.InitCap(@c_name),@vehical_no,@seat,@time,@date,dbo.InitCap(@destination),@charges,@society_id,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.car_polling set c_name=dbo.InitCap(@c_name),vehical_no=@vehical_no,seat=@seat,time=@time,date=@date,destination=dbo.InitCap(@destination),charges=@charges,society_id=@society_id where car_id=@car_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.car_polling where car_id=@car_id
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update dbo.car_polling set active_status=1 where car_id=@car_id
    	End
     
	If @operation='check_name'
	   Begin
	    If @car_id=0
		Select * from dbo.car_polling Where lower(trim(c_name))=lower(trim(@c_name))
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.car_polling Where c_name=@c_name AND car_id=@car_id AND car_id<>@car_id
	   End

	   If @operation='check_no'
	   Begin
	    If @car_id=0
		Select * from  dbo.car_polling Where lower(trim(vehical_no))=lower(trim(@vehical_no))
		ElSE
		Select * from  dbo.car_polling Where vehical_no=@vehical_no AND car_id=@car_id AND car_id<>@car_id
	   End

    If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	     select * from car_polling where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and( c_name like @search+'%' or vehical_no like @search+'%'or destination like @search+'%'or cast(date as varchar) like @search+'%')  ORDER BY car_id DESC
    	End
   If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	     select * from car_polling where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id   ORDER BY car_id DESC
    	End

END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_caretaker_master]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@caretaker_id int=0,
@c_name nvarchar(50)=null,
@doc_id int=0,
@c_address nvarchar(50)=null,
@area nvarchar(50)=null,
@city nvarchar(50)=null,
@pincode nvarchar(50)=null,
@state_id int=0,
@mobile_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@email nvarchar(50)=null,
@doc_executed nvarchar(50)=null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@wing_id int=0,
@flat_no nvarchar(50)=Null,
@search nvarchar(200)=null


AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @caretaker_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = caretaker_id
                    FROM     dbo.caretaker_master
                    ORDER BY caretaker_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO dbo.caretaker_master(caretaker_id, c_name,doc_id,c_address,area,city,pincode,state_id,mobile_no,email,doc_executed,society_id,wing_id,flat_no,active_status)values(@new,dbo.InitCap(@c_name),@doc_id ,dbo.InitCap(@c_address),@area,dbo.InitCap(@city),@pincode,@state_id,@mobile_no,@email,@doc_executed,@society_id,@wing_id,@flat_no,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.caretaker_master set c_name=dbo.InitCap(@c_name),doc_id=@doc_id,c_address=dbo.InitCap(@c_address),area=@area,city=dbo.InitCap(@city),pincode=@pincode,state_id=@state_id,mobile_no=@mobile_no,email=@email,doc_executed=@doc_executed,society_id=@society_id,wing_id=@wing_id,flat_no=@flat_no where caretaker_id=@caretaker_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.caretaker_master where caretaker_id=@caretaker_id
		   
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update dbo.caretaker_master set active_status=1 where caretaker_id=@caretaker_id
		   
    	End
     
	If @operation='check_name'
	   Begin
	    If @caretaker_id=0
		Select * from dbo.caretaker_master Where lower(trim(c_name))=lower(trim(@c_name))
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.caretaker_master Where c_name=@c_name AND caretaker_id=@caretaker_id AND caretaker_id<>@caretaker_id
	   End

	   If @operation='check_no'
	   Begin
	    If @caretaker_id=0
		Select * from dbo.caretaker_master Where flat_no=@flat_no and wing_id=@wing_id
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.caretaker_master Where flat_no=@flat_no AND  wing_id=@wing_id AND wing_id<>@wing_id
	   End

	     If @operation='Grid_Show'
	  Begin
	   Select * from caretaker_master where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id ORDER BY caretaker_id DESC
      END
	     If @operation='Search'
	  Begin
	   Select * from caretaker_master where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and( c_name like @search+'%' or c_address like @search+'%'or mobile_no like @search+'%' or cast (flat_no as varchar)  like @search+'%')  ORDER BY caretaker_id DESC
      END


	    If @operation='fill_wing'
	  Begin
	   Select wing_id,(name + w_name) as name from global_society_view where society_id =@society_id
      END

	    If @operation='fill_doc'
	  Begin
	  Select * from doc_master where society_id =@society_id
      END

	  If @operation='fill_flat'
	  Begin
	  Select * from flat_master where wing_id = @society_id
      END
  
	    If @operation='fill_state'
	  Begin
	 Select *  from state
	 end

END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_cashbook]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:38:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_cashbook]
@operation nvarchar(50)=Null,
@date1 smalldatetime=null,
@date2 smalldatetime=null,
@build_id int = 0,
@type nvarchar(50) =null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null
AS
BEGIN
 If @operation='cashbook'
   Begin
DECLARE @openingBalance DECIMAL(18,2);

-- Calculate Opening Balance
SELECT @openingBalance = ISNULL(SUM(Credit), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(Debit), 0)
FROM society_cashbook
-- Everything before the report window, with no year boundary: the opening
-- balance carries forward across years. The earlier form started from
-- 1 January of @date1's year, so a February report opened at -2,982 when the
-- society actually held 1,06,313.
WHERE [date] < @date1 and society_id=@society_id


-- Main report
SELECT *
FROM (
    -- Opening Balance
    SELECT 
        1 AS seq, 
        NULL AS [Date], 
        'Opening Balance' AS Particular,
        CASE WHEN @openingBalance < 0 THEN ABS(@openingBalance) ELSE 0 END AS Debit,
        CASE WHEN @openingBalance > 0 THEN ABS(@openingBalance) ELSE 0 END AS Credit
    UNION ALL
    -- Transactions
    SELECT 
        2 AS seq, 
        [date], 
        Particular, 
        Debit, 
        Credit
    FROM society_cashbook
    -- Half-open on the upper end so @date2 itself is included, times and all.
    -- BETWEEN @date1 AND DATEADD(DD,-1,@date2) dropped the last day entirely,
    -- and entries are stamped with a time — 28-11 18:06 fell outside a bound
    -- of 28-11 00:00, so that day's entries vanished from its own report.
    WHERE [date] >= @date1 AND [date] < DATEADD(DD, 1, @date2) and society_id=@society_id

    UNION ALL
    -- Closing Balance
    SELECT 
        3 AS seq, 
        NULL AS [Date], 
        'Closing Balance' AS Particular,
        CASE 
            WHEN (@openingBalance + (ISNULL(SUM(Credit), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(Debit), 0))) < 0 
                THEN ABS(@openingBalance + ISNULL(SUM(Credit), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(Debit), 0)) 
            ELSE null
        END AS Debit,
        CASE 
            WHEN (@openingBalance + (ISNULL(SUM(Credit), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(Debit), 0))) > 0 
                THEN (@openingBalance + ISNULL(SUM(Credit), 0) - ISNULL(SUM(Debit), 0)) 
            ELSE null 
        END AS Credit
    FROM society_cashbook
    -- Same window as the transactions above, or the closing balance would be
    -- struck over a different set of rows than the ones listed.
    WHERE [date] >= @date1 AND [date] < DATEADD(DD, 1, @date2) and society_id=@society_id

) AS report

  
   End

END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_dashboard]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:38:26 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_dashboard]
@operation nvarchar(50)=Null,
@date smalldatetime=Null,
@date1 smalldatetime=null,
@date2 smalldatetime=null,
@build_id int =0,
@dateFrom nvarchar(50)=null,
@dateTo nvarchar(50)=null,
@type nvarchar(50)=null,
@minPrice int=0,
@maxPrice int=0,
@society_id nvarchar(50)=Null,
@owner_id int=0,@user_id int=0,
@expense int=0,
@search nvarchar(50)=null,
@query nvarchar(MAX)=null
AS
BEGIN

if @operation ='RecentActivity'
BEgin

select * from recent_activity_vw where society_id=@society_id and date between DATEADD(day,-15,getdate()) and getdate() order by date desc

ENd
if @operation ='RecentActivityAll'
BEgin
select * from recent_activity_vw where society_id=@society_id  order by date desc

ENd
If @operation='notic_show'
   Begin
      SELECT   description  FROM  dbo.notice_master where @date>=date AND @date<=valid_to and active_status=0
   End
  
If @operation='get_meeting'
	Begin
	   SELECT   CASE WHEN subject IS NOT NULL THEN 'M' else '' End  as 'M' ,'' As 'E' , CASE WHEN dbo.meeting_master.subject IS NOT NULL THEN ' The Meeting is On  ' + CONVERT(nvarchar, dbo.meeting_master.meeting_date)+ ' At  ' + CONVERT(nvarchar, dbo.meeting_master.meeting_time) + ' Regarding  ' + dbo.meeting_master.subject ELSE '' END AS meet_sub,''as Event
       FROM  dbo.meeting_master  WHERE (meeting_date = @date) and active_status=0
	   UNION
	   SELECT  '' as 'M' , CASE WHEN event_name IS NOT NULL THEN 'E' ELSE '' END As 'E','' AS meet_sub ,CASE WHEN dbo.event_master.event_name IS NOT NULL THEN dbo.event_master.event_name ELSE '' END AS Event
       FROM dbo.event_master  Where  @date>=from_date And @date<=to_date and active_status=0
    End

If @operation='pdc_reminder'
   Begin
   SELECT   chqno, che_amount, che_date FROM dbo.pdc_reminder Where datepart(mm,che_date)=datepart(mm,@date) AND che_dep=0 and active_status=0
   End

  -- IF @operation='due_person'
  -- Begin
  -- SELECT        dbo.maintenance_cal.bill_no, dbo.building_master.name AS build_name, dbo.owner_master.name AS Owner_name, ISNULL(dbo.maintenance_cal.total_amount, 0) 
  --                       - ISNULL(derivedtbl_1.total_amt_receive, 0) AS balance
  -- FROM            dbo.maintenance_cal INNER JOIN
  --                       dbo.building_master ON dbo.maintenance_cal.build_id= dbo.building_master.build_id INNER JOIN
  --                       dbo.owner_master ON dbo.maintenance_cal.owner_id = dbo.owner_master.owner_id LEFT OUTER JOIN
  --                           (SELECT        SUM(regular_amt) AS total_amt_receive, owner_id
  --                             FROM            dbo.receipt
  --                             GROUP BY owner_id) AS derivedtbl_1 ON dbo.maintenance_cal.maint_cal_id = derivedtbl_1.owner_id
  --WHERE        (ISNULL(dbo.maintenance_cal.total_amount, 0) - ISNULL(derivedtbl_1.total_amt_receive, 0) <> 0)

  -- End


    If @operation='ownerwise_maintenance'
   Begin
	WITH Opening AS (
    SELECT
        ISNULL(SUM(Maintenance), 0) AS OpeningMaintenance,
        ISNULL(SUM(Payment), 0) AS OpeningPayment,
        MAX(gen_date) AS gen_date
    FROM unitwise_main
    WHERE m_date BETWEEN DATEADD(yy, DATEDIFF(yy, 0, @date1), 0) 
                    AND DATEADD(dd, -1, @date1)
      AND owner_id = @owner_id
),
Period AS (
    SELECT
        ISNULL(SUM(Maintenance), 0) AS PeriodMaintenance,
        ISNULL(SUM(Payment), 0) AS PeriodPayment,
        MAX(gen_date) AS gen_date
    FROM unitwise_main
    WHERE m_date BETWEEN @date1 AND DATEADD(dd, -1, @date2)
      AND build_id = @build_id
      AND owner_id = @owner_id
),
Tx AS (
    SELECT 
        m_date,
        gen_date,
        Particular,
        Payment,
        Maintenance,
        ref        -- ← ref added for transactions (replace if you have actual column)
    FROM unitwise_main
    WHERE m_date BETWEEN @date1 AND DATEADD(dd, -1, @date2)
      AND build_id = @build_id
      AND owner_id = @owner_id
)

-- 1: Opening Balance
SELECT
    1 AS seq,
    CONVERT(varchar, @date1, 106) AS date,
    gen_date,
    'Opening Balance' AS Particular,
    OpeningPayment AS Payment,
    OpeningMaintenance AS Maintenance,
    NULL AS ref     -- ← added
FROM Opening

UNION ALL

-- 2: All transactions
SELECT
    2 AS seq,
    m_date AS date,
    gen_date,
    Particular,
    Payment,
    Maintenance,
    ref            -- ← already added in Tx CTE
FROM Tx

UNION ALL

-- 3: Total Balance
SELECT
    3 AS seq,
    NULL AS date,
    Opening.gen_date,
    'Total Balance' AS Particular,
    (OpeningPayment + PeriodPayment) AS Payment,
    (OpeningMaintenance + PeriodMaintenance) AS Maintenance,
    NULL AS ref    -- ← added
FROM Opening 
CROSS JOIN Period

UNION ALL

-- 4: Closing Balance
SELECT
    4 AS seq,
    NULL AS date,
    b.gen_date,
    'Closing Balance' AS Particular,
    CASE WHEN Balance < 0 THEN ABS(Balance) ELSE 0 END AS Payment,
    CASE WHEN Balance > 0 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END AS Maintenance,
    NULL AS ref     -- ← added
FROM (
    SELECT
        (OpeningMaintenance + PeriodMaintenance)
      - (OpeningPayment + PeriodPayment) AS Balance,
        Opening.gen_date
    FROM Opening 
    CROSS JOIN Period
) AS b

ORDER BY seq, gen_date;


   End

   If @operation='ExpenseChart'
   Begin
   if @expense =2 
   begin
	 WITH R(N) AS
	(
		SELECT 0
		UNION ALL
		SELECT N+1 
		FROM R
		WHERE N < 11
	)
	SELECT Left(DATENAME(MONTH,DATEADD(MONTH,+N,DATEADD(yy, DATEDIFF(yy,0 , GETDATE()), 0))),3) AS expense_month,
		   COALESCE(s.expense, 0) as expense,
		   COALESCE(s.Due, 0) as Due,
		   COALESCE(s.Collection, 0) as Collection
	FROM R
	LEFT JOIN 
	(
		SELECT sum(total_amount) as expense, 
			   sum(due) as Due,
			   sum(collection) as Collection,
			   bill_month, 
			   monthno
		FROM expense_tracker  where  society_id=@society_id and date>=  DATEADD(yy, DATEDIFF(yy, 0, GETDATE()), 0)
		GROUP BY bill_month, monthno 
	) s 
	ON DATENAME(MONTH,DATEADD(MONTH,+R.N,DATEADD(yy, DATEDIFF(yy,0 , GETDATE()), 0))) = s.bill_month
	ORDER BY R.N;
	end
	else
	begin
		 WITH R(N) AS
	(
		SELECT 0
		UNION ALL
		SELECT N+1 
		FROM R
		WHERE N < 11
	)
	SELECT left( DATENAME(MONTH,DATEADD(MONTH,+N,DATEADD(yy, DATEDIFF(yy,0 , GETDATE()), 0))),3) AS expense_month,
		   COALESCE(s.expense, 0) as expense,
		   COALESCE(s.Due, 0) as Due,
		   COALESCE(s.Collection, 0) as Collection
	FROM R
	LEFT JOIN 
	(
		SELECT sum(total_amount) as expense, 
			   sum(due) as Due,
			   sum(collection) as Collection,
 bill_month,
			   monthno
		FROM expense_tracker  where society_id=@society_id  and bill_type=@expense and date>=  DATEADD(yy, DATEDIFF(yy, 0, GETDATE()), 0)
		GROUP BY bill_month, monthno 
	) s 
	ON DATENAME(MONTH,DATEADD(MONTH,+R.N,DATEADD(yy, DATEDIFF(yy,0 , GETDATE()), 0))) = s.bill_month
	ORDER BY R.N;
	end

   End
    if @operation= 'Updates'
	 Begin
	 select * from dashUpdates where society_id=@society_id order by date desc 
	 End
    if @operation= 'AdminSearch'
	 Begin
	exec(@query)

	 End

	 if @operation= 'society_receipt'
	 Select *  from admin_vw where society_id = @society_id 


 		  If @operation='IncomeChart'
   Begin
      -- select category,amount from (select sum(due)+sum(tax_interest_amt)as Due,(sum(total_amount)+sum(tax_interest_amt)- (sum(due)+sum(tax_interest_amt))) as Collection
	 --  from maintenance_cal where society_id=@society_id and maintenance_cal.due_date< getdate() and  maintenance_cal.gen_date between @date1 and @date2  ) as p unpivot
	-- (amount for category in (due,collection)) as mkjk
	DECLARE @minDate smalldatetime;

SELECT @minDate = MIN(gen_date)
FROM maintenance_cal
WHERE society_id = @society_id;

SELECT category, amount
FROM
(
    SELECT
        SUM(due) + SUM(tax_interest_amt) AS Due,
        (
            SUM(total_amount) + SUM(tax_interest_amt)
            - (SUM(due) + SUM(tax_interest_amt))
        ) AS Collection
    FROM maintenance_cal
    WHERE society_id = @society_id
      AND maintenance_cal.due_date < GETDATE()
      AND maintenance_cal.gen_date >= @minDate   -- 🔥 auto MIN date
      AND maintenance_cal.gen_date < DATEADD(DAY, 1, @date2)
) AS p
UNPIVOT
(
    amount FOR category IN (Due, Collection)
) AS mkjk;

	 End
	
	 If @operation= 'Get_Ticket'
	 Begin
	 select count(status) as opened,(select count(status) as resolved from Helpdesk_vw where society_id=@society_id and status=4 )as resolved
     from Helpdesk_vw where society_id=@society_id and status=1
	 End

	 if @operation= 'Get_Month'
	 Begin
	 select gen_date,   {fn MONTHNAME(gen_date)}  AS month_name,sum(due) as amount from maintenance_cal where society_id=@society_id group by  gen_date  order by  gen_date 
	 End

	 	 if @operation= 'Notification'
	 Begin
	SELECT notify_status_id,notification_id,notification_type,user_id,user_type,seen_status,society_id,title,body
,dbo.GetRelativeTime(timestamp) as timestamp from notify_status where seen_status=0 and society_id=@society_id
and user_id=@user_id order by notify_status_id desc
	 End
	
	  if @operation= 'defaulter_show'
	 Begin
	 
	SELECT        dbo.owner_search_vw.name as owner_name, dbo.owner_search_vw.bed, dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit, dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_no,dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.email, dbo.owner_search_vw.pre_mob, ISNULL(SUM(m.due)+sum(tax_interest_amt), 0) AS due
FROM            dbo.maintenance_cal m INNER JOIN
                         dbo.owner_search_vw ON m.flat_id = dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id where m.due_date< getdate() and dbo.owner_search_vw.type='Owner'
  AND m.society_id = @society_id
GROUP BY dbo.owner_search_vw.name, dbo.owner_search_vw.bed, dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit, dbo.owner_search_vw.email, dbo.owner_search_vw.pre_mob , dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_no,dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id
	 End
	if @operation= 'getResidents'
	 Begin
	 select count(*) as residents from owner_master  where active_status=0 and type='Owner'  and society_id=@society_id
	 End
	 if @operation= 'UpdateStatus'
	 Begin
	 update notify_Status set seen_status=1 where notify_status_id= @user_id and  society_id=@society_id
	 End
 END



	
   
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_doc_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:38:46 ******/

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_doc_master]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@doc_id int=0,
@doc_name nvarchar(50)=null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@build_id int = 0,
@flat_id int=0,
@upload_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL ,
@document_id int=0

AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @doc_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = doc_id
                    FROM     dbo.doc_master
                    ORDER BY doc_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO doc_master(doc_id,doc_name,society_id,active_status)values(@new,dbo.InitCap(@doc_name),@society_id,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update doc_master set doc_name=dbo.InitCap(@doc_name),society_id=@society_id where doc_id=@doc_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from doc_master where doc_id=@doc_id
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update doc_master set active_status=1 where doc_id=@doc_id
    	End

		
	If @operation='check_name'
	   Begin
	    If @doc_id=0
		Select * from dbo.doc_master Where lower(trim(doc_name))=lower(trim(@doc_name)) and society_id=@society_id
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.doc_master Where doc_name=@doc_name AND doc_id=@doc_id AND doc_id<>@doc_id
	   End

	    If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	     select * from doc_master  where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id   ORDER BY doc_id DESC
	   End
	       If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	     select * from doc_master  where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id and( doc_name like  @search+'%')  ORDER BY doc_id DESC
	   End

	  if @operation = 'doc_master'
		begin
		Select *  from doc_master where society_id=@society_id
		end

		if @operation = 'build_master'
		begin
		Select *  from building_master where society_id=@society_id
		end

		if @operation = 'wing_name'
		begin
		Select * from doc_master where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id
		end

		if @operation = 'flat_no'
		begin
		Select distinct w_name,wing_id from dbo.flat_types where  society_id=@society_id and  build_id=@build_id
		end
--			if @operation = 'GetOwnerDocs'
--		begin
--SELECT DISTINCT o.flat_id, v.doc_name, v.doc_path
--FROM owner_master o
--OUTER APPLY (
--    SELECT d.doc_name, d.doc_path
--    FROM owner_documents d
--    WHERE d.flat_id = o.flat_id

--    UNION ALL
--    SELECT x.doc_name, x.doc_path
--    FROM (
--        VALUES
--            ('Agreement Letter', o.agreement_path),
--            ('ID Proof', o.id_proof)
--    ) x(doc_name, doc_path)
--    WHERE x.doc_path IS NOT NULL AND x.doc_path <> ''
--) v
--WHERE o.flat_id = @flat_id;
--		end
--IF @operation = 'GetOwnerDocs'
--BEGIN
--    SELECT DISTINCT 
--        o.flat_id,
--        v.doc_name,
--        v.doc_path,
--        -- Extract file type from path (e.g., pdf, jpg)
--        RIGHT(v.doc_path, CHARINDEX('.', REVERSE(v.doc_path)) - 1) AS doc_type,
--        -- Upload date for each document
--        v.upload_date
--    FROM owner_master o
--    OUTER APPLY (
--        SELECT 
--            d.doc_name, 
--            d.doc_path,
--            d.upload_date
--        FROM owner_documents d
--        WHERE d.flat_id = o.flat_id

--        UNION ALL

--        -- Static documents from owner_master
--        SELECT 
--            'Agreement Letter' AS doc_name,
--            o.agreement_path AS doc_path,
--            o.aggrement_date AS upload_date
--        WHERE o.agreement_path IS NOT NULL AND o.agreement_path <> ''

--        UNION ALL

--        SELECT 
--            'ID Proof' AS doc_name,
--            o.id_proof AS doc_path,
--            GETDATE() AS upload_date
--        WHERE o.id_proof IS NOT NULL AND o.id_proof <> ''
--    ) v
--    WHERE o.flat_id = @flat_id;
--END


IF @operation = 'GetOwnerDocs'
BEGIN
    SELECT DISTINCT 
        o.flat_id,

        -- ✅ Include document_id (for uploaded docs)
        v.document_id,

        v.doc_name,
        v.doc_path,

        -- Extract file type (e.g., pdf, jpg)
        RIGHT(v.doc_path, CHARINDEX('.', REVERSE(v.doc_path)) - 1) AS doc_type,

        v.upload_date
    FROM owner_master o
    OUTER APPLY (
        SELECT 
            d.document_id,     -- ✅ Include actual DB ID
            d.doc_name, 
            d.doc_path,
            d.upload_date
        FROM owner_documents d
        WHERE d.flat_id = @flat_id

        UNION ALL

        -- Static Agreement Letter (no document_id in DB)
        SELECT 
            0 AS document_id,   -- ✅ 0 for static docs
            'Agreement Letter' AS doc_name,
            o.agreement_path AS doc_path,
            o.aggrement_date AS upload_date
        WHERE o.agreement_path IS NOT NULL AND o.agreement_path <> ''

        UNION ALL

        -- Static ID Proof
        SELECT 
            0 AS document_id,   -- ✅ 0 for static docs
            'ID Proof' AS doc_name,
            o.id_proof AS doc_path,
            GETDATE() AS upload_date
        WHERE o.id_proof IS NOT NULL AND o.id_proof <> ''
    ) v
    WHERE o.flat_id = @flat_id;
END

if @operation = 'deleteDocuments'
		Begin
			delete from owner_documents where document_id = @document_id
		end
		
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_emergency_alert_preferences]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:39:12 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_emergency_alert_preferences]
    @operation NVARCHAR(50),
    @id INT = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @alert_type NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @alert_scope NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @selected_buildings NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @selected_flats NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @start_time TIME = NULL,
    @end_time TIME = NULL,
    @morning_start_time TIME = NULL,
    @morning_end_time TIME = NULL,
    @evening_start_time TIME = NULL,
    @evening_end_time TIME = NULL,
    @has_evening BIT = 0,
    @created_by INT = NULL,
    @alert_title NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
    @message NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@buildings NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@notification_type NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@user_ids NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@title NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@body NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@notification_id int=null
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -----------------------------
    -- UPSERT Regular Alerts
    -----------------------------
    IF @operation = 'Upsert'
    BEGIN
        IF EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM emergency_alert_preferences
            WHERE society_id = @society_id
              AND alert_type = @alert_type
              AND is_active = 1
        )
        BEGIN
            UPDATE emergency_alert_preferences
            SET alert_scope = @alert_scope,
                selected_buildings = @selected_buildings,
                start_time = @start_time,
                end_time = @end_time,
                morning_start_time = @morning_start_time,
                morning_end_time = @morning_end_time,
                evening_start_time = @evening_start_time,
                evening_end_time = @evening_end_time,
                has_evening = @has_evening,
                updated_at = GETDATE()
            WHERE society_id = @society_id
              AND alert_type = @alert_type
              AND is_active = 1;

            SELECT 1 AS success, 'Alert preference updated successfully' AS message;
        END
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            INSERT INTO emergency_alert_preferences (
                society_id, alert_type, alert_scope,
                selected_buildings, start_time, end_time,
                morning_start_time, morning_end_time,
                evening_start_time, evening_end_time,
                has_evening, created_by, is_active
            )
            VALUES (
                @society_id, @alert_type, @alert_scope,
                @selected_buildings, @start_time, @end_time,
                @morning_start_time, @morning_end_time,
                @evening_start_time, @evening_end_time,
                @has_evening, @created_by, 1
            );

            SELECT 1 AS success, 'Alert preference created successfully' AS message;
        END
    END

    -----------------------------
    -- Get Alerts by Society and Type
    -----------------------------
    ELSE IF @operation = 'GetBySocietyAndType'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            id,
            society_id,
            alert_type,
            alert_scope,
            selected_buildings,
            LEFT(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), start_time, 108),5) AS start_time,
            LEFT(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), end_time, 108),5) AS end_time,
            LEFT(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), morning_start_time, 108),5) AS morning_start_time,
            LEFT(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), morning_end_time, 108),5) AS morning_end_time,
            LEFT(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), evening_start_time, 108),5) AS evening_start_time,
            LEFT(CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), evening_end_time, 108),5) AS evening_end_time,
            has_evening,
            alert_title,
            message,
            created_by,
            created_at,
            updated_at
        FROM emergency_alert_preferences
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
          AND alert_type = @alert_type
          AND is_active = 1;
    END

    -----------------------------
    -- Get Active Alerts
    -----------------------------
    ELSE IF @operation = 'GetActive'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM emergency_alert_preferences
        WHERE is_active = 1
          AND (
                (start_time IS NULL AND end_time IS NULL)
                OR (CAST(GETDATE() AS TIME) BETWEEN start_time AND end_time)
              );
    END

    -----------------------------
    -- Society-wise Building List
    -----------------------------
    ELSE IF @operation = 'SocietywiseBuilding'
    BEGIN
        SELECT build_id, name AS build_name
        FROM building_master
        WHERE society_id = @society_id;
    END

    -----------------------------
    -- Insert Custom Message (always new row)
    -----------------------------
    ELSE IF @operation = 'InsertCustomMessage'
    BEGIN
        BEGIN TRY
            INSERT INTO emergency_alert_preferences (
                society_id,
                alert_type,
                alert_scope,
                selected_buildings,
                alert_title,
                message,
                created_by,
                created_at,
                is_active
            )
            VALUES (
                @society_id,
                @alert_type,
                @alert_scope,
                @selected_buildings,
                @alert_title,
                @message,
                @created_by,
                GETDATE(),
                1
            );

            SELECT 1 AS success, 'Custom message inserted successfully' AS message;
        END TRY
        BEGIN CATCH
            SELECT 0 AS success, ERROR_MESSAGE() AS message;
        END CATCH
    END

	IF @operation = 'getTokensUserId'
BEGIN
	
	-- If buildings is NULL or empty → use society filter
	IF (ISNULL(@buildings, '') = '')
	BEGIN
		SELECT 
			owner_id,
			token
		FROM owner_master
		WHERE society_id = @society_id
		  AND ISNULL(token, '') <> '';
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		-- Buildings present → select ONLY by building list
		SELECT 
			om.owner_id,
			om.token
		FROM owner_details_vw om
		INNER JOIN STRING_SPLIT(@buildings, ',') b
			ON om.build_id = TRY_CAST(b.value AS INT)
		WHERE ISNULL(om.token, '') <> '';
	END

END

IF @operation = 'InsertNotify'
BEGIN
    DECLARE @base INT;

    SELECT @base = ISNULL(MAX(notify_status_id), 0)
    FROM notify_status;

    ;WITH UserList AS
    (
        SELECT TRY_CAST(value AS INT) AS user_id
        FROM STRING_SPLIT(@user_ids, ',')
        WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM(value)) <> ''
    )
    INSERT INTO notify_status
    (
        notify_status_id,
        notification_type,
		notification_id,
        user_id,
        user_type,
        seen_status,
        society_id,
        title,
        body,
        [timestamp]
    )
    SELECT 
        @base + ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY (SELECT NULL)) AS notify_status_id,
        @notification_type,
		0,
        u.user_id,
        'Owner',
        0,
        @society_id,
        @title,
        @body,
        GETDATE()
    FROM UserList u;
END


END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_event_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:39:34 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_event_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@event_id INT=0,
@from_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@to_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@event_name NVARCHAR (150)=NULL,
@description NVARCHAR (500)=NULL,
@para NVARCHAR (100)=NULL,
@society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@f_date NVARCHAR (150)=NULL,
@t_date NVARCHAR (150)=NULL,
@is_seen INT=0,
@search varchar(200)=null
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @event_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = event_id
                    FROM     dbo.event_master
                    ORDER BY event_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.event_master (event_id,event_name, description, from_date, to_date, society_id, active_status)
                    VALUES                       (@new,dbo.InitCap(@event_name), dbo.InitCap(@description), @from_date, @to_date, @society_id, 0);
                    select @new as event_id
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.event_master
                    SET    event_name  = dbo.InitCap(@event_name),
                           description = dbo.InitCap(@description),
                           from_date   = @from_date,
                           to_date     = @to_date,
                           society_id  = @society_id
                    WHERE  event_id = @event_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *FROM   event_master
            WHERE  event_id = @event_id;
            
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select_All'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   event_master where active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.event_master set active_status=1
            WHERE  event_id = @event_id;
           
        END
    IF @operation = 'event_name'
        BEGIN
            IF @event_id <> 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.event_master
                WHERE  event_name = @event_name and active_status=0;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.event_master
                WHERE  event_name = @event_name and active_status=0
                       AND event_id <> @event_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'GV_SELECT'
        BEGIN
            IF @para IS NULL
                BEGIN
                    SELECT *
                    FROM   dbo.event_master where active_status=0;
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    SELECT CONCAT(' * from dbo.event_master', @para);
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_f_date'
        BEGIN
            IF @event_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.event_master
                WHERE  from_date = CONVERT (SMALLDATETIME, @f_date, 103);
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.event_master
                WHERE  from_date = @from_date
                       AND event_id = @event_id and active_status=0
                       AND event_id <> @event_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_t_date'
        BEGIN
            IF @event_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.event_master
                WHERE  to_date = CONVERT (SMALLDATETIME, @t_date, 103);
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.event_master                  
                WHERE  to_date = @to_date
                       AND event_id = @event_id
                       AND event_id = @event_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     event_master
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY event_id DESC;
        END
		

END

GO


15)USE [society]
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_facility]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:39:56 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_facility]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL,
@facility_id INT=0, 
@name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@cost DECIMAL=0, 

@description NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@slot_id Int=0,
@slot Int=0,
@start_time Smalldatetime=Null,
@end_time Smalldatetime=Null,
@society_id NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@status Int=0,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@from_date smalldatetime=null,
@capacity int=0,
@isActive bit = null
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @facility_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = facility_id
                    FROM     dbo.facilities
                    ORDER BY facility_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.facilities(facility_id, name, cost,  description, capacity,society_id,slot,status,active_status, isActive)
                    VALUES                       (@new,@name, @cost, N''+@description,@capacity, @society_id,@slot,1,0, @isActive);
					
					select @new as facility_id
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.facilities
                    SET    name = @name,
                           cost = @cost,
                          capacity = @capacity,
                           description = @description,
						   status = @status,
                           society_id   = @society_id
                           where  facility_id=@facility_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.facilities
            WHERE  facility_id=@facility_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update  facilities set active_status=1
            WHERE  facility_id=@facility_id;
			 Update slot_master set active_status=1 WHERE facility_id=@facility_id;           
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT  *
            FROM     facilities
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY facility_id DESC;
        END
		IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT  *
            FROM     facilities
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and( name like @search+'%' or cost like @search+'%')
            ORDER BY facility_id DESC;
        END

		If @operation = 'Delete_Slot'
		   BEGIN
		   Update slot_master set active_status=1 WHERE slot_id = @slot_id
		   END

		 IF @operation = 'S_Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @slot_id = 0
                BEGIN
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.slot_master(facility_id,start_time,end_time,society_id,active_status)
                    VALUES                       (@facility_id,@start_time,@end_time,@society_id,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.slot_master
                    SET    facility_id=@facility_id,
							
						   start_time = @start_time,
						   end_time = @end_time,
                           society_id   = @society_id
                           where  slot_id=@slot_id;
                END
        END

		IF @operation = 'Grid_Show_Slot'
        BEGIN
            SELECT facility_id,slot_id,CONVERT(varchar(15), CAST(start_time AS TIME), 100) AS  start_time,CONVERT(varchar(15), CAST(end_time AS TIME), 100) AS  end_time,society_id
            FROM   dbo.slot_master
            WHERE active_status=0 and facility_id=@facility_id and society_id = @society_id
        END

		IF @operation = 'Get_Slot'
        BEGIN
            SELECT  facility_id,slot_id,CONVERT(varchar(15), CAST(start_time AS TIME), 100) AS  start_time,CONVERT(varchar(15), CAST(end_time AS TIME), 100) AS  end_time,society_id,status from facilitySlotBooking where from_date=@from_date and facility_id=@facility_id and society_id = @society_id
 union select facility_id,slot_id,CONVERT(varchar(15), CAST(start_time AS TIME), 100) AS  start_time,CONVERT(varchar(15), CAST(end_time AS TIME), 100) AS  end_time,society_id,0 as status from slot_master
 where active_status=0 and slot_id not in (select slot_id from facilitySlotBooking where from_date=@from_date  and society_id = @society_id ) and facility_id=@facility_id and society_id = @society_id
           
        END
  
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_facility_booking]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:40:36 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_facility_booking]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@facility_book_id int=0,
@facility_id int=0,
@book_date date=null,
@from_date date=null,
@to_date date=null,
@from_time smalldatetime=null,
@to_time smalldatetime=null,
@name nvarchar(50)=null,
@flat_no int=0,
@address nvarchar(100)=Null,
@contact nvarchar(50)=null,
@society_in int=0,
@owner_id int=0,
@flat_id int=0,
@amount decimal=null,
@note nvarchar(500)=null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@date smalldatetime=null,
@capacity int=0,
 @fromDate DATE = null,
@toDate DATE = null,
 @fromTime TIME = null,
 @toTime TIME = null 
AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @facility_book_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = facility_book_id
                    FROM     dbo.facility_booking
                    ORDER BY facility_book_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO 
			dbo.facility_booking(facility_book_id,society_id,book_date,from_date,to_date,name,address,flat_id,contact,from_time,to_time,society_in,amount,note,facility_id,active_status, capacity)
			values(@new,@society_id,getdate(), @from_date,@to_date,dbo.InitCap(@name),dbo.InitCap(@address),@flat_no,@contact,case when @from_time=null then NULL else @from_time end ,case when @to_time=null then NULL else @to_time end,@society_in,@amount,@note, @facility_id,0,@capacity)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.facility_booking set  society_id=@society_id ,capacity=@capacity, book_date=@book_date,from_date=@from_date,to_date=@to_date,name=dbo.InitCap(@name),address=dbo.InitCap(@address),flat_id=@flat_id,contact=@contact,from_time=@from_time,to_time=@to_time,society_in=@society_in,amount=@amount,note=@note,facility_id=@facility_id where facility_book_id=@facility_book_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.facility_booking where facility_book_id=@facility_book_id
		   End
    	
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update dbo.facility_booking set active_status=1 where facility_book_id=@facility_book_id
	   End
    	
		IF @Operation = 'GetBookingList'
    BEGIN
        SELECT        fb.facility_book_id, f.facility_id, CONVERT(varchar(15), fb.book_date, 106) AS book_date, CONVERT(varchar(15), fb.from_date, 106) AS from_date, CONVERT(varchar(15), fb.to_date, 106) AS to_date, CONVERT(varchar(15), 
                         CAST(fb.from_time AS TIME), 100) AS from_time, CONVERT(varchar(15), CAST(fb.to_time AS TIME), 100) AS to_time, fb.amount, f.name, fb.flat_id, fb.name AS u_name
FROM            dbo.facility_booking AS fb INNER JOIN
                         dbo.facilities AS f ON fb.facility_id = f.facility_id
WHERE        (fb.active_status = 0)
          AND fb.flat_id =@flat_id;
    END

		 If @operation='GetFlat'


	begin 
	 select  flat_no,name, flat_id from owner_search_vw where owner_id =@owner_id and active_status=0
	 end

	  If @operation='GetCharge'

	begin 
	 select cost,slot from facilities where facility_id =@facility_id and active_status=0
	 end

    If @operation='Grid_Show'
	begin 
	 SELECT * from dbo.facility_booking_vw  where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id ORDER BY facility_book_id DESC
	 end
	 If @operation='Search'
	begin 
	 SELECT * from dbo.facility_booking_vw  where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and( name like @search+'%' or facility_name like @search+'%' or cast(from_date as varchar) like @search+'%' or cast(to_date as varchar) like @search+'%' or cast ( amount as varchar) like @search+'%')  ORDER BY facility_book_id DESC
	 end

	     IF @operation = 'GetSlots'
    BEGIN
        SELECT  
            facility_id,
            slot_id,
            CONVERT(VARCHAR(15), CAST(start_time AS TIME), 100) AS start_time,
            CONVERT(VARCHAR(15), CAST(end_time AS TIME), 100) AS end_time,
            society_id,
            status
        FROM facilitySlotBooking
        WHERE active_status = 0 
          AND from_date = @date
          AND facility_id = @facility_id

        UNION

        SELECT  
            facility_id,
            slot_id,
            CONVERT(VARCHAR(15), CAST(start_time AS TIME), 100) AS start_time,
            CONVERT(VARCHAR(15), CAST(end_time AS TIME), 100) AS end_time,
            society_id,
            0 AS status
        FROM slot_master
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND facility_id = @facility_id
          AND slot_id NOT IN (
              SELECT slot_id
              FROM facilitySlotBooking
              WHERE active_status = 0 
                AND from_date = @date
          );
    END


if @operation = 'fill_facilities'
begin
Select * from facilities where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id
end

if @operation = 'fill_owner'
begin
Select * from owner_master where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id
end
if @operation = 'CheckAvailability'
begin
SELECT 
    fb.facility_book_id,
    fb.from_date,
    fb.to_date,
    fb.from_time,
    fb.to_time,
    fb.name,
    fb.amount,
    fb.note
FROM facility_booking fb
WHERE fb.facility_id = @facility_id
  AND fb.active_status = 0
  AND (
        -- Case 1: Daily booking (date range overlap)
        (
          @from_time IS NULL AND @toTime IS NULL
          AND fb.from_date <= @toDate 
          AND fb.to_date >= @fromDate
        )
        OR
        -- Case 2: Hourly booking (same date + time overlap)
        (
          @from_time IS NOT NULL AND @toTime IS NOT NULL
          AND fb.from_date = @fromDate
          AND fb.from_time < @toTime    -- existing starts before new ends
          AND fb.to_time > @fromTime    -- existing ends after new starts
        )
      );
end

end


GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_flat_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:41:04 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
