SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_flat_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL,
@flat_id INT=0,
@wing_id INT=0,
@flat_type_id INT=0,
@bed_id INT=0,
@build_id int=0,
@usage_id INT=0,
@flat_no NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@intercom_no NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@terrace_sq_ft NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@sq_ft NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@society_id NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@flat_status INT=0,
@name NVARCHAR (200)=NULL,
@w_name NVARCHAR (100)=NULL,
@search varchar(200)=null,
@type varchar(50)=null
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @flat_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = flat_id
                    FROM     dbo.flat_master
                    ORDER BY flat_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.flat_master (flat_id,society_id, wing_id, bed_id, usage_id, flat_no, terrace_sq_ft, intercom_no, sq_ft,flat_type_id ,flat_status,active_status, is_rented)
                    VALUES                      (@new,@society_id, @wing_id, @bed_id, @usage_id, @flat_no, @terrace_sq_ft, @intercom_no, @sq_ft,@flat_type_id ,0,0,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.flat_master
                    SET 
                           society_id    = @society_id,
                           usage_id      = @usage_id,
                           sq_ft         = @sq_ft,
                           wing_id          = @wing_id,
                           bed_id        = @bed_id,
                           flat_no       = @flat_no,
                           terrace_sq_ft = @terrace_sq_ft,
                           intercom_no   = @intercom_no,
						   @flat_type_id = @flat_type_id,
                           flat_status   = @flat_status
                    WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.flat_master
            WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.flat_master set active_status=1
            WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id;
        END
  
    IF @operation = 'check_no'
        BEGIN
            IF @flat_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.flat_master
                WHERE  flat_no = @flat_no and active_status=0
                       AND wing_id = @wing_id
                       AND society_id = @society_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.flat_master
                WHERE  flat_no = @flat_no and active_status=0
                       AND flat_id = @flat_id
                       AND flat_id <> @flat_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_count'
        BEGIN
             SELECT COUNT(Distinct flat_id) as flat from flat_master 
			 where  active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id;
        END

		

   

		 IF @operation = 'Fill_list'
        BEGIN
		if @type='wing'
          Select wing_id,(name + w_name) as name from global_society_view where society_id=@society_id

		  if @type='flat_types'
		  select * from types

		  if @type='flat_usage'
		  select * from flat_usage

		  if @type='flat_bedroom'
		  select * from bedroom


            end


    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     flat
            WHERE active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY flat_id DESC;
        END
		IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     flat
            WHERE active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and( build_wing like @search+'%'or flat_no like @search+'%'or bed like @search+'%'
			or sq_ft like @search+'%' or flat_type like @search+'%'or usage like @search+'%')
            ORDER BY flat_id DESC;
        END
    IF @operation = 'getwing'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   global_society_view
            WHERE  name = @name and active_status=0
                   AND w_name = @w_name
                   AND society_id = @society_id;
        END
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_helpdesk]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:43:20 ******/

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_helpdesk]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, @build_id INT=0, @helpdesk_id INT=0,
@society_id nvarchar(50)=null, @query nvarchar(MAX)=null,
@flat_id int=0, @category nvarchar(50)=null,
@req_service_date smalldatetime=null, @urgency int=0,
@status int=1, @date smalldatetime=null,
@active_status int=0,
@owner_id int=0,
@comment_id int = null,
@description nvarchar(MAX)=null,
@type nvarchar(50)=null,
@logintype nvarchar(50)=null
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
	IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @helpdesk_id = 0
                BEGIN
                    DECLARE @new AS INT;
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new=helpdesk_id
                    FROM     dbo.HelpdeskRequest
                    ORDER BY helpdesk_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                       OR @new = 0
                        SELECT @new = 1;
                    ELSE
                        SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                    INSERT  INTO HelpdeskRequest(helpdesk_id,query,flat_id,category,type,req_service_date,urgency,status,date,active_status,owner_id,logintype)
                    VALUES                      (@new,@query,@flat_id,@category,@type,getdate(),@urgency,@status,getdate(),@active_status,@owner_id,@logintype);
					select @new as helpdesk_id
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.HelpdeskRequest
                    SET   query=@query,
					flat_id=@flat_id,
					category=@category,
					req_service_date=@req_service_date,
					urgency=@urgency,
					status=@status,
					date=@date,
					owner_id=@owner_id,
					active_status=@active_status,
					logintype=@logintype

                    WHERE  helpdesk_id=@helpdesk_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Get_Request'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   dbo.HelpdeskRequest.query,
                     STRING_AGG(dbo.HelpdeskImages.documents, ',') AS image,
                     dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name,
                     dbo.person_type.p_type_name AS category,
                     CONVERT (VARCHAR (20), dbo.HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date, 106) AS req_service_date,
                     CONVERT (VARCHAR (20), dbo.HelpdeskRequest.date, 100) AS date,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.name,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit
            FROM     dbo.HelpdeskRequest
                     INNER JOIN
                     dbo.owner_search_vw
                     ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.flat_id = dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id
                     INNER JOIN
                     dbo.person_type
                     ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.category = dbo.person_type.p_type_id
                     LEFT OUTER JOIN
                     dbo.HelpdeskImages
                     ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id = dbo.HelpdeskImages.helpdesk_id
           -- WHERE    (dbo.owner_search_vw.society_id = @society_id and dbo.helpdeskRequest.active_status=0)
            GROUP BY dbo.HelpdeskRequest.query, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name, dbo.person_type.p_type_name, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date, HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.date, dbo.owner_search_vw.name, dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit;
        END
    IF @operation = 'GetRequestById'
        BEGIN
      --      SELECT   dbo.HelpdeskRequest.query,
      --               STRING_AGG(dbo.HelpdeskImages.documents, ',') AS image,
      --               dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id,
					 --dbo.HelpdeskRequest.status,
      --               dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id,
      --               dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name,
      --               ct.c_type_name AS categoryName,
      --               CONVERT (VARCHAR (20), dbo.HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date, 106) AS req_service_date,
      --               CONVERT (VARCHAR (20), dbo.HelpdeskRequest.date, 100) AS date,
      --               dbo.owner_search_vw.name,
      --               dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit
      --      FROM     dbo.HelpdeskRequest
      --               INNER JOIN
      --               dbo.owner_search_vw
      --               ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.flat_id = dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id
      --               INNER JOIN
      --               dbo.complaint_type ct
      --               ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.category = ct.c_type_id
      --               LEFT OUTER JOIN
      --               dbo.HelpdeskImages
      --               ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id = dbo.HelpdeskImages.helpdesk_id
      --      WHERE    (dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id = @helpdesk_id and dbo.helpdeskRequest.active_status=0)
      --      GROUP BY dbo.HelpdeskRequest.query,dbo.HelpdeskRequest.status, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name, ct.c_type_name , dbo.HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date, HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.date, dbo.owner_search_vw.name, dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit;
    SELECT   
    hr.query,
    STRING_AGG(hi.documents, ',') AS image,
    hr.helpdesk_id,
    hr.status,
    u.build_id,
    u.build_name,
    ct.c_type_name AS categoryName,
    CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), hr.req_service_date, 106) AS req_service_date,
    CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), hr.date, 100) AS date,
    u.name,
    u.Unit
FROM dbo.HelpdeskRequest hr
INNER JOIN dbo.UserData u
    ON hr.flat_id = u.flat_id
    AND hr.owner_id = u.owner_id
    AND hr.logintype = u.login
INNER JOIN dbo.complaint_type ct
    ON hr.category = ct.c_type_id
LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.HelpdeskImages hi
    ON hr.helpdesk_id = hi.helpdesk_id
WHERE hr.helpdesk_id = @helpdesk_id
  AND hr.active_status = 0
GROUP BY 
    hr.query,
    hr.status, 
    hr.helpdesk_id, 
    u.build_id, 
    u.build_name, 
    ct.c_type_name, 
    hr.req_service_date, 
    hr.date, 
    u.name, 
    u.Unit;





	
	END
		

		IF @operation ='GetComments'
		Begin 
SELECT        CASE WHEN dbo.UserData.name IS NULL THEN UserLogin.name + ' (' + UserType.USerTypeName + ')' ELSE USerdata.Name END AS name, dbo.HelpDeskComments.helpdesk_id, dbo.HelpDeskComments.comment_id, 
                         dbo.HelpDeskComments.owner_id, dbo.UserData.Unit, dbo.HelpDeskComments.description, CONVERT(varchar, dbo.HelpDeskComments.dateTime, 100) AS dateTime, dbo.HelpDeskComments.type
FROM            dbo.UserData RIGHT OUTER JOIN
                         dbo.HelpDeskComments LEFT OUTER JOIN
                         dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId ON dbo.HelpDeskComments.type = dbo.UserType.UserTypeName AND dbo.HelpDeskComments.owner_id = dbo.UserLogin.owner_id ON 
                         dbo.UserData.owner_id = dbo.HelpDeskComments.owner_id AND dbo.UserData.login = dbo.HelpDeskComments.type where  HelpDeskComments.active_status=0 and helpdesk_id=@helpdesk_id order by comment_id 
		END
		IF @operation ='GetTickets'
		Begin 
--WITH LatestComment AS (
--    SELECT 
--        hc.helpdesk_id,
--        hc.helpdesk_id AS notification_id,
--        ns.seen_status,
--		    ns.notify_status_id,   
--        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (

--        ) AS rn
--    FROM HelpDeskComments hc
--    LEFT JOIN notify_status ns
--        ON ns.notification_id = hc.helpdesk_id
--)
--SELECT

--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.query,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.urgency,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.status,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.category,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.flat_id,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.active_status,
--    CONVERT(varchar, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.date, 100) AS date,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.type AS category_type,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date,
--    dbo.HelpdeskRequest.created_at,
--    dbo.UserData.owner_id,
--    dbo.UserData.society_id,
--    dbo.UserData.name,
--    dbo.UserData.flat_no,
--    dbo.complaint_type.c_type_name AS p_type_name,

--    -- ✅ ONLY ADDING THESE 2 FIELDS
--    lc.notification_id,
--    lc.seen_status,
--	  lc.notify_status_id   

--FROM dbo.HelpdeskRequest
--INNER JOIN dbo.UserData 
--        ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.flat_id = dbo.UserData.flat_id 
--       AND dbo.HelpdeskRequest.owner_id = dbo.UserData.owner_id
--INNER JOIN dbo.complaint_type 
--        ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.category = dbo.complaint_type.c_type_id
--LEFT JOIN LatestComment lc
--        ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id = lc.helpdesk_id
--       AND lc.rn = 1
--WHERE dbo.UserData.society_id = @society_id and  dbo.HelpdeskRequest.active_status=0
--ORDER BY dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id DESC;

--WITH LatestComment AS (
--    SELECT 
--        hc.helpdesk_id,
--        hc.helpdesk_id AS notification_id,
--        ns.seen_status,
--        ns.notify_status_id,
--        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
--            PARTITION BY hc.helpdesk_id
--            ORDER BY hc.dateTime DESC
--        ) AS rn
--    FROM HelpDeskComments hc
--    LEFT JOIN notify_status ns
--        ON ns.notification_id = hc.helpdesk_id
--       AND ns.user_id = @owner_id
--       AND ns.notification_type = 'helpdesk comment'
--)

--SELECT 
--    hr.helpdesk_id,
--    hr.query,
--    hr.urgency,
--    hr.status,
--    hr.category,
--    hr.flat_id,
--    hr.active_status,
--    CONVERT(VARCHAR, hr.date, 100) AS date,
--    hr.type AS category_type,
--    hr.req_service_date,
--    hr.created_at,

--    u.owner_id,
--    u.name,
--    u.flat_no,
--    u.society_id,

--    ct.c_type_name AS p_type_name,

--    lc.notification_id,
--    lc.seen_status,
--    lc.notify_status_id

--FROM HelpdeskRequest hr

---- Only requests from this society
--INNER JOIN UserData u
--       ON hr.owner_id = u.owner_id
--      AND u.society_id = @society_id

---- Category info
--LEFT JOIN complaint_type ct 
--       ON hr.category = ct.c_type_id

---- Latest comment
--LEFT JOIN LatestComment lc
--       ON hr.helpdesk_id = lc.helpdesk_id
--      AND lc.rn = 1

--ORDER BY hr.helpdesk_id DESC;
WITH LatestComment AS (
    SELECT 
        hc.helpdesk_id,
        hc.helpdesk_id AS notification_id,
        ns.seen_status,
        ns.notify_status_id,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY hc.helpdesk_id
            ORDER BY hc.dateTime DESC
        ) AS rn
    FROM HelpDeskComments hc
    LEFT JOIN notify_status ns
        ON ns.notification_id = hc.helpdesk_id
       AND ns.user_id = @owner_id
       AND ns.notification_type = 'helpdesk comment'
),
UserDataCombined AS (
    -- Owner entries
    SELECT 
        owner_id,
        name,
        flat_no,
        society_id,
        type AS login_type,
        'Owner' as user_source
    FROM owner_search_vw
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Family entries
    SELECT 
        o_ex_id AS owner_id,
        name,
        flat_no,
        society_id,
        'Family' AS login_type,
        'Family' as user_source
    FROM owner_family
)
SELECT 
    hr.helpdesk_id,
    hr.query,
    hr.urgency,
    hr.status,
    hr.category,
    hr.flat_id,
    hr.active_status,
    CONVERT(VARCHAR, hr.date, 100) AS date,
    hr.type AS category_type,
    hr.req_service_date,
    hr.created_at,
    hr.logintype,  -- Include this from HelpdeskRequest
    u.owner_id,
    u.name,
    u.flat_no,
    u.society_id,
    u.login_type,
    ct.c_type_name AS p_type_name,
    lc.notification_id,
    lc.seen_status,
    lc.notify_status_id
FROM HelpdeskRequest hr
-- Join with combined user data, matching on BOTH owner_id AND logintype
INNER JOIN UserDataCombined u
       ON hr.owner_id = u.owner_id
      AND hr.logintype = u.login_type  -- THIS IS KEY!
      AND u.society_id = @society_id
-- Category info
LEFT JOIN complaint_type ct 
       ON hr.category = ct.c_type_id
-- Latest comment
LEFT JOIN LatestComment lc
       ON hr.helpdesk_id = lc.helpdesk_id
      AND lc.rn = 1
ORDER BY hr.helpdesk_id DESC;
		--SELECT *
  --  FROM Helpdesk_vw
  --  WHERE active_status = 0 and society_id=@society_id;
		END
		IF @operation ='DeleteRequest'
		Begin 
		Update HelpdeskRequest set active_status=1 where helpdesk_id=@helpdesk_id
		Update HelpdeskImages set active_status=1 where helpdesk_id=@helpdesk_id
		END
		  If @operation='SupportTicket'
	   Begin
SELECT        ct.c_type_name as p_type_name, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.query, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.helpdesk_id, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.req_service_date, dbo.HelpdeskRequest.urgency, dbo.HelpdeskStatus.status, dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit,
                          dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name, dbo.owner_search_vw.owner_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id
FROM            dbo.HelpdeskRequest INNER JOIN
                         dbo.complaint_type ct ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.category = ct.c_type_id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.HelpdeskStatus ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.status = dbo.HelpdeskStatus.id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.owner_search_vw ON dbo.HelpdeskRequest.flat_id = dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id where   society_id=@society_id order by req_service_date
	   End 
	   IF @operation = 'HelpdeksBYOwner'
        BEGIN
    SELECT *
    FROM Helpdesk_vw
    WHERE active_status = 0  
      AND flat_id = @flat_id;
        END
		if @operation='InsertComments'
		Begin
		   DECLARE @new1 AS INT;
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new1=comment_id
                    FROM     dbo.HelpDeskComments
                    ORDER BY comment_id DESC;
                    IF @new1 IS NULL
                       OR @new1 = 0
                        SELECT @new1 = 1;
                    ELSE
                        SELECT @new1 = @new1 + 1;
		 INSERT INTO HelpdeskComments (comment_id,helpdesk_id, owner_id, flat_id,type,dateTime, description, status, active_status)
    VALUES (@new1,@helpdesk_id, @owner_id, @flat_id,@type,getdate(), @description,  1, 0);
 
		End

IF @operation='updaterequest'
BEGIN
    IF @status = 1
    BEGIN
        -- Update HelpdeskRequest for this helpdesk_id to status = 4
        UPDATE HelpdeskRequest
        SET status = 4
        WHERE helpdesk_id = @helpdesk_id
          AND active_status = 0;

    END
    ELSE IF @status = 2
    BEGIN
        -- Soft delete the specific comment
        UPDATE HelpdeskComments
        SET active_status = 1
        WHERE comment_id = @comment_id;
    END
END

IF @operation = 'UpdateStatus'
BEGIN
    UPDATE dbo.HelpdeskRequest
    SET status = @status
    WHERE helpdesk_id = @helpdesk_id AND active_status = 0;
END

 IF @operation = 'GetAllHelpdeskStatus'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM HelpdeskStatus; -- optional filter if you have an active_status column
    END

	IF @operation = 'getOwnerToken'  -- for the support ticket updates
		BEGIN
			select name, owner_id as user_id,type,token from owner_search_vw where token is not null and active_status=0 and owner_id = @owner_id;
		END

		IF @operation = 'getUserTokan'    -- for the vendor Approvels
		BEGIN
			 select name, owner_id as user_id,type, web_token from UserLogin where web_token is not null and active_status=0  and user_id = @owner_id;
		END

		if @operation = 'getFlatTokens'
			BEGIN
				select token,owner_id,type, is_rented from UserData where flat_id = @flat_id and token is not null --AND type=(case when is_rented=0 then 'Owner' else 'Rent' end)
			END;
		

	IF @operation = 'getImage'
		BEGIN	
			select documents from HelpdeskImages where helpdesk_id= @helpdesk_id
		END
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_house]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:43:44 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_house]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@house_id int=0,
@village_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@house_no int=0,
@house_type int=0,
@area decimal(18,2) = NULL,
@gharpatti_charges DECIMAL(18,2)=NULL,
@no_of_tab INT=0,
@water_charges decimal(18, 2)=NULL,
@waste_charges decimal(18, 2)=NULL,
@audt_modify_id int=0

AS
BEGIN
IF @operation = 'Update'
BEGIN
    DECLARE @finalId INT = 0;

    IF @house_id = 0
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @new INT = 0;

        SELECT TOP (1) @new = house_id
        FROM dbo.house
        ORDER BY house_id DESC;

        IF @new IS NULL
            SET @new = 1; 
        ELSE
            SET @new = @new + 1;

        INSERT INTO house(house_id, house_no, house_type, AREA, village_id,
                          gharpatti_charges, no_of_tab, water_charges, waste_charges,audt_modify_id)
        VALUES (@new, @house_no, @house_type, @AREA, @village_id,
                @gharpatti_charges, @no_of_tab, @water_charges, @waste_charges,@audt_modify_id);

        SET @finalId = @new;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        UPDATE house
        SET house_no        = @house_no,
            area            = @area,
            gharpatti_charges = @gharpatti_charges,
            no_of_tab       = @no_of_tab,
            water_charges   = @water_charges,
            waste_charges   = @waste_charges,
			audt_modify_id  = @audt_modify_id
        WHERE house_id = @house_id;

        SET @finalId = @house_id;
    END

    -- return the ID
    SELECT @finalId AS house_id;
END

          
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM  house
            WHERE  house_id = @house_id;
        END

   
   
   

      IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
      SELECT dbo.house.house_no, dbo.house_type.house_type, dbo.house.area, dbo.house_owner.name, dbo.house.gharpatti_charges, dbo.house.village_id, dbo.house.no_of_tab, dbo.house.water_charges, dbo.house.waste_charges, 
                  dbo.house.house_id, dbo.house_owner.address, dbo.house_owner.pre_mob, dbo.house_owner.village_owner_id
      FROM     dbo.house INNER JOIN
                  dbo.house_owner ON dbo.house.house_id = dbo.house_owner.house_id INNER JOIN
                  dbo.house_type ON dbo.house.house_type = dbo.house_type.house_type_id
            WHERE  dbo.house.village_id=@village_id  ; 
       END

	 IF @operation = 'Grid_Show_hist'
        BEGIN
    SELECT        dbo.house.house_no, dbo.house_type.house_type, dbo.house.area, dbo.house_owner.name, dbo.house.gharpatti_charges, dbo.house.village_id, dbo.house.no_of_tab, dbo.house.water_charges, dbo.house.waste_charges, 
                         dbo.house.house_id, dbo.house_owner.address, dbo.house_owner.pre_mob, dbo.house_owner.village_owner_id, dbo.UserLogin.name AS audt_modify_name, dbo.house.audt_modify_id
    FROM            dbo.house INNER JOIN
                         dbo.house_owner ON dbo.house.house_id = dbo.house_owner.house_id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.house_type ON dbo.house.house_type = dbo.house_type.house_type_id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.UserLogin ON dbo.house.audt_modify_id = dbo.UserLogin.user_id
            WHERE  dbo.house.village_id=@village_id  ; 
       END
    IF @operation = 'house_history'
        BEGIN
	   SELECT        TOP (100) PERCENT dbo.house.house_no, dbo.UserLogin.name AS updated_by, dbo.house$ARC.modification_date, dbo.house$ARC.action_type, dbo.house$ARC.area, dbo.house$ARC.gharpatti_charges, 
                         dbo.house$ARC.no_of_tab, dbo.house$ARC.water_charges, dbo.house$ARC.waste_charges, dbo.house.house_id
       FROM            dbo.house$ARC INNER JOIN
                         dbo.house ON dbo.house$ARC.house_id = dbo.house.house_id AND dbo.house$ARC.village_id = dbo.house.village_id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.UserLogin ON dbo.house$ARC.audt_modify_id = dbo.UserLogin.user_id
						 WHERE  dbo.house.village_id=@village_id  
       ORDER BY dbo.house$ARC.modification_date DESC;
	   
	   END


   END

 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_house_owner]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@village_owner_id int=0,
@name NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@pre_mob NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@alter_mob NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@village_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@house_id int=0,
@id_proof NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL, 
@address nvarchar(50)=Null

AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @village_owner_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = village_owner_id
                    FROM     dbo.house_owner
                    ORDER BY village_owner_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    
                    INSERT  INTO house_owner(village_owner_id,name,house_id,address,pre_mob,village_id,alter_mob,id_proof,active_status)
                    VALUES                          (@new,@name,@house_id,@address,@pre_mob,@village_id,@alter_mob,@id_proof,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE house_owner
                    SET    name         =  @name,
                           house_id     = @house_id,
                           address      = @address,
						   pre_mob      = @pre_mob,
						   village_id   = @village_id,
						   alter_mob    = @alter_mob,
						   id_proof     = @id_proof
                    where  village_owner_id = @village_owner_id;
                END
        END
             
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM  house_owner
            WHERE  village_owner_id = @village_owner_id;
        END

    --IF @operation = 'Delete'
    --    BEGIN
    --        SELECT @flat_id = flat_id
    --        FROM   owner_master
    --        WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
    --        UPDATE flat_master
    --        SET    flat_status = 0
    --        WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id;

    --        UPDATE dbo.owner_master set login_status=1,active_status=1
    --        WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
    --       UPDATE dbo.owner_extension set login_status= 1,active_status=1
    --        WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
    --    END
   
   If @operation='house_id_fetch'
	  Begin
	   Select house_id from house_owner where village_id= @village_id and active_status=0 ORDER BY house_id DESC
    	End
			If @operation='address_fetch'
	  Begin
	   SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM( ISNULL(division, '') + ', ' + ISNULL(district, '') + ', ' +  ISNULL(state, '') )) AS address_line1 FROM village_master_vw  where village_id= @village_id and active_status=0 ORDER BY house_id DESC
     	End




		 If @operation='Delete'
	Begin
	  
	 Update house_owner set active_status=1 where village_owner_id=@village_owner_id
	
	End

      IF @operation = 'GetVillageId'
    BEGIN
    --SELECT village_id
    --FROM house_owner
    --WHERE village_owner_id = @village_owner_id;

	   IF @operation = 'GetVillageId'
    BEGIN

	IF @village_owner_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new1 AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new1 = village_owner_id
                    FROM     dbo.house_owner
                    ORDER BY village_owner_id DESC;
                    IF @new1 IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new1 = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new1 = @new1 + 1;
                        END

    SELECT village_id
    FROM house_owner
    WHERE village_owner_id = @village_owner_id;
         END

     END

 END

  If @operation='check_pre_mob'
	   Begin
	    If @village_owner_id=0
		Select * from dbo.house_owner Where name=@name AND pre_mob=@pre_mob and active_status=0
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.house_owner Where  name=@name AND pre_mob=@pre_mob and active_status=0 AND village_owner_id=@village_owner_id AND village_owner_id<>@village_owner_id
	   End
 
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_house_tax]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:44:28 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_house_tax]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@house_tax_id int=0,
@village_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@house_no int=0,
@house_type_id int=0, 
@from_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@to_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@house_tax_amount decimal(18, 2)=NULL,
@bill_gen smalldatetime=Null,
@due_date smalldatetime=null,
@owner_id int=0,
@chqno nvarchar(50)=NULL,
@due decimal(18, 2)=NULL,
@house_tax_bill_no int=0

AS
BEGIN
      
	   IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM  house_tax
            WHERE  house_tax_id = @house_tax_id;
        END

      IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
          SELECT * from house_tax
            WHERE active_status=0 
                     AND village_id=@village_id   
        END

		 If @operation='Delete'
	Begin
	  
	 Update house_tax set active_status=1 where house_tax_id = @house_tax_id
	
	End
	

	If @operation='generate'

	 Begin
	  Declare house_type_cur cursor for 
	  select distinct house_type,village_id from house_owner where active_status=0
	  OPEN house_type_cur
	  FETCH NEXT FROM house_type_cur into @house_type_id,@village_id
	  while(@@FETCH_STATUS=0)
		BEGIN 
		select @bill_gen=bill_gen_date from square_ft_rate where house_type_id=@house_type_id and village_id=@village_id and  active_status=0
	if  convert(varchar,@bill_gen,105) = convert(varchar,getdate(),105)
	begin
			declare cur cursor 
			for select house_no,isnull(sq_ft*(select rate from square_ft_rate where house_type_id=@house_type_id and village_id=@village_id and  active_status=0),0), 
			(select bill_gen_date from square_ft_rate where house_type_id=@house_type_id and village_id=@village_id and  active_status=0),
			(select due_date from square_ft_rate where house_type_id=@house_type_id and village_id=@village_id and  active_status=0) 
			from house_owner where active_status=0 and house_type=@house_type_id and village_id=@village_id
			open cur 
			fetch next from cur into @house_no,@house_tax_amount,@bill_gen,@due_date 
			while(@@FETCH_STATUS=0)
				 BEGIN 
					  DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
									SELECT   TOP (1) @new = house_tax_id
									FROM     dbo.house_tax
									ORDER BY house_tax_id DESC;
									IF @new IS NULL
										BEGIN
											SELECT @new = 0;
										END
									ELSE
										BEGIN
											SELECT @new = @new + 1;
										END
                    
									INSERT  INTO house_tax(house_tax_id,house_no,house_type,village_id,from_date,to_date,house_tax_amount,active_status,due,advance,house_tax_bill_no)
									VALUES                          (@new,@house_no,@house_type_id,@village_id,@bill_gen,@due_date,@house_tax_amount,0,@house_tax_amount,0,@new);
				 
			  fetch next from cur into @house_no,@house_tax_amount,@bill_gen,@due_date 
			  END
			 CLOSE cur
			 DEALLOCATE cur
			 end
		  FETCH NEXT FROM house_type_cur into @house_type_id,@village_id
		
		
	  End
	   CLOSE house_type_cur
		 DEALLOCATE house_type_cur
 END

  If @operation='house_tax_fetch'
	Begin
	    select sum(due) as due from house_tax where house_no=@house_no
		
		end   

	  If @operation='cheque_select'
     Begin
	
		SELECT dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date,dbo.pdc_reminder.che_amount  FROM dbo.pdc_reminder WHERE chqno=@chqno and owner_id=@owner_id
  End 

 End

  
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_house_tax_receipt]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:45:27 ******/

CREATE procedure [dbo].[sp_house_tax_receipt]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@house_receipt_id int=0,
@village_id nvarchar(50)=null,
@house_id int=0,
@amount_paid decimal=0,
@pay_date Date=NULL,
@pay_mode int=0,
@chqno nvarchar(50)=null,
@chqdate datetime=NULL,
@receipt_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@payment_type int=0,
@all_housereceipt_id nvarchar(600) =null,
@Transation_ref   nvarchar(50)=null,
@remark nvarchar(200)=null

AS
BEGIN

    If @operation='Update'

      
	Begin
	If @house_receipt_id=0
	begin
	 DECLARE @new AS int =0
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = house_receipt_id
                    FROM     dbo.house_tax_receipt
                    ORDER BY house_receipt_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
	     Insert into house_tax_receipt(house_receipt_id,house_id ,amount_paid,pay_date,pay_mode,payment_status,village_id,chqno,chqdate,receipt_no,payment_type,Transation_ref,remark)values(@new,@house_id,@amount_paid,getdate(),@pay_mode,0,@village_id,@chqno,@chqdate,@receipt_no,@payment_type,@Transation_ref, @remark)

		end
    else
	begin
	     Update house_tax_receipt set house_id=@house_id,amount_paid=@amount_paid,pay_mode=@pay_mode,village_id=@village_id,chqno=@chqno,chqdate=@chqdate,receipt_no=@receipt_no,Transation_ref=@Transation_ref where house_receipt_id=@house_receipt_id
	End
	End

	 If @operation='Payment_update'
	Begin
	  
	 Update house_tax_receipt set payment_status=1 where house_receipt_id=@house_receipt_id
	  
	
	End

	 If @operation='Select'
	Begin
	  
	  Select * from house_tax_receipt where house_receipt_id=@house_receipt_id

	End

     If @operation='Grid_not_paid_p'
	   Begin
	     select * from house_wise_payment_vw where payment_status=0 and village_id=@village_id and house_id=@house_id and payment_type=1 ORDER BY house_receipt_id DESC
	   End

	    If @operation='Grid_paid_p'
	   Begin
	     select * from house_wise_payment_vw where payment_status=1 and village_id=@village_id and payment_type=1  ORDER BY house_receipt_id DESC
	   End

	    If @operation='Grid_not_paid_W'
	   Begin
	     select * from house_wise_payment_vw where payment_status=0 and village_id=@village_id and house_id=@house_id and payment_type=2 ORDER BY house_receipt_id DESC
	   End

	    If @operation='Grid_paid_W'
	   Begin
	     select * from house_wise_payment_vw where payment_status=1 and village_id=@village_id and payment_type=2  ORDER BY house_receipt_id DESC
	   End

	    If @operation='Grid_not_paid_M'
	   Begin
	     select * from house_wise_payment_vw where payment_status=0 and village_id=@village_id and house_id=@house_id and payment_type=3 ORDER BY house_receipt_id DESC
	   End

	    If @operation='Grid_paid_M'
	   Begin
	     select * from house_wise_payment_vw where payment_status=1 and village_id=@village_id and payment_type=3  ORDER BY house_receipt_id DESC
	   End


	 If @operation='receipt_fetch'
	  Begin
	   Select receipt_no from house_tax_receipt where village_id= @village_id  ORDER BY receipt_no DESC
    	End

If @operation='propety_tax_bill_gen'
	  Begin
		DECLARE @house_id1 int,@receipt_no1 int=1001, @gharpatti_charges DECIMAL(18,2); 
DECLARE Village_bill_gen CURSOR FOR
SELECT  house_id, gharpatti_charges  FROM  dbo.house where village_id='V10004'

OPEN Village_bill_gen;

FETCH NEXT FROM Village_bill_gen INTO @house_id1, @gharpatti_charges;

WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN
   --Select receipt_no into @receipt_no  from house_tax_receipt where village_id='V10004'and payment_type=1
   
   ;--    ='PTX001';
 Insert into house_tax_receipt(house_id ,amount_paid,pay_date,pay_mode,payment_status,village_id,chqno,chqdate,receipt_no,payment_type)values(@house_id1,@gharpatti_charges,getdate(),null,0,'V10004',null,null,@receipt_no1,1)
  set @receipt_no1=@receipt_no1+1;

FETCH NEXT FROM Village_bill_gen INTO @house_id1, @gharpatti_charges;
END

CLOSE Village_bill_gen;
DEALLOCATE Village_bill_gen;
END

If @operation='water_bill_gen'
	  Begin
		DECLARE @house_id2 int,@receipt_no2 int=2001, @water_charges DECIMAL(18,2); 
DECLARE Village_bill_gen CURSOR FOR
SELECT  house_id, water_charges  FROM  dbo.house where village_id='V10004'

OPEN Village_bill_gen;

FETCH NEXT FROM Village_bill_gen INTO @house_id2, @water_charges;

WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN
   --Select receipt_no into @receipt_no  from house_tax_receipt where village_id='V10004'and payment_type=1
   
   ;--    ='PTX001';
 Insert into house_tax_receipt(house_id ,amount_paid,pay_date,pay_mode,payment_status,village_id,chqno,chqdate,receipt_no,payment_type)values(@house_id2,@water_charges,getdate(),null,0,'V10004',null,null,@receipt_no2,2)
  set @receipt_no2=@receipt_no2+1;

FETCH NEXT FROM Village_bill_gen INTO @house_id2, @water_charges;
END

CLOSE Village_bill_gen;
DEALLOCATE Village_bill_gen;
END

If @operation='waste_bill_gen'
	  Begin
		DECLARE @house_id3 int,@receipt_no3 int=3001, @waste_charges DECIMAL(18,2); 
DECLARE Village_bill_gen CURSOR FOR
SELECT  house_id, waste_charges  FROM  dbo.house where village_id='V10004'

OPEN Village_bill_gen;

FETCH NEXT FROM Village_bill_gen INTO @house_id3, @waste_charges;

WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN
   --Select receipt_no into @receipt_no  from house_tax_receipt where village_id='V10004'and payment_type=1
   
   ;--    ='PTX001';
 Insert into house_tax_receipt(house_id ,amount_paid,pay_date,pay_mode,payment_status,village_id,chqno,chqdate,receipt_no,payment_type)values(@house_id3,@waste_charges,getdate(),null,0,'V10004',null,null,@receipt_no3,3)
  set @receipt_no3=@receipt_no3+1;

FETCH NEXT FROM Village_bill_gen INTO @house_id3, @waste_charges;
END

CLOSE Village_bill_gen;
DEALLOCATE Village_bill_gen;
END

--select * from house_tax_receipt

	 If @operation='Grid_pending_charges'
	Begin
	  
	 SELECT       house_id, owner_name, house_no, SUM(ISNULL(pending_property_tax, 0)) AS pending_property_tax, SUM(ISNULL(pending_water_charges, 0)) AS pending_water_charges, SUM(ISNULL(pending_waste_charges, 0)) as pending_waste_charges,pre_mob
                
    FROM            dbo.Housewise_pending_charges_vw

                         dbo.pay_mode.pay_mode, dbo.Village_payment_type.payment_type_name
FROM            dbo.house_owner INNER JOIN
                         dbo.house_tax_receipt ON dbo.house_owner.house_id = dbo.house_tax_receipt.house_id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.house ON dbo.house_tax_receipt.house_id = dbo.house.house_id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.pay_mode ON dbo.house_tax_receipt.pay_mode = dbo.pay_mode.pay_id INNER JOIN
                         dbo.Village_payment_type ON dbo.house_tax_receipt.payment_type = dbo.Village_payment_type.payment_type
WHERE        (dbo.house_tax_receipt.payment_status = 1) AND dbo.house_owner.village_id = @village_id

	End

	If @operation = 'get_receipt_data'
		Begin
	  
SELECT dbo.house_owner.address, dbo.house_owner.name, dbo.house_owner.pre_mob, dbo.house_tax_receipt.house_receipt_id, dbo.house_tax_receipt.receipt_no, dbo.house_tax_receipt.house_id, dbo.house_tax_receipt.payment_type, 
                  dbo.house_tax_receipt.pay_date, dbo.house_tax_receipt.Transation_ref, dbo.house_tax_receipt.Amount_paid, dbo.house_tax_receipt.chqdate, dbo.house_tax_receipt.chqno, dbo.house_owner.village_id, dbo.house.house_no, dbo.pay_mode.pay_mode
FROM     dbo.house_owner INNER JOIN
                  dbo.house_tax_receipt ON dbo.house_owner.house_id = dbo.house_tax_receipt.house_id INNER JOIN
                  dbo.house ON dbo.house_tax_receipt.house_id = dbo.house.house_id INNER JOIN
                  dbo.pay_mode ON dbo.house_tax_receipt.pay_mode = dbo.pay_mode.pay_id where payment_status = 1 AND dbo.house_tax_receipt.house_receipt_id = @house_receipt_id

			
		END
	
      IF @operation = 'Update_Payment'
        BEGIN
            DECLARE @housereceipt_id        INT,
                    @pending_amont     decimal(18,2)
                   
            DECLARE bill_paymentt CURSOR FOR
              select house_receipt_id,pending_amount from House_wise_payment_vw where house_receipt_id IN (SELECT value
              FROM STRING_SPLIT(@all_housereceipt_id, ','))
              and  village_id = @village_id;

            OPEN bill_paymentt;

            FETCH next FROM bill_paymentt INTO @housereceipt_id, @pending_amont;
          
            WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
              BEGIN
                  Update house_tax_receipt set amount_paid=@pending_amont,pay_date=getdate(),pay_mode=@pay_mode, payment_status=1, chqno=@chqno,chqdate=@chqdate,Transation_ref=@Transation_ref WHERE house_receipt_id=@housereceipt_id
                     
                FETCH next FROM bill_paymentt INTO @housereceipt_id, @pending_amont;

              END

            CLOSE bill_paymentt;

            DEALLOCATE bill_paymentt;
        END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_inventory_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:46:33 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

ALTER   PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_inventory_master]
    @operation NVARCHAR(20),              -- 'INSERT', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'VIEW', 'VIEWBYID'
    @item_id INT = 0,
    @item_name NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
      @total_amount DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
       @tax DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
    @quantity INT = 0,
    @unit NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @purchase_date DATE = NULL,
    @purchase_cost DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
    @vendor_id INT = 0,
	@vendor_bill_id int=0,
    @condition_status INT = 0,
    @last_audit_date DATE = NULL,
    @remarks NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	    @warranty INT = 0

AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- ================================
    -- INSERT
    -- ================================
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'UPDATE'
	if @item_id=0
    BEGIN
	
        INSERT INTO dbo.inventory_master
        (item_id,
            item_name, total_amount, tax, quantity, unit, society_id, 
            purchase_date, purchase_cost, vendor_id, condition_status, 
            last_audit_date, remarks, created_at, updated_at,vendor_bill_id,warranty
        )
        VALUES
        ( (SELECT ISNULL(MAX(item_id), 0) + 1 FROM inventory_master),
            @item_name, @total_amount, @tax, @quantity, @unit, @society_id,
            @purchase_date, @purchase_cost, @vendor_id, @condition_status,
            @last_audit_date, @remarks, GETDATE(), GETDATE(),@vendor_bill_id,@warranty
        );

       
    END;
	else
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.inventory_master
        SET
            item_name = @item_name,
            tax = @tax,
            total_amount = @total_amount,
            quantity = @quantity,
            unit = @unit,
            society_id = @society_id,
            purchase_date = @purchase_date,
            purchase_cost = @purchase_cost,
            vendor_id = @vendor_id,
            condition_status = @condition_status,
            last_audit_date = @last_audit_date,
            remarks = @remarks,
			warranty =@warranty,
            updated_at = GETDATE()
        WHERE item_id = @item_id;

      
    END;

    -- ================================
    -- DELETE
    -- ================================
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM dbo.inventory_master WHERE item_id = @item_id;
       
    END;

    -- ================================
    -- VIEW ALL
    -- ================================
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'Grid_Show'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            i.item_id,
            i.item_name,
			i.warranty,
            i.tax,
            i.total_amount,
            i.quantity,
            i.unit,
            i.society_id,
            i.purchase_date,
            i.purchase_cost,
            i.vendor_id,
            v.vendor_name,
            i.condition_status,
            i.last_audit_date,
            i.remarks,
            i.created_at,
            i.updated_at,
			  -- Warranty Expiry Date
        DATEADD(MONTH, i.warranty, i.purchase_date) AS warranty_last_date
        FROM dbo.inventory_master i
        LEFT JOIN dbo.vendor_master v ON i.vendor_id = v.vendor_id Inner Join  vendor_bills as vb on vb.bill_id=i.vendor_bill_id where vb.status=2 or vb.status =3 and i.society_id=@society_id
        ORDER BY i.item_id DESC;

    END;

    -- ================================
    -- VIEW BY ID
    -- ================================
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'Select'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            i.item_id,
            i.item_name,
            i.tax,
            i.total_amount,
			i.warranty,
            i.quantity,
            i.unit,
            i.society_id,
            i.purchase_date,
            i.purchase_cost,
            i.vendor_id,
            v.vendor_name,
            i.condition_status,
            i.last_audit_date,
            i.remarks,
            i.created_at,
            i.updated_at
        FROM dbo.inventory_master i
        LEFT JOIN dbo.vendor_master v ON i.vendor_id = v.vendor_id
        WHERE i.item_id = @item_id;

        RETURN;
    END;

	IF @operation = 'update_Condition'
		BEGIN
			update inventory_master set condition_status = @condition_status where item_id = @item_id
		END

    -- ================================
    -- INVALID OPERATION
    -- ================================
  
END;

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_ledger]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:54:22 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO







CREATE procedure [dbo].[sp_ledger]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@led_id int=0,
@led_description nvarchar(MAX)=null,
@led_status nvarchar(50)=null,
@society_id nvarchar(50)=null,
@search nvarchar(50)=null

AS
BEGIN

    If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	If @led_id=0
	begin
	 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = led_id
                    FROM     dbo.ledger
                    ORDER BY led_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
	     Insert into ledger(led_id,led_description,led_status,society_id,date,active_status)values(@new,@led_description,@led_status,@society_id,getdate(),0)
		 begin
		select top (1)  @@IDENTITY as led_id from ledger order by led_id desc
		select SCOPE_IDENTITY() as led_id
		end
		end
    else
	begin
	     Update ledger set led_description=@led_description,led_status=@led_status,society_id=@society_id where led_id=@led_id
	End
     end
	 If @operation='Delete'
	Begin
	  
	 Update ledger set active_status=1 where led_id=@led_id
	  
	
	End

	 If @operation='Select'
	Begin
	  
	  Select * from ledger where led_id=@led_id

	End

     If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	     select * from ledger where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id  ORDER BY led_id DESC
	   End
	   If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	     select * from ledger where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id and( led_status like @search+'%'or cast(date as varchar) like @search+'%' )   ORDER BY led_id DESC
	   End

END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_loan]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:54:43 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_loan]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@loan_id int=0,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@bank nvarchar(50)=null,
@flat_id int=0,
@type_id int=0,
@society_noc date=null,
@loan_clearance date=null,
@cert_id int=0,
@noc_issued nvarchar(50)=null,
@search nvarchar(200)=null

AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @loan_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = loan_id
                    FROM     dbo.loan
                    ORDER BY loan_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		  
		    INSERT INTO loan(loan_id,bank,flat_id,cert_id,type_id,noc_issued,loan_clearance,society_noc,society_id,active_status)values(@new,@bank,@flat_id,@cert_id,@type_id,@noc_issued,DateAdd(Day, 15,@society_noc),@society_noc,@society_id,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update loan set bank=@bank,flat_id=@flat_id,cert_id=@cert_id,type_id=@type_id,noc_issued=@noc_issued,loan_clearance=@loan_clearance,society_noc=@society_noc,society_id=@society_id where loan_id=@loan_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from loan where loan_id=@loan_id
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update loan set active_status=0 where loan_id=@loan_id
    	End

	   If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	     select * from loan where society_id= @society_id and active_status=0 ORDER BY loan_id DESC
    	End
		 If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	     select * from loan where society_id= @society_id and active_status=0 and( bank like @search+'%' or cast(loan_clearance as varchar) like @search+'%' )ORDER BY loan_id DESC
    	End

		if @operation='flat_master'
		begin
		Select *  from flat_master where society_id= @society_id
		end

			if @operation='loan_type'
			begin
			Select *  from loan_type
			end

	         if @operation='certificate'
				begin
				Select *  from certificate
				end



END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_maintanance_cal]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:55:22 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_maintanance_cal]
@operation NVARCHAR(40) = NULL,
@maint_cal_id INT = 0,
@bill_no INT = 0,
@bill_id INT = 0,
@bill_type INT = 0,
@para NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@amount DECIMAL(18, 2) = 0,
@pending_amount DECIMAL(18, 2) = 0,
@date SMALLDATETIME = NULL,
@owner_id INT = 0,
@flat_id INT = 0,
@rate decimal(18,0)=null,
@parking decimal(18,0)=null,
@cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
@sql NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- 🟢 Show Maintenance Grid
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
    BEGIN
        SELECT DISTINCT 
            bill_id, 
            gen_date, 
            due_date,  
            society_id, 
            DATEPART(mm, gen_date) AS month, 
            DATENAME(mm, DATEADD(mm, DATEPART(mm, gen_date), -1)) AS month_name, 
            DATEPART(yyyy, gen_date) AS year,
            CASE WHEN generate_status = 1 THEN 'Bill Generated' END AS Status
        FROM maintenance_cal
        WHERE society_id = @society_id;
    END

    -- 🟢 Check Bill by Name/Owner
    IF @operation = 'check_name'
    BEGIN
        IF @owner_id = 0
            SELECT * FROM dbo.maintenance_cal WHERE bill_no = @bill_no;
        ELSE
            SELECT * FROM dbo.maintenance_cal WHERE bill_no = @bill_no;
    END

    -- 🟢 Select Detailed Bill View (merged with view_bill logic)
IF @operation = 'Select'
BEGIN
    DECLARE @flat_count INT = 1;

    IF @society_id IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SELECT @society_id = society_id 
        FROM flat_master 
        WHERE flat_id = @flat_id;
    END;

    -- 1️⃣ Flat count from audit mode
    SELECT @flat_count = COUNT(DISTINCT flat_id)
    FROM flat_master
    WHERE active_status = 0 
      AND society_id = @society_id;

    -- 2️⃣ Bill type
    SELECT @bill_type = bill_type 
    FROM maintenance_cal 
    WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;

    -- 3️⃣ Rate per sqft from audit table
	SELECT @rate = ISNULL(rate_per_sqfeet, 0)
FROM account_setting_audit
WHERE audit_id = (
    SELECT TOP 1 setting_charges_id 
    FROM bill_charges 
    WHERE bill_id = @bill_id
      AND charges_type = @bill_type
      AND society_id = @society_id
      AND setting_charges_id IS NOT NULL
      AND ISNUMERIC(setting_charges_id) = 1
    ORDER BY bill_charges_id DESC
);

    --SELECT @rate = ISNULL(rate_per_sqfeet,0)
    --FROM account_setting_audit
    --WHERE audit_id = (
    --    SELECT setting_charges_id 
    --    FROM bill_charges 
    --    WHERE bill_id = @bill_id
    --      AND charges_type = @bill_type
    --      AND society_id = @society_id
    --);

    --------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 4️⃣ BUILD DYNAMIC CHARGE COLUMNS USING CROSS APPLY (FIXED LOGIC)
    --------------------------------------------------------------------

    SELECT @cols = STRING_AGG(
        CONCAT(
            'MAX(CASE WHEN rn = ', rn, ' THEN charges END) AS col', rn + 2, '_name, ',
            'MAX(CASE WHEN rn = ', rn, ' THEN per_flat_amount END) AS col', rn + 2, '_amount'
        ), ', '
    )
    FROM (
        SELECT DISTINCT rn
        FROM (
            SELECT 
                ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY mca.society_id ORDER BY mca.charges) AS rn
            FROM maintenance_charges_audit mca
            INNER JOIN bill_charges bc 
                ON bc.bill_id = @bill_id AND bc.society_id = @society_id
            CROSS APPLY STRING_SPLIT(bc.bill_charges_id, ',') AS ss   -- ← FIXED
            WHERE 
                mca.audit_id = TRY_CAST(ss.value AS INT)              -- ← FIXED
                AND mca.status = 1
                AND mca.charges_type = @bill_type
                AND mca.society_id = @society_id
        ) AS a
    ) AS b;

    IF @cols IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@cols)) = ''
        SET @cols = 'NULL AS col3_name, NULL AS col3_amount';

    --------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 5️⃣ MAIN SQL (AUDIT MODE)
    --------------------------------------------------------------------
    SET @sql = N'
    SELECT 
        DATEPART(mm, mc.m_date) AS month,
        DATENAME(mm, DATEADD(mm, DATEPART(mm, mc.m_date), -1)) AS month_name,
        DATEPART(yyyy, mc.m_date) AS year,
        ow.name AS owner_name,
        ow.flat_no,
        ow.w_name,
        mc.bill_no,
        mc.total_amount,
        ow.flat_id,
        rec.receivable_amt,
        ow.sq_ft,
        -- due, not total_amount: sp_new_maintenance folds arrears into
        -- total_amount, so summing that counted the dues twice — flat 102's
        -- March bill read 6,746.13 where 1,765.38 was owed. due holds only
        -- what that bill is short, and settlement drives it to zero, which is
        -- also why the bill_status <> 'Paid' join is no longer needed.
        --
        -- society_id belongs here too: flat_id is not unique across societies.
		        ISNULL((
            SELECT SUM(m2.due)
            FROM maintenance_cal m2
            WHERE m2.flat_id = mc.flat_id
              AND m2.society_id = mc.society_id
              AND m2.gen_date < mc.gen_date
        ), 0) AS amt_forward,

        --mc.amt_forward,
        mc.tax_interest_amt,
        ow.Unit,
        ow.type,
        ow.pre_mob,
        ow.bed,
        ow.email,
        mc.advance,
        t.terms,
      mc.due,
        bs.bill_status,
        ow.build_id,
        ow.owner_id,
        mc.gen_date,
        mc.due_date,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN ''Regular'' ELSE ''Add-on'' END AS bill_type,


		  

		 CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 and parking.parking_amount is not null   THEN ''Parking Charges'' END AS col2_name,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN parking.parking_amount END AS col2_amount,
       
        se.*,

        ow.build_name,
        ow.society_name,
        bm.address1,
        bm.address2,
        bm.print_name,
        bm.registration_no,
        mc.bill_id
		FROM maintenance_cal mc
INNER JOIN bill_status bs ON mc.status = bs.status_id
INNER JOIN owner_search_vw ow ON mc.flat_id = ow.flat_id

    --FROM maintenance_cal mc
    --INNER JOIN owner_search_vw ow ON mc.flat_id = ow.flat_id
    INNER JOIN building_master bm ON ow.build_id = bm.build_id
    left JOIN terms t ON ow.society_id = t.society_id
	left join (SELECT 
  v.flat_id, 
   ISNULL(SUM(
        CASE 
            WHEN v.vehicle_type = 0 THEN a.two_wheeler_rate
            WHEN v.vehicle_type = 1 THEN a.four_wheeler_rate
            ELSE 0
        END
    ), 0) as parking_amount
FROM dbo.Vehicle AS v
INNER JOIN dbo.account_setting_audit AS a 
    ON v.society_id = a.society_id
INNER JOIN dbo.bill_charges AS bc
    ON a.audit_id = TRY_CAST(bc.setting_charges_id AS INT)
WHERE 
    v.society_id = ' + QUOTENAME(@society_id, '''') + '
    AND ISNULL(v.park_place_id, 0) != 0 group by v.flat_id) as parking on parking.flat_id=mc.flat_id 

    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT flat_id, SUM(ISNULL(total_amount,0)) AS receivable_amt
        FROM maintenance_cal
        WHERE m_date < DATEADD(month, DATEDIFF(month, 0, GETDATE()), 0)
        GROUP BY flat_id
    ) rec ON rec.flat_id = ow.flat_id

    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT society_id, ' + @cols + '
        FROM (
            SELECT 
                mca.society_id,
                mca.charges,
                CAST(mca.amount / NULLIF(' + CAST(@flat_count AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ', 0) AS DECIMAL(10,2)) AS per_flat_amount,
                ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY mca.society_id ORDER BY mca.charges) AS rn
            FROM maintenance_charges_audit mca
            left JOIN bill_charges bc 
                ON bc.bill_id = '+CAST(@bill_id AS NVARCHAR(10))+' 
                AND bc.society_id = '''+@society_id+'''
            CROSS APPLY STRING_SPLIT(bc.bill_charges_id, '','') ss
            WHERE 
                mca.audit_id = TRY_CAST(ss.value AS INT)
                AND mca.status = 1
                AND mca.charges_type = '+CAST(@bill_type AS NVARCHAR(10))+'
                AND mca.society_id = '''+@society_id+'''
        ) x
        GROUP BY society_id
    ) se ON se.society_id = ow.society_id

    WHERE ow.type = ''Owner''
      AND mc.bill_id = '+CAST(@bill_id AS NVARCHAR(10))+'
      AND (
          ('+CAST(@flat_id AS NVARCHAR(10))+' = 0 AND mc.society_id = '''+ISNULL(@society_id,'')+''')
          OR ('+CAST(@flat_id AS NVARCHAR(10))+' <> 0 AND mc.flat_id = '+CAST(@flat_id AS NVARCHAR(10))+')
      );';

    EXEC sp_executesql @sql;
	end

END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_maintenance_charges]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:55:50 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_maintenance_charges]
    @operation NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @user_id INT = 0,
    @charges NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@charges_type bit = 1,
    @amount DECIMAL(18,2) = 0,
    @status BIT = 1,
	@charge_id int = NULL,
    @date DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @operation = 'insert'
		BEGIN
			IF @charge_id = 0
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO maintenance_charges (
					society_id,
					user_id,
					charges,
					amount,
					status,
					charges_type,
					created_at
				)
				VALUES (
					@society_id,
					@user_id,
					@charges,
					@amount,
					@status,
					@charges_type,
					GETDATE()
				);
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				UPDATE maintenance_charges
				SET 
					society_id = @society_id,
					user_id = @user_id,
					charges = @charges,
					amount = @amount,
					status = @status,
					charges_type = @charges_type
					
				WHERE charge_id = @charge_id;
			END
		END


    ELSE IF @operation = 'grid_show'
    BEGIN
				SELECT 
					charge_id,
					society_id,
					user_id,
					charges AS NatureOfCharge,
					amount,
					charges_type,
					status,
					CONVERT(VARCHAR(11), created_at, 106) AS [Date]
				FROM maintenance_charges
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
        ORDER BY charge_id DESC;
    END

	ELSE IF	@operation = 'select'
		BEGIN
			select * from maintenance_charges where society_id = @society_id AND charge_id = @charge_id;
		END

	ELSE IF @operation='send_notification'
	BEGIN

	SELECT        dbo.owner_master.owner_id, dbo.maintenance_cal.total_amount, dbo.maintenance_cal.due_date, DATEDIFF(day, GETDATE(), dbo.maintenance_cal.due_date) AS remaining_days, dbo.owner_master.token, 
                         dbo.maintenance_cal.society_id
    FROM            dbo.owner_master INNER JOIN
                         dbo.maintenance_cal ON dbo.owner_master.flat_id = dbo.maintenance_cal.flat_id
    WHERE        (dbo.maintenance_cal.status <> 3) AND (DATEDIFF(day, GETDATE(), dbo.maintenance_cal.due_date) <= 3) AND (DATEDIFF(day, GETDATE(), dbo.maintenance_cal.due_date) > 0)
  
  END
END


GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_MaintenanceReceipt]
    @Action NVARCHAR(20),                   -- 'INSERT', 'UPDATE', 'CANCEL', 'GET'
    @ReceiptID INT = NULL,

    @FlatID INT = NULL,
	@owner_id int =0,
    @PayMode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @ChequeNo NVARCHAR(30) = NULL,
    @ChequeDate DATE = NULL,
    @BankName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @TransactionRef NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @PaidAmount DECIMAL(10,2) = NULL,
    @Remarks NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Status INT = 1,                        -- 1 = Paid, 2 = Cancelled, 3 = Refunded
    @CreatedBy NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @MaintenanceType NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
	@bills NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,-- 'Regular', 'AddOn', 'Settlement'
    @ReceiptDate SMALLDATETIME = NULL,
	@start_date date = NULL,
	@end_date date = NULL

AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

  --  IF @Action = 'INSERT'
  --  BEGIN
  --      DECLARE @NewReceiptNo NVARCHAR(50);
  --      DECLARE @NextNum INT;

  --      -- Generate new sequential receipt number per society
  --      SELECT @NextNum = ISNULL(MAX(receipt_id), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.receipt;

  --      SET @NewReceiptNo = 'RCPT-' + CAST(YEAR(GETDATE()) AS NVARCHAR(4)) + '-' + RIGHT('0000' + CAST(@NextNum AS NVARCHAR(4)), 4);

  --      IF @ReceiptDate IS NULL
  --          SET @ReceiptDate = GETDATE();

  --      INSERT INTO dbo.receipt (
  --          receipt_id, society_id, flat_id, receipt_no, receipt_date,
  --          pay_mode, cheque_no, cheque_date, bank_name, transaction_ref,
  --         bill_details, paid_amount, remarks, status, created_by
  --      )
  --      VALUES (
  --          @NextNum, @SocietyID, @FlatID, @NewReceiptNo, @ReceiptDate,
  --          @PayMode, @ChequeNo, @ChequeDate, @BankName, @TransactionRef,
  --         @bills, @PaidAmount, @Remarks, 3 , @CreatedBy
  --      );
		--UPDATE notify_status set seen_status=1 where user_id=@CreatedBY
  --    exec sp_SettleMaintenancePayment @ReceiptID=@NextNum
	 --  SELECT 
  --      @NextNum AS receipt_id
  --  END

  --  ELSE IF @Action = 'UPDATE'
  --  BEGIN
  --      UPDATE dbo.receipt
  --      SET 
  --          pay_mode = @PayMode,
  --          cheque_no = @ChequeNo,
  --          cheque_date = @ChequeDate,
  --          bank_name = @BankName,
  --          transaction_ref = @TransactionRef,
  --          paid_amount = @PaidAmount,
  --          remarks = @Remarks,
  --          status = @Status
  --      WHERE receipt_id = @ReceiptID;

  --      PRINT 'Receipt updated successfully.';
  --  END

  --  ELSE IF @Action = 'CANCEL'
  --  BEGIN
  --      UPDATE dbo.receipt
  --      SET status = 2, remarks = ISNULL(@Remarks, 'Cancelled'), cheque_no = NULL
  --      WHERE receipt_id = @ReceiptID;

  --      PRINT 'Receipt cancelled successfully.';
  --  END

  --  ELSE IF @Action = Upper('GETRECEIPT')
  --  BEGIN
  --      SELECT 
  --          *
  --      FROM receipt_search_vw
  --      WHERE receipt_id = @ReceiptID
  --      ORDER BY date DESC;
  --  END
    IF @Action = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @NewReceiptNo NVARCHAR(50);
        DECLARE @NextNum INT;

        -- Generate new sequential receipt number per society
        SELECT @NextNum = ISNULL(MAX(receipt_id), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.receipt;

        SET @NewReceiptNo = 'RCPT-' + CAST(YEAR(GETDATE()) AS NVARCHAR(4)) + '-' + RIGHT('0000' + CAST(@NextNum AS NVARCHAR(4)), 4);

        IF @ReceiptDate IS NULL
            SET @ReceiptDate = GETDATE();

        -- Insert receipt
        INSERT INTO dbo.receipt (
            receipt_id, society_id, flat_id, receipt_no, receipt_date,
            pay_mode, cheque_no, cheque_date, bank_name, transaction_ref,
            bill_details, paid_amount, remarks, status, created_by
        )
        VALUES (
            @NextNum, @SocietyID, @FlatID, @NewReceiptNo, @ReceiptDate,
            @PayMode, @ChequeNo, @ChequeDate, @BankName, @TransactionRef,
            @bills, @PaidAmount, @Remarks, 3, @CreatedBy
        );

        -- Update notify_status (no SELECT)
        UPDATE notify_status 
        SET seen_status = 1 
        WHERE user_id = @CreatedBy;

        -- Settle maintenance (ensure no SELECT inside sp_SettleMaintenancePayment)
        EXEC sp_SettleMaintenancePayment @ReceiptID = @NextNum;

        -- Return receipt_id as the only result set
        SELECT @NextNum AS receipt_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @Action = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.receipt
        SET 
            pay_mode = @PayMode,
            cheque_no = @ChequeNo,
            cheque_date = @ChequeDate,
            bank_name = @BankName,
            transaction_ref = @TransactionRef,
            paid_amount = @PaidAmount,
            remarks = @Remarks,
            status = @Status
        WHERE receipt_id = @ReceiptID;
    END

    ELSE IF @Action = 'CANCEL'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.receipt
        SET status = 2, remarks = ISNULL(@Remarks, 'Cancelled'), cheque_no = NULL
        WHERE receipt_id = @ReceiptID;
    END

    ELSE IF @Action = 'GETRECEIPT'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM receipt_search_vw
        WHERE receipt_id = @ReceiptID
        ORDER BY date DESC;
    END

	IF @Action = 'ResidentFill'
    BEGIN
       
                  SELECT flat_id, Name + ' ( ' + Unit+')' as resident_name 
                                   FROM owner_search_vw where type='Owner' and society_id=@SocietyID 
                                   ORDER BY Name
    END
		IF @Action = 'GetBills'
    BEGIN
       
SELECT 
    bill_id AS BillId,
    bill_type AS BillType,

    due +tax_interest_amt AS Amount,
    due_date AS DueDate,
    CASE 
        WHEN GETDATE() > due_date THEN 'Overdue'
		When total_amount=due then 'Pending'
        ELSE 'Partially Paid'
    END AS Status,
    -- Custom bill format: INV-<YEAR>-<3-digit bill_no>
    'MBL-' + CAST(YEAR(gen_date) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + 
    RIGHT('000' + CAST(bill_no AS VARCHAR(3)), 3) AS BillNo,
	bill_no
FROM maintenance_cal
 WHERE due>0 and flat_id = @FlatId 
 
 ORDER BY due_date
    END
		IF @Action = 'GetPDCCheque'
    BEGIN
       select @owner_id=owner_id from owner_master where  type='Owner' and flat_id=@FlatId and society_id=@SocietyID
                 SELECT  pdc_rem_id, chqno,  che_date, bank_name, che_amount FROM pdc_reminder WHERE owner_id =@owner_id ORDER BY che_date
 
    END
	IF @Action = 'Grid_Show'
    BEGIN
     select receipt_id,receipt_no, receipt_date, paid_amount, bill_status, ISNULL(transaction_ref,cheque_no) as transaction_ref, name +' ('+unit+')' as owner from receipt as r inner join owner_search_vw as ow on ow.flat_id=r.flat_id 
	 inner join bill_status as bs on bs.status_id=r.status  where ow.type = 'owner' AND r.society_id=@SocietyID order by receipt_date desc

    END


	IF @Action = 'GetAgm'
		BEGIN
			SELECT Sum(total)as total, charges, null as gen_date1
			FROM agm_bill_cal
			WHERE gen_date BETWEEN @start_date AND @end_date AND society_id = @SocietyID group  by charges  ;
		END

END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_ManageOtherCredits]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:56:41 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

-- =============================================
-- Single Stored Procedure for All Other Credits Operations
-- =============================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ManageOtherCredits]
    @Operation NVARCHAR(20),           -- 'SELECT', 'INSERT', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'GETBYID', 'TOTAL', 'DATERANGE'
    @Id INT = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Amount DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL,
    @SearchText NVARCHAR(255) = '',
    @StartDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @EndDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@society_id nvarchar(20) = NULL,
	@payment_date DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    -- SELECT Operation (Get all with search)
    IF @Operation = 'SELECT'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            Id,
            Description,
            Amount,
            PaymentDate,
            CreatedDate,
            ModifiedDate,
			society_id
        FROM OtherCredits
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
        ORDER BY Id DESC;
    END
    
    -- GETBYID Operation (Get single record by ID)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'GETBYID'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            Id,
            Description,
            Amount,
            PaymentDate,
            CreatedDate,
            ModifiedDate
        FROM OtherCredits
        WHERE Id = @Id AND society_id = @society_id;
    END
    
    -- INSERT Operation (Add new credit)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        BEGIN TRY
            IF @Description IS NULL OR @Description = ''
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Description is required' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            IF @Amount IS NULL OR @Amount <= 0
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Valid amount is required' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            INSERT INTO OtherCredits (Description, Amount, PaymentDate, CreatedDate, society_id)
            VALUES (@Description, @Amount, @payment_date, GETDATE(), @society_id);
            
            SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS Result, 'Success' AS Status, 'Credit added successfully' AS Message;
        END TRY
        BEGIN CATCH
            SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, ERROR_MESSAGE() AS Message;
        END CATCH
    END
    
    -- UPDATE Operation (Update existing credit)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        BEGIN TRY
            IF @Id IS NULL OR @Id <= 0
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Invalid ID' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM OtherCredits WHERE Id = @Id)
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Credit not found' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            IF @Description IS NULL OR @Description = ''
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Description is required' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            IF @Amount IS NULL OR @Amount <= 0
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Valid amount is required' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            UPDATE OtherCredits
            SET 
                Description = @Description,
                Amount = @Amount,
				PaymentDate = @payment_date,
                ModifiedDate = GETDATE()
            WHERE Id = @Id;
            
            SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS Result, 'Success' AS Status, 'Credit updated successfully' AS Message;
        END TRY
        BEGIN CATCH
            SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, ERROR_MESSAGE() AS Message;
        END CATCH
    END
    
    -- DELETE Operation (Delete credit)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        BEGIN TRY
            IF @Id IS NULL OR @Id <= 0
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Invalid ID' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM OtherCredits WHERE Id = @Id)
            BEGIN
                SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Credit not found' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
            
            DELETE FROM OtherCredits
            WHERE Id = @Id;
            
            SELECT @@ROWCOUNT AS Result, 'Success' AS Status, 'Credit deleted successfully' AS Message;
        END TRY
        BEGIN CATCH
            SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, ERROR_MESSAGE() AS Message;
        END CATCH
    END
    
    -- TOTAL Operation (Get summary statistics)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'TOTAL'
    BEGIN
        IF @StartDate IS NULL
            SET @StartDate = '1900-01-01';
        
        IF @EndDate IS NULL
            SET @EndDate = GETDATE();
        
        SELECT 
            COUNT(*) AS TotalRecords,
            ISNULL(SUM(Amount), 0) AS TotalAmount,
            ISNULL(AVG(Amount), 0) AS AverageAmount,
            ISNULL(MIN(Amount), 0) AS MinAmount,
            ISNULL(MAX(Amount), 0) AS MaxAmount
        FROM OtherCredits
        WHERE PaymentDate BETWEEN @StartDate AND @EndDate AND society_id = @society_id;
    END
    
    -- DATERANGE Operation (Get credits by date range)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'DATERANGE'
    BEGIN
        IF @StartDate IS NULL
            SET @StartDate = '1900-01-01';
        
        IF @EndDate IS NULL
            SET @EndDate = GETDATE();
        
        SELECT 
            Id,
            Description,
            Amount,
            PaymentDate,
            CreatedDate,
            ModifiedDate
        FROM OtherCredits
        WHERE PaymentDate BETWEEN @StartDate AND @EndDate AND society_id = @society_id
        ORDER BY PaymentDate DESC, Id DESC;
    END
    
    -- Invalid Operation
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT 0 AS Result, 'Error' AS Status, 'Invalid operation specified' AS Message;
    END
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_meeting_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:57:02 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_meeting_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, @meet_id INT=0, @meeting_date Date=NULL,@search nvarchar(200)=null, @meeting_time DATETIME=NULL, @subject NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, @meet_ex_id INT=0, @details NVARCHAR (1000)=NULL, @para NVARCHAR (100)=NULL, @society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @meet_id = 0
                BEGIN
                    DECLARE @new AS INT;
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = meet_id
                    FROM     dbo.meeting_master
                    ORDER BY meet_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                       OR @new = 0
                        SELECT @new = 1;
                    ELSE
                        SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.meeting_master (meet_id, subject, details, meeting_date, meeting_time, society_id,active_status)
                    VALUES                         (@new, dbo.InitCap(@subject), dbo.InitCap(@details), @meeting_date, @meeting_time, @society_id,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.meeting_master
                    SET    subject      = dbo.InitCap(@subject),
                           details      = dbo.InitCap(@details),
                           meeting_date = @meeting_date,
                           meeting_time = @meeting_time,
                           society_id   = @society_id
                    WHERE  meet_id = @meet_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT meeting_date,
                   meeting_time,
                   subject,
                   details,
                   society_id
            FROM   meeting_master
            WHERE  meet_id = @meet_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select_All'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   meeting_master where active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.meeting_master set active_status=1 
            WHERE  meet_id = @meet_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'subject_already_exist'
        BEGIN
            IF @meet_id <> 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.meeting_master
                WHERE  subject = @subject
                       AND meet_id <> @meet_id and active_status=0;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.meeting_master
                WHERE  subject = @subject and active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'get_meeting'
        BEGIN
            SELECT meet_id,
                   subject,
                   meeting_date,
                   society_id
            FROM   dbo.meeting_master
            WHERE  (meeting_date = @meeting_time);
        END
    IF @operation = 'add_row'
        BEGIN
            INSERT  INTO meeting_extension (meet_id, details)
            VALUES                        (@meet_id, '');
        END
    IF @operation = 'grid_fill'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   meeting_extension
            WHERE meet_ex_id = @meet_ex_id and active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'grid_save'
        BEGIN
            UPDATE meeting_extension
            SET    meet_id    = @meet_id,
                   details    = @details,
                   society_id = @society_id
            WHERE  meet_ex_id = @meet_ex_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'D_Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update meeting_extension set active_status=1
            WHERE  meet_ex_id = @meet_ex_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'GV_SELECT'
        BEGIN
            IF @para IS NULL
                BEGIN
                    SELECT *
                    FROM   dbo.meeting_master where active_status=0;
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    SELECT CONCAT(' * from dbo.meeting_master', @para);
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_date'
        BEGIN
            IF @meet_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.meeting_master
                WHERE  active_status=0 and meeting_date = @meeting_date;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.meeting_master
                WHERE  meeting_date = @meeting_date and active_status=0
                       AND meet_id = @meet_id
                       AND meet_id <> @meet_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     meeting_master
            WHERE   active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY meet_id DESC;
        END
		 IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     meeting_master
            WHERE   active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and( subject like  @search+'%' or cast (meeting_date as varchar) like  @search+'%')
            ORDER BY meet_id DESC;
        END
END

GO
