/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_new_maintenance]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:57:21 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

		CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_new_maintenance] 
		@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
		@bill_id int=0,
		@wing_id int=0,
		@build_id int=0,
		@regular decimal=0,
		@additional decimal=0,
		@m_date smalldatetime= NULL,
		@status  int=0,
		@m_total decimal=0,
		@flat_id int=0,
		@search nvarchar(50)=null,
		 @startDate smalldatetime=NULL,
		 @endDate smalldatetime=NULL,
		 @bill_type int=0,
		 @owner_id int=0,
		 @due_period int =0,
		 @society_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
		 @interest decimal(18,0)=0
		AS
		BEGIN

		  If @operation='bill_exist'
			   Begin
				 Select * from maintenance_cal Where bill_id = @bill_id
			  End

			 If @operation='check_delete'
			  Begin
			 select * from maintenance_cal Where bill_id = @bill_id
			  End

			  IF @operation='owner_details'
			  Begin
			  SELECT * FROM dbo.owner_master WHERE  (type = 'Owner') AND wing_id=@wing_id
			  End

			  if @operation='owner_select'
			  Begin
			  SELECT distinct owner_id, name as owner_name  FROM dbo.owner_search_vw where build_id=@build_id
			  End
			  If @operation='Select_owner_email'
			  Begin
			  SELECT  email,name FROM dbo.owner_master WHERE owner_id=@owner_id
			  End

			IF @operation='GetBills'
			Begin
			Select * from maintenance_cal  where  bill_id = @bill_id and society_id= @society_id
			End
			If @operation='generate'
			Begin 
			 select   @bill_id= ISNULL(MAX(bill_id), 0) + 1  from maintenance_cal 
					DECLARE @flat_count INT,
					@bill_charges_ids nvarchar(100)=null,
				@setting_charges_ids nvarchar(100)=null;
					SELECT @flat_count = COUNT(DISTINCT flat_id)  FROM flat_master   WHERE active_status=0  and society_id = @society_id;

					DECLARE flat_cursor CURSOR FOR
					SELECT flat_id   FROM owner_search_vw    WHERE society_id = @society_id       AND type = 'Owner';
					SELECT @bill_charges_ids=STRING_AGG(max_audit_id, ',')
		FROM (
			-- Joined to the live table. status = 1 on an audit row only means
			-- the head was on at some point — every head has such a row — so
			-- filtering the audit listed charges retired months ago. Bill 42
			-- printed nine heads worth 18,290.58 against 846.15 actually due.
			SELECT MAX(a.audit_id) AS max_audit_id
			FROM maintenance_charges_audit a
			INNER JOIN maintenance_charges c
			        ON c.charge_id = a.charge_id AND c.society_id = a.society_id
			WHERE a.charges_type = 0 AND a.society_id = @society_id
			  AND c.status = 1 AND c.charges_type = 0
			GROUP BY a.charge_id, a.society_id
		) AS sub;

			SELECT @setting_charges_ids= MAX(audit_id) FROM account_setting_audit  WHERE society_id =@society_id  GROUP BY acc_set_id, society_id
					OPEN flat_cursor;
					FETCH NEXT FROM flat_cursor INTO @flat_id
					WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
					BEGIN
						DECLARE 
							@total_amt DECIMAL(10,2) = 0,
							@amt_forward DECIMAL(10,2) = 0,
							@tax_interest DECIMAL(10,2) = 0,
							@prev_due DECIMAL(10,2) = 0,
							@due Decimal(10,2)=0,

							--@due Decimal(18,0)=0,
							@prev_due_date DATE = NULL;

					  SELECT   @total_amt = ISNULL(SUM(amount / NULLIF(@flat_count, 0)), 0)   FROM maintenance_charges
				WHERE  status =1 and charges_type =0 and society_id = @society_id 
						   set @due=@total_amt;
						-- society_id belongs here: flat_id is not unique across societies,
						-- so without it another society's arrears land on this bill. Same
						-- fix as gen_bill.
						-- No bill_type filter: this counted only unpaid add-on runs and
						-- ignored the regular ones, so an add-on bill showed 769.23 where
						-- 5,749.98 was actually owed. gen_bill has no such filter, and a
						-- resident's arrears are their arrears whichever run raised them.
						SELECT   @prev_due = ISNULL(Sum(due), 0) FROM maintenance_cal WHERE flat_id = @flat_id and society_id = @society_id and due_date <  DATEADD(day,-1, GETDATE())

						-- '> 0', not 'IS NOT NULL': the ISNULL above means @prev_due is
						-- never NULL, so this branch always ran and the else never did.
						IF (@prev_due > 0)
						BEGIN
							SET @amt_forward = @prev_due;
							SET @tax_interest = ROUND((@prev_due * @interest)/100, 2);
							SET @total_amt = @total_amt + @amt_forward + @tax_interest;
						END
						else
						set @interest=0
						INSERT INTO maintenance_cal
						(
							maintenance_id, bill_id, bill_no, gen_date, due_date, 
							total_amount, due, flat_id, m_date, bill_type, society_id,
							amt_forward,interest_forward, tax_interest_amt,advance,generate_status,status
						)
						VALUES
						(
							(SELECT ISNULL(MAX(maintenance_id), 0) + 1 FROM maintenance_cal),
							@bill_id,
							(SELECT ISNULL(MAX(bill_no), 0) + 1 FROM maintenance_cal),
							CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE),
							DATEADD(Month, @due_period, GETDATE()),
							@total_amt,
							@due,
							@flat_id,
							GETDATE(),
							0,
							@society_id,
							@amt_forward,
							@interest,
							@tax_interest,0,1,1
						);
				
						FETCH NEXT FROM flat_cursor INTO @flat_id;
					END

					CLOSE flat_cursor;
					DEALLOCATE flat_cursor; 
					IF @bill_charges_ids IS NULL
BEGIN
    SELECT @bill_charges_ids = STRING_AGG(max_audit_id, ',')
    FROM (
        -- Same join as the block above; this fallback runs when that one
        -- found nothing, and would otherwise reintroduce the retired heads.
        SELECT MAX(a.audit_id) AS max_audit_id
        FROM maintenance_charges_audit a
        INNER JOIN maintenance_charges c
                ON c.charge_id = a.charge_id AND c.society_id = a.society_id
        WHERE a.charges_type = 0 AND a.society_id = @society_id
          AND c.status = 1 AND c.charges_type = 0
        GROUP BY a.charge_id, a.society_id
    ) AS sub;
END
insert into bill_charges values (@bill_charges_ids,@setting_charges_ids,@society_id,@bill_type,@bill_id)


					UPDATE maintenance_charges SET status = 0 WHERE charge_id IN (SELECT MAX(charge_id) AS max_audit_id
    FROM maintenance_charges
    WHERE charges_type = 0 
      AND status = 1 
      AND society_id = @society_id
    GROUP BY charge_id, society_id
);
 select   @bill_id= ISNULL(MAX(bill_id), 0)   from maintenance_cal;
	select bill_id as maintenance_id,token,type,owner_id  from maintenance_cal m inner join userdata o on  m.flat_id=o.flat_id
	where bill_id=@bill_id and token is not null and type='Owner' and active_status=0 order by o.flat_id ;
					
				END

				 IF @operation = 'TotalDue'
			BEGIN
				SELECT CONVERT(decimal(10, 2), ISNULL(SUM(due)+Sum(tax_interest_amt ), 0)) AS balance
				FROM maintenance_cal
				WHERE due > 0 
				  AND flat_id = @flat_id;
			END

		ELSE IF @operation = 'DueHistory'
BEGIN
    WITH ReceiptData AS (
        SELECT DISTINCT 
            0 AS bill_id,
            0 AS bill_no,
            flat_id,
            receipt_id,
            date,
            pay_mode AS due_date,
            NULL AS bill_type,
            0 AS due,
            paid_amount AS total_amount,
            -- A receipt is a payment, so it always reads as Paid. The owner
            -- app splits its tabs on this value.
            'Paid' AS type,
            'Receipt' AS pay_mode_name,
            NULL AS seen_status,
            NULL AS notify_status_id,
            0 AS tax_interest_amt
        -- dbo.receipt, not receipt_search_vw: that view INNER JOINs
        -- maintenance_cal, so a receipt settling a bill absent from that table
        -- never appeared. Flat 7 showed 5 of its 14 receipts — the resident's
        -- own app was missing 1,076 they had paid. status 3 is Paid; 2 is a
        -- cancelled receipt, which should stay out.
        FROM receipt
        WHERE flat_id = @flat_id AND status = 3
    ),
    InvoiceData AS (
        SELECT 
            m.bill_id,
            m.bill_no,
            m.flat_id,
            0 AS receipt_id,
            m.gen_date AS date,
            CAST(m.due_date AS VARCHAR) AS due_date,
            m.bill_type,
            CASE 
                WHEN bs.bill_status = 'Paid' THEN 0
                ELSE ISNULL(SUM(m.due) + SUM(tax_interest_amt), 0)
            END AS due,
            ISNULL(SUM(m.total_amount), 0) AS total_amount,
            bs.bill_status AS type,
            'Invoice' AS pay_mode_name,
            ISNULL(ns.seen_status, 1) AS seen_status,
            ns.notify_status_id,
            ISNULL(SUM(tax_interest_amt), 0) AS tax_interest_amt
        FROM maintenance_cal AS m
        INNER JOIN owner_master AS o ON o.flat_id = m.flat_id
        INNER JOIN bill_status AS bs ON m.status = bs.status_id

        OUTER APPLY (
            SELECT TOP 1 
                seen_status,
                notify_status_id
            FROM notify_status
            WHERE notification_id = m.bill_id
              AND user_id = o.owner_id
              AND notification_type = 'maintenance'
        ) ns

        WHERE o.[type] = 'Owner'
          AND m.flat_id = @flat_id
        GROUP BY 
            m.bill_id, m.bill_no, m.gen_date, m.flat_id,
            m.bill_type, bs.bill_status, m.due_date,
            ns.seen_status, ns.notify_status_id
    )

    SELECT *
    FROM (
        SELECT * FROM ReceiptData
        UNION ALL
        SELECT * FROM InvoiceData
    ) AS Combined
    ORDER BY [date] DESC;
END

		END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_notice_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:57:43 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_notice_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL,
@notice_id INT=0, 
@date date=NULL, 
@name NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@description NVARCHAR(500)=NULL, 
@valid_to SMALLDATETIME=NULL, 
@para NVARCHAR (100)=NULL, 
@recipients_id int=0,
@society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, 
@status INT=0,
@search nvarchar(200)=null
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @notice_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = notice_id
                    FROM     dbo.notice_master
                    ORDER BY notice_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.notice_master (notice_id,name, description, date, valid_to, society_id, active_status,recipients_id)
                    VALUES                        (@new,dbo.InitCap(@name), @description, getdate(), @valid_to, @society_id, 0,@recipients_id);
                   select @new as notice_id
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.notice_master
                    SET    name        = dbo.InitCap(@name),
                           description = dbo.InitCap(@description),
                           date        = @date,
                           valid_to    = @valid_to,
						   recipients_id = @recipients_id,
                           society_id  = @society_id
                    WHERE  notice_id = @notice_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   notice_master
            WHERE  notice_id = @notice_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select_All'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   notice_master where active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update  dbo.notice_master set active_status=1
            WHERE  notice_id = @notice_id;
           
        END
    IF @operation = 'GV_SELECT'
        BEGIN
            IF @para IS NULL
                BEGIN
                    SELECT *
                    FROM   dbo.notice_master where active_status=0;
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    SELECT CONCAT(' * from dbo.notice_master', @para);
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_date'
        BEGIN
            IF @notice_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.notice_master
                WHERE  date = @date and active_status=0;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.notice_master
                WHERE  date = @date and active_status=0
                       AND notice_id = @notice_id
                       AND notice_id <> @notice_id;
        END

		 if @operation='owner_select'
	  Begin
	  SELECT distinct owner_id, name  FROM dbo.owner_search_vw where society_id=@society_id
	  End

	   IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     notice_master
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY notice_id;
        END 
   IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     notice_master
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and( name like @search+'%'   or cast(date as varchar)  like  @search+'%')
            ORDER BY notice_id DESC;
        END

		IF @operation = 'fill_list'
		begin

		Select * from notice_recipients
		end

		 IF @operation = 'GetAllRecipients'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM notice_recipients; -- optional: if you have an active_status field
    END

END

GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_notification]
  @operation nvarchar(50)=NULL,
  @notification_id int=0,
  @user_id int=0,
  @id int=0,
  @user_type nvarchar(50)=NULL,
  @notification_type nvarchar(50)=null,
  @seen_status int=0,
  @society_id nvarchar(10)=NULL,
  @title nvarchar(50)=null,
  @body nvarchar(500)=NULL,
  @notify_status_id int=0,
  @timestamp smalldatetime=null,
  @owner_id int=0
 -- @List Cursor
  AS 
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;

  If @operation='Update'
     Begin
       If @notify_status_id=0
         begin
	 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = notify_status_id
                    FROM     dbo.notify_status
                    ORDER BY notify_status_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
           Insert Into dbo.notify_status(notify_status_id,notification_id,notification_type,user_id,user_type,seen_status,society_id,title,body,timestamp)
		   Values(@new,@notification_id,@notification_type,@user_id,@user_type,@seen_status,@society_id,@title,@body,getdate())
         end
       Else
         begin
           Update dbo.notify_status Set notification_id=@notification_id,notification_type=@notification_type,user_id=@user_id,user_type=@user_type,seen_status=@seen_status,society_id=@society_id,title=@title,body=@body where notify_status_id=@notify_status_id
         end
     End

	 if @operation ='Buildingwisenotification'
	 Begin
	 select * from dbo.notify_status where notify_status_id=@notify_status_id
	 end

  If @operation='Select'
     Begin
       SELECT * FROM dbo.notify_status where notify_status_id=@notify_status_id
     End
 

  If @operation='Select_ALL'
     Begin
       SELECT * FROM dbo.notify_status 
     End

	    -- Notice
    IF @operation = 'Notice'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            name,
            description,
            date AS from_date,
            valid_to AS to_date
        FROM notice_master
        WHERE active_status = 0 
          AND society_id = @society_id
          AND notice_id = @id;
    END

    -- Event
    ELSE IF @operation = 'Event'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            event_name AS name,
            description,
            from_date,
            to_date
        FROM event_master
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND society_id = @society_id
          AND event_id = @id;
    END
	  IF @operation = 'Count'
    BEGIN
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS notification_count
        FROM notify_status
        WHERE seen_status = 0
          AND society_id = @society_id
          AND user_id = @user_id;
    END
    -- Visitor
    ELSE IF @operation = 'Visitor'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM visitor
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND society_id = @society_id
          AND visitor_id = @id;
    END

    -- Maintenance
    --ELSE IF @operation = 'Maintenance'
    --BEGIN
    --    SELECT *
    --    FROM maintainance
    --    WHERE active_status = 0
    --      AND society_id = @society_id
    --      AND n_m_id = @id;
    --END
  If @operation='Delete'
     Begin
       DELETE FROM dbo.notify_status where notify_status_id=@notify_status_id
     End


  If @operation='check_delete'
     Begin
       SELECT * FROM dbo.notify_status where notify_status_id=@notify_status_id
     End

	  --IF @operation = 'GetNotifications'
   -- BEGIN
   --     SELECT 
   --         notify_status_id,
   --         notification_id,
   --         notification_type,
   --         user_id,
   --         user_type,
   --         seen_status,
   --         society_id,
   --         title,
   --         body,
   --         dbo.GetRelativeTime(timestamp) AS timestamp
   --     FROM notify_status
   --     WHERE notification_id!=0 and society_id = @society_id
   --       AND user_id = @owner_id and seen_status=0
   --     ORDER BY notify_status_id DESC;
   -- END
	
 IF @operation = 'GetNotifications'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            notify_status_id,
            notification_id,
            notification_type,
            user_id,
            user_type,
            seen_status,
            society_id,
            title,
            body,
            dbo.GetRelativeTime(timestamp) AS timestamp
        FROM notify_status
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
          AND user_id = @owner_id and seen_status=0
        ORDER BY notify_status_id DESC;
    END
	
	IF @operation = 'UpdateNotificationStatus'
BEGIN
    UPDATE notify_status 
    SET seen_status =1
    WHERE notify_status_id = @notify_status_id;
END

IF @operation = 'MarkAllSeenByType'
BEGIN
    UPDATE notify_status
    SET seen_status = 1
    WHERE society_id = @society_id
      AND user_id = @owner_id
      AND notification_type = @notification_type
      AND seen_status = 0;
END



END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_owner_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@owner_id INT=0, 
@name NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@flat NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@build_id INT=0, 
@poss_date DATE=NULL, 
@pre_addr1 NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@pre_add2 NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@pre_mob NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@alter_mob NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@email NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@age INT=0, @married_id INT=0, 
@dob DATE=NULL, @f_id INT=0, 
@flat_type_id INT=0, 
@occup NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@monthly_income NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@language NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@off_addr1 NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@off_addr2 NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 
@off_tel NVARCHAR (150)=NULL, 

@id_proof NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL, 
@agreement_path NVARCHAR (MAX) = NULL,
@police_verification_path NVARCHAR (MAX) = NULL,

@photo_name NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL, 
@wing_id INT=0, 
@aggrement_date DATE=NULL, 
@aggrement_period_from DATE=NULL, 
@aggrement_period_to DATE=NULL, 
@police_verification_date DATE=NULL, 
@type NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, 
@o_ex_id INT=0, 
@f_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@flat_id Int=0,
@relation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@f_occu NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@f_dob SMALLDATETIME=NULL, 
@society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@home_no int=0,
@division_id int=0,
@district_id int=0,
@village_id nvarchar(50)=Null,
@enrolment_date datetime=Null,
@termination_date datetime=Null,
@house_no int=0,
@house_type nvarchar(50)=Null,
@sq_ft decimal(18,2) = NULL,
@address nvarchar(50)=Null,
@hobby nvarchar(50)=Null,
@area_of_work nvarchar(50)=Null,
@recipients_id int=0,
 @new AS int =0,
 @doc_id int=0,
 @search nvarchar(200)=null,
 @type_id int =0,
 @notify_app int=0, 
 @messages nvarchar(500) = null,
   @mask_phone BIT = NULL,
  @mask_email BIT = NULL,
  @share_gate BIT = NULL,
    @job_title NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@contact nvarchar(10) = null,
	@login_type nvarchar(10) = null,
	@profile_image nvarchar(MAX)=null,
	@token nvarchar(max)=null,
	 @message_sub nvarchar(50)=null,
	 @owner_type int=0,
	 @r_id int = 0
	


AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update' --sagar d changes : id_paths added (2)
        BEGIN
            IF @owner_id = 0

                BEGIN
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = owner_id

                    FROM     dbo.owner_master

                    ORDER BY owner_id DESC;

                    IF @new IS NULL

                        BEGIN

                            SELECT @new = 0;

                        END

                    ELSE

                        BEGIN

                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;

                        END

					if @type = 'rent'
						 UPDATE flat_master SET is_rented = 1 WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id;
					else
						 UPDATE flat_master SET	flat_status = 1  WHERE flat_id = @flat_id;

                    INSERT  INTO dbo.owner_master (owner_id,poss_date, name, pre_mob, alter_mob, email, married_id, dob, flat_id, occup, monthly_income, off_addr1, off_addr2, off_tel, id_proof, photo_name, wing_id, type, society_id, aggrement_date, aggrement_period_from, aggrement_period_to, police_verification_date, flat_type_id,doc_id, mask_phone,mask_email,login_status,active_status,app_notify,call_notify,agreement_path,police_verification_path)

                    VALUES  (@new,@poss_date, dbo.InitCap(@name), @pre_mob, @alter_mob, @email, @married_id, @dob, @flat_id, @occup, @monthly_income, dbo.InitCap(@off_addr1), dbo.InitCap(@off_addr2), @off_tel, @id_proof, @photo_name, @wing_id, @type, @society_id, @aggrement_date, @aggrement_period_from, @aggrement_period_to, @police_verification_date, @flat_type_id,@doc_id,1,1,0,0,0,0,@agreement_path,@police_verification_path);

                   

                        SELECT @new AS owner_id;

                 
                END
 
 
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.owner_master
                    SET    poss_date                = @poss_date,
                           name                     = dbo.InitCap(@name),
                           pre_mob                  = @pre_mob,
                           alter_mob                = @alter_mob,
                           email                    = @email,
                           @married_id              = @married_id,
                           dob                      = @dob,
                           flat_id                  = @flat_id,
                           occup                    = @occup,
                           monthly_income           = @monthly_income,
                           off_addr1                = dbo.InitCap(@off_addr1),
                           off_addr2                = dbo.InitCap(@off_addr2),
                           off_tel                  = @off_tel,
                           id_proof                 = @id_proof,
						   agreement_path			= @agreement_path,
						   police_verification_path = @police_verification_path,
                           photo_name               = @photo_name,
                           wing_id                     = @wing_id,
                           type                     = @type,
                           society_id               = @society_id,
                           aggrement_date           = @aggrement_date,
                           aggrement_period_from    = @aggrement_period_from,
                           aggrement_period_to      = @aggrement_period_to,
                           police_verification_date = @police_verification_date,
                           flat_type_id             = @flat_type_id,
						   doc_id=@doc_id
                    WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
                END
        END
	
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   owner_search_vw
            WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
        END
		 IF @operation = 'GetBasicInfo'
        BEGIN
             SELECT *
    FROM UserData
    WHERE active_status = 0
      AND login_status = 0
      AND pre_mob = @pre_mob
      AND flat_id = @flat_id and owner_id=@owner_id
        END
		 IF @operation = 'UserExist'
        BEGIN
             SELECT pre_mob
    FROM UserData
    WHERE active_status = 0
      AND login_status = 0
      AND pre_mob = @pre_mob
     
        END
		 IF @operation = 'CheckFlat'
        BEGIN
             SELECT *
    FROM UserData
    WHERE active_status = 0
      AND login_status = 0
      AND trim(pre_mob) =@pre_mob
	 
    -- update owner_extension set contactNo='7057133255' where owner_id=22
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            SELECT @flat_id = flat_id
            FROM   owner_master
            WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
            UPDATE flat_master
            SET    flat_status = 0
            WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id;

            UPDATE dbo.owner_master set login_status=1,active_status=1
            WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
           UPDATE dbo.owner_extension set login_status= 1,active_status=1
            WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'D_Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @o_ex_id = 0
                BEGIN
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.owner_extension (owner_id, f_name, relation, f_occu, f_dob, society_id,mask_phone,mask_email,login_status,active_status)
                    VALUES                          (@owner_id, dbo.InitCap(@f_name), dbo.InitCap(@relation), @f_occu, @f_dob, @society_id,1,1,0,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.owner_extension
                    SET    owner_id       = @owner_id,
                           f_name     = dbo.InitCap(@f_name),
                           relation   = dbo.InitCap(@relation),
                           f_occu     = @f_occu,
                           f_dob      = @f_dob,
                           society_id = @society_id
                    WHERE  o_ex_id = @o_ex_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show_Family'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.owner_extension                                                
            WHERE login_status=0 and active_status=0 and owner_id = @owner_id;
        END

		 IF @operation = 'GetFamily'
        BEGIN
            SELECT name,login, relation,profile_image,owner_id, pre_mob
            FROM   dbo.userdata                                                
            WHERE login_status=0 and active_status=0 and flat_id = @flat_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_family_mem_exist'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.owner_extension
            WHERE  f_name = @f_name and owner_id=@owner_id and o_ex_id=@o_ex_id and active_status=0;
        END
		    IF @operation = 'ownerDue'
        BEGIN
SELECT        dbo.owner_master.name, owner_master.flat_id,  DATENAME(M, dbo.maintenance_cal.gen_date) AS month, Year(dbo.maintenance_cal.gen_date)as year, dbo.maintenance_cal.tax_interest_amt, dbo.maintenance_cal.due as amt_forward, dbo.maintenance_cal.tax_interest_amt+ dbo.maintenance_cal.due as total, 
                         dbo.flat_types.w_name + ' ' + dbo.flat_types.flat_no AS unit
FROM            dbo.owner_master left  JOIN
                         dbo.flat_types ON dbo.owner_master.flat_id = dbo.flat_types.flat_id LEFT OUTER JOIN
                         dbo.maintenance_cal ON dbo.owner_master.flat_id = dbo.maintenance_cal.flat_id
WHERE        dbo.maintenance_cal.status <>3 and dbo.maintenance_cal.amt_forward <>0 and    maintenance_cal.due_date< getdate()  and  dbo.maintenance_cal.society_id=@society_id and  dbo.maintenance_cal.flat_id=@flat_id
        END

    IF @operation = 'D_delete'
        BEGIN
		if Exists (select o_ex_id from owner_extension where o_ex_id=@o_ex_id)
            Update dbo.owner_extension  Set active_status=1 Where o_ex_id = @o_ex_id;
		else
			Update owner_master set active_status=1 where owner_id=@o_ex_id
        END
		

		 IF @operation = 'Add_Hobby'
        BEGIN
		 SELECT   TOP (1) @new = user_hobby_id    FROM     dbo.userHobby   ORDER BY user_hobby_id DESC;
             IF @new IS NULL
               BEGIN
                 SELECT @new = 0;
               END
             ELSE
                 BEGIN
                  SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                 END        
		Insert into userHobby VALUES(@new,@hobby,@owner_id,@o_ex_id,0)           
        END
		 IF @operation = 'Add_Work'
        BEGIN
		 SELECT   TOP (1) @new = work_id     FROM     dbo.userAreaofWork   ORDER BY work_id DESC;
             IF @new IS NULL
               BEGIN
                 SELECT @new = 0;
               END
             ELSE
                 BEGIN
                  SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                 END        
		Insert into userAreaofWork VALUES(@new,@area_of_work,@owner_id,@o_ex_id,0)           
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select_Hobby'
        BEGIN
            IF @type = 'family'
                SELECT hobby,
                       o_ex_id AS owner_id
                FROM   userHobby
                WHERE  o_ex_id = @owner_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT hobby,
                       owner_id
                FROM   userHobby
                WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
        END

	 	if @operation  = 'AddFamilyMember'
			Begin
			SELECT   TOP (1) @new = o_ex_id

                    FROM     dbo.owner_extension

                    ORDER BY o_ex_id DESC;

                    IF @new IS NULL

                        BEGIN

                            SELECT @new = 0;

                        END

                    ELSE

                        BEGIN

                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;

                        END

			insert into owner_extension (o_ex_id, owner_id, f_name, relation, society_id, contactNo, active_status, login_status) values (@new, @owner_id, @f_name, @relation, @society_id, @contact, 0, 0);
			end


    IF @operation = 'Select_Work'
        BEGIN
            IF @type = 'family'
                SELECT area_of_work,
                       o_ex_id AS owner_id
                FROM   userAreaofWork
                WHERE  o_ex_id = @owner_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT area_of_work,
                       owner_id
                FROM   userAreaofWork
                WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete_Work'
        BEGIN
            IF @type = 'family'
                delete from userAreaofWork 
                WHERE  o_ex_id = @owner_id;
            ELSE
                delete from userAreaofWork    
                WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete_Hobby'
        BEGIN
            IF @type = 'family'
               delete from userHobby 
                WHERE  o_ex_id = @owner_id;
            ELSE
                delete from userHobby 
                WHERE  owner_id = @owner_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'getDefaulter'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   dbo.owner_search_vw.name,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.type,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit,
                     SUM(dbo.maintenance_cal.due) AS due,
                     'defaulterList' AS listType,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.email,
                     dbo.owner_search_vw.pre_mob
            FROM     dbo.maintenance_cal
                     INNER JOIN
                     dbo.owner_search_vw
                     ON dbo.maintenance_cal.flat_id = dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id
            WHERE    (dbo.maintenance_cal.due > 0)
                    and active_status=0 and owner_search_vw.society_id=@society_id
            GROUP BY dbo.owner_search_vw.name, dbo.owner_search_vw.type, dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit, dbo.owner_search_vw.email, dbo.owner_search_vw.pre_mob;
        END
    IF @operation = 'cust_sel'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   owner_master
            WHERE  pre_mob = @pre_mob and active_status=0;  
                   
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_no'
        BEGIN
            IF @owner_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.owner_master
                WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id and active_status=0
                       AND wing_id = @wing_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.owner_master
                WHERE  flat_id = @flat_id and active_status=0
                       AND owner_id = @owner_id
                       AND owner_id <> @owner_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_build'
        BEGIN
            IF @owner_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.owner_master
                WHERE  wing_id = @wing_id and active_status=0
                       AND flat_id = @flat_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.owner_master
                WHERE  wing_id = @wing_id and active_status=0
                       AND owner_id = @owner_id
                       AND owner_id <> @owner_id;
        END
   
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     owner_search_vw
            WHERE  login_status=0 and active_status=0 and type = @type
                     AND society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY flat_id DESC;
        END



	IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     owner_search_vw
            WHERE  login_status=0 and active_status=0 and type = @type
                     AND society_id = @society_id and( name like @search+'%' or build_name like @search+'%' or unit like @search+'%' or sq_ft like @search+'%' or flat_type like @search+'%')
            ORDER BY flat_id DESC;
        END
    IF @operation = 'getFlat'
        BEGIN
            SELECT flat_id
            FROM   flat_master
            WHERE  wing_id = @wing_id and active_status=0
                   AND flat_type_id = @flat_type_id
                   AND flat_no = @flat
                   AND society_id = @society_id;
        END

		IF @operation = 'Fill_wing'
		BEGIN
		if @build_id  !=0 and @type_id !=0 
		Select distinct wing_id,(name + w_name) as name from dbo.flat_types where  active_status=0 and society_id =@society_id and wing_id = @build_id and flat_type_id =@type_id 
		else Select wing_id,(name + w_name) as name from global_society_view where society_id= @society_id 
		

		END
		if @operation = 'types'
		begin
		Select *  from types
		end

		if @operation = 'married'
		begin
		Select *  from married_status
		end

		if @operation = 'Fill_doc'
		begin
		Select * from doc_master where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id
		end

		if @operation = 'flat_types' --Rental Master
		begin
		if @build_id  !=0  and @type_id !=0
		 begin
		  if @type_id=3
		 Select distinct flat_type,flat_id from dbo.flat_types where is_rented= 0  and active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id  and 
		 wing_id = @build_id and flat_type_id =@type_id
		 else
		Select distinct flat_type,flat_id from dbo.flat_types where is_rented= 0 and flat_status=1 and active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id  and 
		 wing_id = @build_id and flat_type_id =@type_id
		
		 end

		else 
		Select society_id,flat_id,(flat_no +'  '+ usage+'  '+ bed +'  '+ sq_ft) as flat_type  from flat where flat_status=1  and active_status=0 and    society_id=@society_id
		end

		if @operation = 'flat_owners' --Owner master
		begin
		if @build_id  !=0  and @type_id !=0
		
		Select distinct flat_type,flat_id from dbo.flat_types where flat_status=0 and   active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id  and 
		 wing_id = @build_id and flat_type_id =@type_id
		else 
		Select society_id,flat_id,(flat_no +'  '+ usage+'  '+ bed +'  '+ sq_ft) as flat_type  from flat where flat_status=0  and active_status=0 and    society_id=@society_id
		end
	  IF @operation = 'HomeNoFetch'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM owner_master 
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND pre_mob = @pre_mob
          AND flat_id = @flat_id;
    END


		IF @operation = 'GetOwnerList'
        BEGIN
            select name,Unit,build_name,type, 'All' as listType from owner_search_vw where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id
        END
		
		IF @operation = 'get_users'
        BEGIN
		if @recipients_id=1
            select owner_id as user_id,type,token from userdata where token is not null and type='owner' and active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id  -- owners
	    if @recipients_id=2
			      select owner_id as user_id,type,token from userdata where token is not null and type='Rent' and active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id  --rent
		if @recipients_id=3
				  select name, owner_id as user_id,type,token from userdata where token is not null and active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id     --owener and rent
		if @recipients_id=4
				  select user_id,'Member' as type,token,web_token from UserLogin where web_token is not null and active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id   --associate member
        if @recipients_id=5
				  select owner_id as user_id,type,token from userdata where token is not null and active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id union all
		select user_id,'Member' as type,web_token as token from UserLogin where web_token is not null and active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id  
       
		END


		IF @operation = 'Deactivate'
    BEGIN
        IF @type = 'owner'
            UPDATE owner_master SET login_status = 0 WHERE owner_id = @owner_id;
        ELSE
            UPDATE owner_extension SET login_status = 0 WHERE o_ex_id = @owner_id;
    END

	IF @operation = 'NotifyCall'
    BEGIN
        IF @type = 'owner'
            UPDATE owner_master SET call_notify = @notify_app WHERE owner_id = @owner_id;
        ELSE
            UPDATE owner_extension SET call_notify = @notify_app WHERE o_ex_id = @owner_id;
    END

	 IF @operation = 'NotifyApp'
    BEGIN
        IF @type = 'owner'
            UPDATE owner_master SET app_notify = @notify_app WHERE owner_id = @owner_id;
        ELSE
            UPDATE owner_extension SET app_notify = @notify_app WHERE o_ex_id = @owner_id;
    END

	
		IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerDob'
BEGIN
    UPDATE owner_master 
    SET dob = @dob 
    WHERE pre_mob = @pre_mob 
      AND owner_id = @owner_id;
END

-- For Owner Extension
IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerExtensionDob'
BEGIN
    UPDATE owner_extension 
    SET f_dob = @dob 
    WHERE contactNo = @pre_mob 
      AND o_ex_id = @owner_id;
END

-- Case 1: Add Owner Message
--IF @operation = 'AddOwnerMessage'
--BEGIN
--    INSERT INTO Owner_Messages (flat_id, message, type, society_id, date,owner_id,message_sub,owner_type)
--    VALUES (@flat_id, @messages, @type, @society_id, GETDATE(),@owner_id,@message_sub,@owner_type);
--END
IF @operation = 'AddOwnerMessage'
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO Owner_Messages (flat_id, message, type, society_id, date, owner_id, message_sub, owner_type, view_status)
    OUTPUT INSERTED.r_id  -- ✅ return the inserted ID
    VALUES (@flat_id, @messages, @type, @society_id, GETDATE(), @owner_id, @message_sub, @owner_type, 0);
END


-- Case 2: Add Security Alert
IF @operation = 'AddSecurityAlert'
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO Security_Alert (flat_id, type, society_id, date)
	    OUTPUT INSERTED.security_id
    VALUES (@flat_id, @type, @society_id, GETDATE());
END

-- Case 1: Update Email in owner_master
IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerEmail'
BEGIN
    UPDATE owner_master
    SET email = @email
    WHERE pre_mob = @pre_mob 
      AND owner_id = @owner_id;
END

-- Case 2: Update Email in owner_extension
IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerExtensionEmail'
BEGIN
    UPDATE owner_extension
    SET email_id = @email
    WHERE contactNo = @pre_mob 
      AND o_ex_id = @owner_id;
END

-- Case 1: Update Mobile in owner_master
IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerMobile'
BEGIN
    UPDATE owner_master
    SET pre_mob = @pre_mob
    WHERE owner_id = @owner_id;
END

-- Case 2: Update Mobile in owner_extension
IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerExtensionMobile'
BEGIN
    UPDATE owner_extension
    SET contactNo = @pre_mob
    WHERE o_ex_id = @owner_id;
END
 IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerSetting'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE owner_master
        SET mask_phone = @mask_phone,
            mask_email = @mask_email,
            share_gate = @share_gate
        WHERE owner_id = @owner_id;
    END

    -- Update Owner Extension Settings
    IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerExtensionSetting'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE owner_extension
        SET mask_phone = @mask_phone,
            mask_email = @mask_email,
            share_gate = @share_gate
        WHERE o_ex_id = @owner_id;
    END

	 IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerJobTitle'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE owner_master
        SET occup = @job_title
        WHERE pre_mob = @pre_mob
          AND owner_id = @owner_id;
    END

    -- Update Job Title in owner_extension
    IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerExtensionJobTitle'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE owner_extension
        SET f_occu = @job_title
        WHERE contactNo = @pre_mob
          AND o_ex_id = @owner_id;
    END

	    IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerLanguage'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE owner_master
        SET language = @language
        WHERE pre_mob = @pre_mob
          AND owner_id = @owner_id;
    END

    -- Update Extension Language
    IF @operation = 'UpdateOwnerExtensionLanguage'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE owner_extension
        SET language = @language
        WHERE contactNo = @pre_mob
          AND o_ex_id = @owner_id;
    END

	    IF @operation = 'UpdateHomeNumber'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE owner_master
        SET home_no = @home_no
        WHERE flat_id = @flat_id;
    END

	IF @operation = 'GetResidenceDirectory'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM owner_search_vw
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND society_id = @society_id;
    END

	    IF @operation = 'GetUnitList'
    BEGIN
        SELECT distinct
            flat_id,
            unit
        FROM owner_search_vw
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND society_id = @society_id;
    END

	if @operation = 'updatePersonDetails'
		BEGIN
			if @login_type = 'owner'
				BEGIN
					update owner_master set name = @name, pre_mob = @contact, email = @email where owner_id = @owner_id;
				END
			if @login_type = 'family'
				update owner_extension set f_name = @name, contactNo = @contact, email_id = @email where o_ex_id = @owner_id;
			
		END

		if @operation = 'ProfileImage'
		BEGIN
			if @login_type = 'owner'
				BEGIN
					update owner_master set profile_image = @profile_image where owner_id = @owner_id;
				END
			if @login_type = 'family'
				update owner_extension set profile_image = @profile_image where o_ex_id = @owner_id;
			
		END

		if @operation = 'get_messages_count'
			BEGIN
				select count(*) as message_count from owner_Messages where society_id = @society_id AND view_status = 0;
			END
	

		if @operation = 'updateViewStatus'
			BEGIN
				update Owner_Messages set view_status = 1 where r_id = @r_id
			END

		if @operation = 'GetMessages'
			BEGIN
				select * from owner_messages_vw where society_id = @society_id AND type = 'admin'
				
			END
			IF @operation = 'ProfileImageDelete'
BEGIN
	IF @login_type = 'owner'
	BEGIN
		UPDATE owner_master
		SET profile_image = NULL
		WHERE owner_id = @owner_id;
	END
	ELSE IF @login_type = 'family'
	BEGIN
		UPDATE owner_extension
		SET profile_image = NULL
		WHERE o_ex_id = @owner_id;
	END

	SELECT 'Profile image deleted successfully' AS message, 1 AS success;


			
		END
		--if @operation = 'AddToken'
		--BEGIN
		--	if @login_type = 'owner'
		--		BEGIN
		--			update owner_master set token = @token where owner_id = @owner_id;
		--		END
		--	if @login_type = 'family'
		--		update owner_extension set token = @token where o_ex_id = @owner_id;
			
		--END 
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_parking]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 16:58:48 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_parking]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@place_id int=0,
@parking_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@park_for int =0,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@flat_id int=0,
@vehicle_id int = Null,
@vehicle_type int = Null,
@owner_id int = Null,
@vehicle_no nvarchar(50) = Null,
@model_name nvarchar(50) = Null


AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @place_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = place_id
                    FROM     dbo.parking
                    ORDER BY place_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO dbo.parking(place_id,parking_no,park_for,society_id,active_status)values(@new,dbo.InitCap(@parking_no),@park_for,@society_id,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.parking set parking_no=dbo.InitCap(@parking_no),park_for=@park_for,society_id=@society_id where place_id=@place_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'	
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.parking where  place_id=@place_id
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	    Update dbo.parking set active_status=1 where  place_id=@place_id             
    	End

  If @operation='Grid_Show'

	begin 
	 SELECT place_id,parking_no, society_id, Case when park_for=0 then 'Bike' else 'Car'  end AS park_for  from dbo.parking  where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id  ORDER BY place_id DESC
	 end
	 if @operation ='ParkingSlot'
	 begin  
	 SELECT place_id, parking_no
    FROM parking
    WHERE active_status = 0
      AND park_for = @park_for
      AND society_id = @society_id;
	  end
	   If @operation='Search'

	begin 
	 SELECT * from dbo.parking  where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and(  park_for like  @search+'%' or parking_no like  @search+'%') ORDER BY place_id DESC
	 end

	 --if @operation='VehicleList'
	 --Begin 
	 --  SELECT *
  --  FROM Vehicle
  --  WHERE flat_id = @flat_id
  --    AND society_id = @society_id;
	 -- End

	IF @operation = 'VehicleList'
BEGIN
    SELECT 
        vehicle_id,
        vehicle_no,
        model_name,
        society_id,
        flat_id,
        vehicle_type,
        park_place_id,
        CASE 
            WHEN park_place_id IS NULL OR park_place_id = 0 THEN NULL
            ELSE 'Parking Reserved'
        END AS parking_status
    FROM Vehicle
    WHERE flat_id = @flat_id
      AND society_id = @society_id;
END



	  If @operation='check_exist'
	   Begin
	    If @place_id=0
		Select * from dbo.parking Where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and parking_no=@parking_no 
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.parking Where parking_no=@parking_no AND place_id=@place_id AND place_id<>@place_id
	   End

	if @operation = 'deleteFamilyVehicle'
		Begin
			delete from vehicle where vehicle_id = @vehicle_id
		end
	

	    IF @operation = 'Insertfamilyvehicle'
    BEGIN
	SELECT   TOP (1) @new = vehicle_id

                    FROM     dbo.Vehicle

                    ORDER BY vehicle_id DESC;

                    IF @new IS NULL

                        BEGIN

                            SELECT @new = 0;

                        END

                    ELSE

                        BEGIN

                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;

                        END
        INSERT INTO Vehicle (vehicle_id, society_id, flat_id, vehicle_no, vehicle_type, model_name)
        VALUES (@new, @society_id, @flat_id, @vehicle_no, @vehicle_type, @model_name);

    END
	
END
GO

GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_parking_master]
(
    @operation NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @parking_id INT = 0,
    @name NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @park_for NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @park_type NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @place_id INT = 0,
    @from_date DATE = NULL,
    @to_date DATE = NULL,
    @status INT = 0,
    @model_name NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @contact_no NCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @vehicle_no NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @sticker_no NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @search NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
    @flat_id INT = 0,
    @vehicle_id INT = 0
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    /* ================= INSERT / UPDATE ================= */
    IF @operation = 'Update'
    BEGIN
        IF @parking_id = 0
        BEGIN
            DECLARE @new INT;
            SELECT @new = ISNULL(MAX(parking_id), 0) + 1 FROM parking_master;

            INSERT INTO parking_master
            (
                parking_id, name, park_for, park_type, place_id,
                from_date, to_date, society_id, contact_no,
                vehicle_no, model_name, sticker_no, status, active_status
            )
            VALUES
            (
                @new, dbo.InitCap(@name), @park_for, @park_type, @place_id,
                @from_date, @to_date, @society_id, @contact_no,
                dbo.InitCap(@vehicle_no), @model_name, @sticker_no, @status, 0
            );
        END
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            UPDATE parking_master
            SET
                name = dbo.InitCap(@name),
                park_for = @park_for,
                park_type = @park_type,
                place_id = @place_id,
                from_date = @from_date,
                to_date = @to_date,
                society_id = @society_id,
                contact_no = @contact_no,
                vehicle_no = dbo.InitCap(@vehicle_no)
            WHERE parking_id = @parking_id;
        END
    END

    /* ================= SELECT VEHICLE ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'Select'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM vehicle
        WHERE vehicle_id = @vehicle_id;
    END

    /* ================= DELETE ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'Delete'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE vehicle
        SET park_place_id = 0
        WHERE vehicle_id = @vehicle_id;
    END

    /* ================= CHECK DUPLICATE VEHICLE ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'check_no'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM parking_master
        WHERE LOWER(LTRIM(RTRIM(vehicle_no))) = LOWER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@vehicle_no)))
          AND active_status = 0
          AND (@parking_id = 0 OR parking_id <> @parking_id);
    END

    /* ================= GRID SHOW ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
    BEGIN
       SELECT
    v.vehicle_id,
    v.model_name,
    v.flat_id,
    v.vehicle_type,
    v.vehicle_no,
    p.parking_no,
    CASE 
        WHEN v.vehicle_type = 0 THEN 'Bike' 
        ELSE 'Car' 
    END AS park_for,
    o.name,
    o.pre_mob               -- contact_no comes from owner table
FROM Vehicle v
INNER JOIN parking p ON p.place_id = v.park_place_id
INNER JOIN owner_master o ON o.flat_id = v.flat_id
WHERE v.society_id = @society_id;
    END

    /* ================= SEARCH ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'Search'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM parking_master
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
          AND active_status = 0
          AND (
                name LIKE @search + '%'
                OR contact_no LIKE @search + '%'
                OR vehicle_no LIKE @search + '%'
              )
        ORDER BY parking_id DESC;
    END

    /* ================= ASSIGN PLACE ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'AssignPlace'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE vehicle
        SET park_place_id = @place_id
        WHERE vehicle_no = @vehicle_no
          AND flat_id = @flat_id;
    END

    /* ================= FILL OWNER ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'fill_owner'
    BEGIN
        SELECT flat_id, name
        FROM owner_master
        WHERE society_id = @society_id;
    END

    /* ================= FILL VEHICLE ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'fill_vehicle'
    BEGIN
        SELECT vehicle_id, vehicle_no, vehicle_type
        FROM Vehicle
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
          AND flat_id = @flat_id
          AND (park_place_id IS NULL OR park_place_id = 0);
    END

    /* ================= FILL FREE PARKING ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'fill_parking_place'
    BEGIN
        SELECT place_id, parking_no
        FROM parking
        WHERE park_for = @vehicle_id
          AND society_id = @society_id
          AND place_id NOT IN (
                SELECT park_place_id
                FROM Vehicle
                WHERE park_place_id IS NOT NULL
                  AND park_place_id <> 0
          );
    END

    /* ================= FILL ALL PARKING ================= */
    ELSE IF @operation = 'fill_place'
    BEGIN
       SELECT p.place_id, p.parking_no
FROM parking p
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM vehicle v
    WHERE v.park_place_id = p.place_id 
)AND park_for = @vehicle_id
          AND society_id = @society_id;
    END
END
GO


37)USE [society]
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_pdc_reminder]
  @operation nvarchar(50)=NULL,
  @pdc_rem_id int=0,
  @owner_id int=0,
  @build_id int=0,
  @wing_id int=0,
  @chqno nvarchar(10)=NULL,
  @che_amount float=0,
  @che_date date=NULL,
  @che_dep int=0,
  @che_ret int=0,
  @che_can int=0,
  @startdate date=Null,
  @enddate date=Null,
  @society_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
  @address1 nvarchar(100)= Null,
  @address2 nvarchar(100)=Null,
  @o_name nvarchar(50)=null,
  @search nvarchar(50) =null,
 @recivable decimal =0


AS
BEGIN

  If @operation='Update'
     Begin
       If @pdc_rem_id=0
         begin
           Declare @new int
           Select TOP(1) @new=pdc_rem_id FROM dbo.pdc_reminder ORDER BY pdc_rem_id DESC
           If @new IS NULL OR @new=0
              Select @new=1
           Else
              Select @new = @new + 1

           
		   Insert Into dbo.pdc_reminder(pdc_rem_id,owner_id,wing_id,chqno,che_amount,che_date,che_dep,che_ret,che_can,society_id,active_status)Values(@new,@owner_id,@wing_id,@chqno,@che_amount,@che_date,@che_dep,@che_ret,@che_can,@society_id,0)
            Select @new as p_id from dbo.pdc_reminder
		 end
       Else
         begin
           Update dbo.pdc_reminder Set wing_id=@wing_id,chqno=@chqno,che_amount=@che_amount,che_date=@che_date,che_dep=@che_dep,che_ret=@che_ret,che_can=@che_can,society_id=@society_id WHERE pdc_rem_id=@pdc_rem_id
           Select @pdc_rem_id as p_id from dbo.pdc_reminder

		 end
     End


  If @operation='Select'
     Begin
     Select * from pdc_reminder  WHERE pdc_rem_id=@pdc_rem_id
     End


  If @operation='Select_All'
     Begin
       Select * FROM dbo.pdc_reminder where active_status=0
     End


  If @operation='Delete'
     Begin
       Update dbo.pdc_reminder set active_status=0 WHERE pdc_rem_id=@pdc_rem_id
     End


  If @operation='check_delete'
     Begin
       SELECT * FROM dbo.pdc_reminder WHERE pdc_rem_id=@pdc_rem_id and active_status=0
     End


  If @operation='check_o_name_exists'
     Begin
       if @pdc_rem_id<>0
           SELECT * FROM dbo.pdc_reminder WHERE owner_id=@owner_id AND pdc_rem_id<>@pdc_rem_id and active_status=0
       else
           SELECT * FROM dbo.pdc_reminder WHERE  owner_id=@owner_id and active_status=0
     End

    
   If @operation='owner_select'
      Begin
   	     SELECT        dbo.owner_search_vw.name AS owner_name, dbo.owner_search_vw.pre_mob, dbo.owner_search_vw.alter_mob, dbo.owner_search_vw.email, dbo.owner_search_vw.w_name, dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name, 
                         dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.wing_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_no + ', ' + dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name + ' ,' + dbo.building_master.address1 AS pre_addr1, dbo.building_master.address2 AS pre_add2, 
                         dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_no
FROM            dbo.owner_search_vw INNER JOIN
                         dbo.building_master ON dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id = dbo.building_master.build_id
		  WHERE          dbo.owner_search_vw.owner_id=@owner_id and dbo.owner_search_vw.active_status=0
	  End 


   If @operation='ownerwise_cheq'
   if @owner_id is not null
      Begin
	       --SELECT p_id, che_no, che_amount, che_date,  Case when che_dep=0 then 'Not' Else 'Yes' End as che_dep , case when che_ret=0 then 'No' else 'Yes' end as che_ret ,case when che_can=0 then 'No' Else 'Yes' end as che_can FROM dbo.pdc_reminder Where o_id=@o_id
	  	  SELECT pdc_rem_id, chqno, che_amount, che_date, che_dep,che_ret ,che_can FROM dbo.pdc_reminder Where owner_id=@owner_id and active_status=0
	  End

  If @operation='check_chq_no_exists'
     Begin
       if @pdc_rem_id=0
           SELECT * FROM dbo.pdc_reminder WHERE chqno=@chqno and active_status=0
		   --AND p_id<>@p_id
       else
           SELECT * FROM dbo.pdc_reminder WHERE chqno=@chqno and active_status=0
     End

	 --IF @operation='save_change_rem'

	 --  if @che_dep=1
	 --  begin
		--	SELECT        @che_amount=dbo.pdc_reminder.che_amount, @owner_id=dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id, @build_id=dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id, @wing_id=dbo.owner_search_vw.wing_id, @society_id=dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id, @recivable=ISNULL(SUM(dbo.maintenance_cal.due), 0) , @chqno= dbo.pdc_reminder.chqno, @che_date=dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date
		--FROM            dbo.pdc_reminder INNER JOIN
		--						 dbo.owner_search_vw ON dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id = dbo.owner_search_vw.owner_id INNER JOIN

		--			  DECLARE @LastInvoiceNumber AS VARCHAR (20);
		--			DECLARE @NewInvoiceNumber AS VARCHAR (20);

		--			  SET @NewInvoiceNumber='RPT0001'
		--		   Else
		--		   begin
		--			  SELECT   @LastInvoiceNumber = receipt_no  FROM receipt   ORDER BY receipt_no DESC;
		--			SET @NewInvoiceNumber = CAST (SUBSTRING(@LastInvoiceNumber, 4, 8) AS VARCHAR) + 1;
		--			SET @NewInvoiceNumber = REPLACE(@LastInvoiceNumber, @NewInvoiceNumber - 1, @NewInvoiceNumber);
		--			if   len(@NewInVoiceNumber)>7
		--			 SET @NewInvoiceNumber = REPLACE(@NewInvoiceNumber,'RPT0','RPT');
		--		  end
		--		  exec sp_receipt @operation='Update',  @build_id=@build_id,@wing_id=@wing_id,@owner_id=@owner_id,@chqdate=@che_date,@recivable_amt=@recivable,@received_amt=@che_amount,@society_id=@society_id,@pay_mode=3,@receipt_no=@NewInvoiceNumber ,@date=@che_date
		--end  
  -- End
  IF @operation='save_change_rem'
Begin
    UPDATE dbo.pdc_reminder 
    SET dbo.pdc_reminder.che_dep=@che_dep,
        dbo.pdc_reminder.che_ret=@che_ret, 
        dbo.pdc_reminder.che_can=@che_can  
    WHERE pdc_rem_id=@pdc_rem_id
    
    IF @che_dep=1
    BEGIN
        SELECT @che_amount=dbo.pdc_reminder.che_amount, 
               @owner_id=dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id, 
               @build_id=dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id, 
               @wing_id=dbo.owner_search_vw.wing_id, 
               @society_id=dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id, 
               @recivable=ISNULL(SUM(dbo.maintenance_cal.due), 0), 
               @chqno=dbo.pdc_reminder.chqno, 
               @che_date=dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date
        FROM dbo.pdc_reminder 
        INNER JOIN dbo.owner_search_vw ON dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id = dbo.owner_search_vw.owner_id 
        INNER JOIN dbo.maintenance_cal ON dbo.owner_search_vw.flat_id = dbo.maintenance_cal.flat_id  -- Changed from owner_id to flat_id
        WHERE pdc_rem_id=@pdc_rem_id
        GROUP BY dbo.pdc_reminder.che_amount, 
                 dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id, 
                 dbo.owner_search_vw.build_id, 
                 dbo.owner_search_vw.wing_id, 
                 dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id, 
                 dbo.pdc_reminder.chqno, 
                 dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date
        
        DECLARE @LastInvoiceNumber AS VARCHAR(20);
        DECLARE @NewInvoiceNumber AS VARCHAR(20);
        
        SELECT TOP(1) @NewInvoiceNumber=receipt_no FROM receipt ORDER BY receipt_no DESC;
        
        IF @NewInvoiceNumber IS NULL
            SET @NewInvoiceNumber='RPT0001'
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            SELECT @LastInvoiceNumber = receipt_no FROM receipt ORDER BY receipt_no DESC;
            SET @NewInvoiceNumber = CAST(SUBSTRING(@LastInvoiceNumber, 4, 8) AS VARCHAR) + 1;
            SET @NewInvoiceNumber = REPLACE(@LastInvoiceNumber, @NewInvoiceNumber - 1, @NewInvoiceNumber);
            IF LEN(@NewInvoiceNumber) > 7
                SET @NewInvoiceNumber = REPLACE(@NewInvoiceNumber,'RPT0','RPT');
        END
        
        EXEC sp_receipt @operation='Update',  
                        @build_id=@build_id,
                        @wing_id=@wing_id,
                        @owner_id=@owner_id,
                        @chqdate=@che_date,
                        @recivable_amt=@recivable,
                        @received_amt=@che_amount,
                        @society_id=@society_id,
                        @pay_mode=3,
                        @receipt_no=@NewInvoiceNumber,
                        @date=@che_date
    END  
END
   If @operation='pdc_clear_grid_show'
    Begin
	SELECT        dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id, dbo.owner_master.name AS owner_name, dbo.pdc_reminder.chqno, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_amount, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_dep, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_ret, 
                         dbo.pdc_reminder.che_can, dbo.pdc_reminder.pdc_rem_id
FROM            dbo.pdc_reminder INNER JOIN
                         dbo.owner_master ON dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id = dbo.owner_master.owner_id
	WHERE         dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date BETWEEN @startdate AND @enddate and dbo.pdc_reminder.active_status=0 and dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id=@society_id
	End
  
  If @operation='Grid_Show'
   
      Begin
	  	  --SELECT p_id, chqno, che_amount, che_date, che_dep,che_ret ,che_can FROM dbo.pdc_reminder Where o_id=@o_id
		SELECT        TOP (100) PERCENT dbo.pdc_reminder.pdc_rem_id, dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id, dbo.pdc_reminder.chqno, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_amount, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_dep, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_ret, 
                         dbo.pdc_reminder.che_can, dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id, dbo.owner_search_vw.Unit, dbo.owner_search_vw.build_name,dbo.owner_search_vw.name
FROM            dbo.pdc_reminder INNER JOIN   dbo.owner_search_vw ON dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id = dbo.Owner_search_vw.owner_id	
WHERE dbo.pdc_reminder.active_status=0 and dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id = @society_id ORDER BY dbo.pdc_reminder.pdc_rem_id DESC
   
	  End
	  If @operation='Search'
   
      Begin
	  	  --SELECT p_id, chqno, che_amount, che_date, che_dep,che_ret ,che_can FROM dbo.pdc_reminder Where o_id=@o_id
		SELECT        TOP (100) PERCENT dbo.pdc_reminder.pdc_rem_id, dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id, dbo.pdc_reminder.chqno, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_amount, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_date, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_dep, dbo.pdc_reminder.che_ret, 
                         dbo.pdc_reminder.che_can, dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id, dbo.UserData.Unit, dbo.UserData.build_name,dbo.UserData.name
FROM            dbo.pdc_reminder INNER JOIN   dbo.UserData ON dbo.pdc_reminder.owner_id = dbo.UserData.owner_id	
WHERE dbo.pdc_reminder.active_status=0 and dbo.pdc_reminder.society_id = @society_id and(  Userdata.name like @search+'%' or userdata.build_name like @search+'%' or  userdata.unit like @search+'%' or cast (pdc_reminder.chqno as varchar) like @search+'%' or  cast(pdc_reminder.che_amount as varchar) like @search+'%') ORDER BY dbo.pdc_reminder.pdc_rem_id DESC
           
		 
	  End

	  if @operation= 'fill_owner'
	  begin
	  select owner_id,(name +' '+ flat_no) as name from customer_flat where society_id=@society_id
	  end


	   if @operation= 'fill_wing'
	  begin
	  Select wing_id,(name +' '+ w_name) as name from global_society_view
	  end


END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_PollOptions]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:00:57 ******/

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_PollOptions]
    @Operation NVARCHAR(10),        -- 'INSERT', 'SELECT', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE'
    @PollId    INT = NULL,          -- Now INT for easier math
    @Options   NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, -- Comma-separated for INSERT
    @OptionId  INT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @Operation = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @OptionTable TABLE (RowNum INT IDENTITY(1,1), OptionValue NVARCHAR(200));

        -- Split comma-separated options into table
        INSERT INTO @OptionTable (OptionValue)
        SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(value))
        FROM STRING_SPLIT(@Options, ',');

        -- Insert each option into poll_Options table
        INSERT INTO [dbo].[poll_Options] (PollId, Options, OptionId)
        SELECT 
            CAST(@PollId AS NCHAR(10)),
            OptionValue,
            CAST((@PollId * 100) + RowNum AS NCHAR(10))
        FROM @OptionTable;
    END

    ELSE IF @Operation = 'SELECT'
    BEGIN
        SELECT PollId, Options, OptionId
        FROM [dbo].[poll_Options]
        WHERE (@PollId IS NULL OR PollId = CAST(@PollId AS NCHAR(10)))
          AND (@OptionId IS NULL OR OptionId = CAST(@OptionId AS NCHAR(10)));
    END

    ELSE IF @Operation = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE [dbo].[poll_Options]
        SET Options = @Options
        WHERE PollId = CAST(@PollId AS NCHAR(10))
          AND OptionId = CAST(@OptionId AS NCHAR(10));
    END

    ELSE IF @Operation = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM [dbo].[poll_Options]
        WHERE PollId = CAST(@PollId AS NCHAR(10))
          AND OptionId = CAST(@OptionId AS NCHAR(10));
    END
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_polls]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:01:16 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_polls]
    @Mode NVARCHAR(10),             
    @PollId INT = NULL,
    @user_id INT = 0,
    @Topic NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @ExpiryDate DATE = NULL,
    @AllowMultipleVotes INT = NULL,
    @OneVotePerUnit INT = NULL,
    @Audience int=0,
    @options NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(50)=NULL,
	@user_type NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@ownerApp int =5
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- INSERT MODE
    IF @Mode = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @new INT;

        -- Always get next PollId for this society
        SELECT @new = ISNULL(MAX(PollId), 0) + 1
        FROM dbo.Polls
        WHERE society_id = @society_id;

        -- Insert new poll
        INSERT INTO Polls 
        (
            PollId, 
            user_id, 
            Topic, 
            Description, 
            ExpiryDate, 
            AllowMultipleVotes, 
            OneVotePerUnit, 
            Audience, 
            options, 
            society_id
        )
        VALUES 
        (
            @new, 
            @user_id, 
            @Topic, 
            @Description, 
            @ExpiryDate, 
            @AllowMultipleVotes, 
            @OneVotePerUnit, 
            @Audience, 
            @options, 
            @society_id
        );

        -- Return new PollId
        SELECT @new AS PollId;
    END

    -- UPDATE MODE
    ELSE IF @Mode = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE Polls
        SET
            user_id = @user_id,
            Topic = @Topic,
            Description = @Description,
            ExpiryDate = @ExpiryDate,
            AllowMultipleVotes = @AllowMultipleVotes,
            OneVotePerUnit = @OneVotePerUnit,
            Audience = @Audience,
            options = @options
        WHERE PollId = @PollId
          AND society_id = @society_id;
    END

    -- DELETE MODE
    ELSE IF @Mode = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM Polls
        WHERE PollId = @PollId
          AND society_id = @society_id;
    END

    -- SELECT SINGLE POLL
    ELSE IF @Mode = 'SELECT'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM Polls
        WHERE PollId = @PollId
          AND society_id = @society_id;
    END

    -- SELECT ALL POLLS (WITH OPTIONS)
    --ELSE IF @Mode = 'SELECTALL'
    --BEGIN
    --    DECLARE @cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = '';
    --    DECLARE @query NVARCHAR(MAX);

    --    ;WITH RankedOptions AS
    --    (
    --        SELECT 
    --            p.PollId,
    --            p.user_id,
    --            p.Topic,
    --            p.Description,
    --            ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.PollId ORDER BY po.OptionId) AS OptionNumber,
    --            po.Options + '-' + po.OptionId AS OptionValue
    --        FROM dbo.Polls p
    --        JOIN dbo.poll_Options po 
    --            ON p.PollId = po.PollId
    --        WHERE p.society_id = @society_id
    --    )
    --    SELECT @cols += QUOTENAME('Option' + CAST(OptionNumber AS NVARCHAR)) + ','
    --    FROM RankedOptions
    --    GROUP BY OptionNumber
    --    ORDER BY OptionNumber;

    --    SET @cols = LEFT(@cols, LEN(@cols) - 1);

    --    SET @query = '
    --    ;WITH RankedOptions AS
    --    (
    --        SELECT 
    --            p.PollId,
    --            p.user_id,
    --            p.Topic,
    --            p.Description,
    --            ''Option'' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.PollId ORDER BY po.OptionId) AS NVARCHAR) AS OptionCol,
    --            po.Options + ''-'' + po.OptionId AS OptionValue
    --        FROM dbo.Polls p
    --        JOIN dbo.poll_Options po 
    --            ON p.PollId = po.PollId
    --        WHERE p.society_id = @society_id
    --    )
    --    SELECT PollId, user_id, Topic, Description, ' + @cols + '
    --    FROM RankedOptions
    --    PIVOT
    --    (
    --        MAX(OptionValue)
    --        FOR OptionCol IN (' + @cols + ')
    --    ) p
    --    ';

    --    EXEC sp_executesql @query, N'@society_id NVARCHAR(50)', @society_id = @society_id;
    --END

ELSE IF @Mode = 'SELECTALL'
BEGIN
    DECLARE @cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = '';
    DECLARE @query NVARCHAR(MAX);

    ;WITH RankedOptions AS
    (
        SELECT 
            p.PollId,
            p.user_id,
            p.Topic,
            p.Description,
            p.ExpiryDate,
            p.AllowMultipleVotes,
			p.Audience,
            ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.PollId ORDER BY po.OptionId) AS OptionNumber,
            LTRIM(RTRIM(po.Options)) + '`' 
                + CAST(po.OptionId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '`' 
                + CAST(ISNULL(v.VoteCount, 0) AS NVARCHAR(10)) AS OptionValue,
            CASE 
                WHEN pv_user.option_ids IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0
            END AS HasVoted,
            pv_user.option_ids AS VotedOptionIds
        FROM dbo.Polls p
        JOIN dbo.poll_Options po 
            ON p.PollId = po.PollId
        OUTER APPLY 
        (
            SELECT COUNT(*) AS VoteCount
            FROM dbo.poll_voting pv
            WHERE pv.option_id = po.OptionId
        ) v
        OUTER APPLY
        (
            SELECT STRING_AGG(CAST(option_id AS NVARCHAR(10)), ',') AS option_ids
            FROM dbo.poll_voting
            WHERE poll_id = p.PollId
              AND user_id = @user_id
        ) pv_user
        WHERE p.society_id = @society_id
          AND p.ExpiryDate >= CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) 
    )
    SELECT @cols += QUOTENAME('Option' + CAST(OptionNumber AS NVARCHAR)) + ','
    FROM RankedOptions
    GROUP BY OptionNumber
    ORDER BY OptionNumber;

    SET @cols = LEFT(@cols, LEN(@cols) - 1);

    SET @query = '
    ;WITH RankedOptions AS
    (
        SELECT 
            p.PollId,
            p.user_id,
            p.Topic,
            p.Description,
            p.ExpiryDate,
            p.AllowMultipleVotes,
			p.Audience,
            ''Option'' + CAST(ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.PollId ORDER BY po.OptionId) AS NVARCHAR) AS OptionCol,
            LTRIM(RTRIM(po.Options)) + ''`'' 
                + CAST(po.OptionId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ''`'' 
                + CAST(ISNULL(v.VoteCount, 0) AS NVARCHAR(10)) AS OptionValue,
            CASE 
                WHEN pv_user.option_ids IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0
            END AS HasVoted,
            pv_user.option_ids AS VotedOptionIds
        FROM dbo.Polls p
        JOIN dbo.poll_Options po 
            ON p.PollId = po.PollId
        OUTER APPLY 
        (
            SELECT COUNT(*) AS VoteCount
            FROM dbo.poll_voting pv
            WHERE pv.option_id = po.OptionId
        ) v
        OUTER APPLY
        (
            SELECT STRING_AGG(CAST(option_id AS NVARCHAR(10)), '','') AS option_ids
            FROM dbo.poll_voting
            WHERE poll_id = p.PollId
              AND user_id = @user_id
        ) pv_user
        WHERE p.society_id = @society_id
          AND p.ExpiryDate >= CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) 
    )
    SELECT PollId, user_id, Topic, Description, ExpiryDate, AllowMultipleVotes, Audience,HasVoted, VotedOptionIds, ' + @cols + '
    FROM RankedOptions
    PIVOT
    (
        MAX(OptionValue)
        FOR OptionCol IN (' + @cols + ')
    ) p
    ';

    EXEC sp_executesql 
        @query, 
        N'@society_id NVARCHAR(50), @user_id int=0,@ownerApp int=5', 
        @society_id = @society_id,
        @user_id = @user_id,
		@ownerApp=@ownerApp;
END




ELSE IF @Mode = 'DELETEALL'
BEGIN
    BEGIN TRY
        BEGIN TRANSACTION;

        -- First delete from poll_voting (depends on poll_Options)
        DELETE pv
        FROM dbo.poll_voting pv
        INNER JOIN dbo.poll_Options po
            ON pv.option_id = po.OptionId
        INNER JOIN dbo.Polls p
            ON po.PollId = p.PollId
        WHERE p.PollId = @PollId
          AND p.user_id = @user_id;

        -- Then delete from poll_Options
        DELETE po
        FROM dbo.poll_Options po
        INNER JOIN dbo.Polls p
            ON po.PollId = p.PollId
        WHERE p.PollId = @PollId
          AND p.user_id = @user_id;

        -- Finally delete from Polls (only if it belongs to user)
        DELETE FROM dbo.Polls
        WHERE PollId = @PollId
          AND user_id = @user_id;

        COMMIT TRANSACTION;
    END TRY
    BEGIN CATCH
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
        THROW;
    END CATCH
END

		if @mode='GetPolls'
		begin
		SELECT  
    p.PollId,
    p.user_id,
    p.Topic,
    p.Description,
    p.ExpiryDate,
	p.Expiredate,  --as created data
    p.AllowMultipleVotes,
    p.OneVotePerUnit,
    p.Audience,
    p.society_id,
    COUNT(DISTINCT v.voting_id) AS total_votes
FROM polls p
LEFT JOIN poll_voting v 
    ON p.PollId = v.Poll_id 
   AND v.Society_id = p.society_id
CROSS APPLY (
    SELECT login 
    FROM userData 
    WHERE owner_id = @user_id
) u
WHERE  p.society_id = @society_id AND
    p.ExpiryDate >= CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
    AND (
        p.Audience = 1
        OR (u.login = 'Owner'   AND p.Audience = 3)
        OR (u.login = 'Rent'  AND p.Audience = 4)
        OR (u.login = 'Amember' AND p.Audience = 2)
    )
GROUP BY  
    p.PollId,
    p.user_id,
    p.Topic,
    p.Description,
    p.ExpiryDate,
	p.Expiredate, --as created data
    p.AllowMultipleVotes,
    p.OneVotePerUnit,
    p.Audience,
    p.society_id
ORDER BY p.ExpiryDate DESC;


			end


ELSE IF @Mode = 'pollVotes'
BEGIN
    SELECT 
        LTRIM(RTRIM(po.OptionId)) AS OptionId,
        po.Options AS [text],
        COUNT(pv.Voting_Id) AS votes,
        ISNULL(STRING_AGG(CAST(pv.voting_id AS NVARCHAR), ','), '') AS voting_Id,  -- voting IDs comma-separated
        CASE 
            WHEN EXISTS (
                SELECT 1
                FROM dbo.poll_voting uv
                WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM(uv.option_id)) = LTRIM(RTRIM(po.OptionId))
                  AND uv.Poll_id = po.PollId
                  AND uv.user_id = @user_id
                  AND uv.Society_id = @society_id
            ) 
            THEN CAST(1 AS BIT)
            ELSE CAST(0 AS BIT)
        END AS isSelected
    FROM dbo.poll_Options po
    LEFT JOIN dbo.poll_voting pv 
        ON LTRIM(RTRIM(po.OptionId)) = LTRIM(RTRIM(pv.option_id))
       AND po.PollId = pv.Poll_id
    WHERE po.PollId = @pollId
    GROUP BY po.OptionId, po.Options, po.PollId
    ORDER BY po.OptionId;
END






		 if @Mode='Uservotes'
		 Begin 
		   SELECT cast(option_id as varchar) as option_id
          FROM poll_voting
          WHERE user_id = @user_id AND Poll_id = @PollId AND Society_id = @society_id
		 End

END
GO
