/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_PollVoting]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:01:58 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_PollVoting]
    @Operation   NVARCHAR(20),       -- 'INSERT', 'SELECT', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'VOTEDUSERS'
    @Society_id  NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @User_Id     INT = NULL,
    @Poll_id     INT = NULL,
    @Option_id   INT = NULL,
    @User_type   NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @AllowMultipleVotes INT = NULL,
    @Voting_Id   INT = 0, -- For UPDATE/DELETE by ID
	@OneVotePerUnit INT = 0
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- INSERT (with upsert behavior)
    IF @Operation = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        -- OneVotePerUnit = 1 → Check if someone from same flat already voted
        IF @OneVotePerUnit = 1
        BEGIN
            IF EXISTS (
  SELECT 
    1
FROM poll_voting pv
INNER JOIN UserData ud_voter 
    ON pv.user_id = ud_voter.owner_id  -- the person who voted
WHERE pv.poll_id = @Poll_id
  AND ud_voter.flat_id = (
        SELECT top 1 flat_id
        FROM UserData 
        WHERE owner_id = @User_Id   -- the main owner
    )
            )
            BEGIN
                SELECT 'Someone from your flat has already voted in this poll' AS Message;
                RETURN;
            END
        END

        -- Case 1: AllowMultipleVotes = 0 → Only one vote per poll
        IF @AllowMultipleVotes = 0
        BEGIN
            IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM poll_voting WHERE User_Id = @User_Id AND Poll_id = @Poll_id)
            BEGIN
                UPDATE poll_voting
                SET Option_id = @Option_id,
                    Society_id = ISNULL(@Society_id, Society_id),
                    User_type = ISNULL(@User_type, User_type)
                WHERE User_Id = @User_Id AND Poll_id = @Poll_id;

                SELECT 'Vote updated successfully' AS Message;
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
                DECLARE @newVotingId INT = 0;

                SELECT TOP (1) @newVotingId = voting_id
                FROM poll_voting
                WHERE society_id = @Society_id
                ORDER BY voting_id DESC;

                IF @newVotingId IS NULL SET @newVotingId = 0;
                ELSE SET @newVotingId = @newVotingId + 1;

                INSERT INTO poll_voting (Voting_Id, Society_id, User_Id, Poll_id, Option_id, User_type)
                VALUES (@newVotingId, @Society_id, @User_Id, @Poll_id, @Option_id, @User_type);

                SELECT @newVotingId AS Voting_Id, 'Vote inserted successfully' AS Message;
            END
        END

        -- Case 2: AllowMultipleVotes = 1 → Multiple options allowed, but once per option
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM poll_voting WHERE User_Id = @User_Id AND Poll_id = @Poll_id AND Option_id = @Option_id)
            BEGIN
                SELECT 'Already voted for this option' AS Message;
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
                DECLARE @newVotingId2 INT = 0;

                SELECT TOP (1) @newVotingId2 = voting_id
                FROM poll_voting
                WHERE society_id = @Society_id
                ORDER BY voting_id DESC;

                IF @newVotingId2 IS NULL SET @newVotingId2 = 0;
                ELSE SET @newVotingId2 = @newVotingId2 + 1;

                INSERT INTO poll_voting (Voting_Id, Society_id, User_Id, Poll_id, Option_id, User_type)
                VALUES (@newVotingId2, @Society_id, @User_Id, @Poll_id, @Option_id, @User_type);

                SELECT @newVotingId2 AS Voting_Id, 'Vote inserted successfully' AS Message;
            END
        END
    END

    -- SELECT (All or Filtered)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'SELECT'
    BEGIN
        SELECT voting_id, Society_id, User_Id, Poll_id, Option_id, User_type
        FROM poll_voting
        WHERE (@Poll_id IS NULL OR Poll_id = @Poll_id)
          AND (@User_Id IS NULL OR User_Id = @User_Id);
    END

    -- UPDATE (by Voting_Id)
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE poll_voting
        SET Society_id = ISNULL(@Society_id, Society_id),
            Option_id  = ISNULL(@Option_id, Option_id),
            User_type  = ISNULL(@User_type, User_type)
        WHERE voting_id = @Voting_Id;

        SELECT 'Record updated successfully' AS Message;
    END

    -- DELETE
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM poll_voting
        WHERE voting_id = @Voting_Id AND user_id = @User_Id;

        SELECT 'Record deleted successfully' AS Message;
    END

    -- NEW OPERATION: Get users who voted in a poll
    ELSE IF @Operation = 'VOTEDUSERS'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM pollvotes
        WHERE Poll_id = @Poll_id;
    END

    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT 'Invalid operation. Use INSERT, SELECT, UPDATE, DELETE, or VOTEDUSERS.' AS Message;
    END
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_product]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:02:41 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_product]
    @operation NVARCHAR(50)= null,
    @active_status BIT = NULL,
    @product_name NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @description NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @price DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL,
    @warranty NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @status NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @available_quantity INT = NULL,
    @society_id nvarchar(10) = NULL,
    @owner_id INT = NULL,
	@product_id int=0,
    @contact_no NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @posted_date DATE = NULL,
    @availability NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@type nvarchar(50)=null
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @operation = 'Update'
    BEGIN
	 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = product_id
                    FROM     dbo.PRoduct_sell
                    ORDER BY product_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
        INSERT INTO Product_Sell (product_id,
            active_status, product_name, description, price, warranty, status,
            available_quantity, society_id, owner_id, contact_no, posted_date, availability,type
        )
        VALUES (
            @new,@active_status, @product_name, @description, @price, @warranty, @status,
            @available_quantity, @society_id, @owner_id, @contact_no, @posted_date, @availability,@type
        );

        SELECT @new AS product_id;
    END
	 IF @operation = 'GetProductsForSociety'
    BEGIN
	
       SELECT 
         * from product_details
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
    END
   -- Get details of a single product
    IF @operation = 'GetProductDetails'
    BEGIN
     SELECT        dbo.Product_Sell.product_id, dbo.Product_Sell.product_name, dbo.Product_Sell.description, dbo.Product_Sell.price, dbo.Product_Sell.warranty, dbo.Product_Sell.status, dbo.Product_Sell.available_quantity, 
                         dbo.Product_Sell.society_id, STRING_AGG(dbo.Product_Images.image_path, ',') AS image_path, dbo.Product_Sell.contact_no, dbo.Product_Sell.owner_id, dbo.Product_Sell.posted_date, 
                         CASE WHEN dbo.Product_Sell.availability = 1 THEN 'WhatsApp' WHEN dbo.Product_Sell.availability = 2 THEN 'Call' ELSE 'WhatsApp & Call' END AS availability_name, dbo.Product_Sell.availability, 
                         dbo.UserData.name AS owner_name
FROM            dbo.UserData INNER JOIN
                         dbo.Product_Sell ON dbo.UserData.owner_id = dbo.Product_Sell.owner_id AND dbo.UserData.login = dbo.Product_Sell.type RIGHT OUTER JOIN
                         dbo.Product_Images ON dbo.Product_Sell.product_id = dbo.Product_Images.product_id
						 where Product_sell.product_id=@product_id
GROUP BY dbo.Product_Sell.product_id, dbo.Product_Sell.product_name, dbo.Product_Sell.description, dbo.Product_Sell.price, dbo.Product_Sell.warranty, dbo.Product_Sell.status, dbo.Product_Sell.available_quantity, 
                         dbo.Product_Sell.society_id, dbo.Product_Sell.contact_no, dbo.Product_Sell.owner_id, dbo.Product_Sell.posted_date, dbo.Product_Sell.availability, dbo.UserData.name 
    END

    -- Get all products by an owner within a society
    --ELSE IF @operation = 'GetOwnerProducts'
    --BEGIN
    --    SELECT 
    --     * from product_details
    --    WHERE active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
    --      AND owner_id = @owner_id;
    --END
	ELSE IF @operation = 'GetOwnerProducts'
BEGIN
    SELECT pd.*
    FROM product_details pd
    WHERE pd.active_status = 0
      AND pd.society_id = @society_id
      AND pd.owner_id IN (
          SELECT ud2.owner_id
          FROM dbo.UserData ud1
          JOIN dbo.UserData ud2 ON ud2.flat_id = ud1.flat_id
          WHERE ud1.owner_id = @owner_id
      )
END


	if @operation='ProductUpdate'
	 BEGIN
		update Product_Sell set product_name=@product_name,description=@description,price=@price,available_quantity=@available_quantity,contact_no=@contact_no where product_id=@product_id and owner_id=@owner_id
END
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_request]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:03:08 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_request]
@operation nvarchar(10)=null,
@req_id int=0,
@req_date smalldatetime=Null,
@reply_date smalldatetime=Null,
@request nvarchar(250)=null,
@request_by int=0,
@reply_by int=0,
@reply nvarchar(250)=Null
AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @req_id=0
	       Begin
		   Declare @new int 
		      Select TOP(1) @new=req_id from dbo.request Order By req_id DESC
		        If @new IS Null Or @new=0
		             Select @new=1
				 Else
				     Select @new=@new+1

		    INSERT INTO dbo.request(req_id,request,reply,req_date,reply_date,request_by,reply_by)values(@new,@request,@reply,@req_date,@reply_date,@request_by,@reply_by)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.request set request=@request,reply=@reply,req_date=@req_date,reply_date=@reply_date where req_id=@req_id
		   End
	End

	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT       req_date,reply_date,request,reply
          FROM          request
		  where         req_id=@req_id
    	End

		If @operation='Select_All'
	   Begin
	      SELECT  * FROM  request
		 
    	End

	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Delete from dbo.request where req_id=@req_id
    	End

    If @operation='request_already_exist'
	   Begin
	    If @req_id<>0
		Select * from dbo.request Where request=@request AND req_id<>@req_id AND reply=@reply
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.request Where request=@request  AND reply=@reply
	   End

END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_search]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:03:25 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_search]
@query NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL

    EXECUTE (@query);
END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_servent_maid_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:03:46 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_servent_maid_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@servent_id INT=0,
@s_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@s_address_1 NVARCHAR (250)=NULL, 
@s_address_2 NVARCHAR (250)=NULL, 
@mobile_no1 NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, 
@mobile_no2 NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, 
@cloth_wash INT=0, 
@cloth_wash_charge FLOAT=0, 
@b_wash INT=0, 
@b_wash_charge FLOAT=0,
@f_wash INT=0, 
@f_wash_charge FLOAT=0, 
@baby_set INT=0, 
@b_set_charge FLOAT=0, 
@meal INT=0, 
@meal_charge FLOAT=0, 
@remark NVARCHAR (500)=NULL, 
@para NVARCHAR (100)=NULL, 
@society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@p_type_id int = 0,
@flat_id int =0,
@usefull_contact_id int = 0,
@contact_no nvarchar(15) = null,
@p_name nvarchar(200) = null


AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @servent_id = 0
                BEGIN
                    DECLARE @new AS INT;
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = servent_id
                    FROM     dbo.servent_maid_master
                    ORDER BY servent_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                       OR @new = 0
                        SELECT @new = 1;
                    ELSE
                        SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.servent_maid_master (servent_id, s_name, s_address_1, s_address_2, mobile_no1, mobile_no2, cloth_wash, cloth_wash_charge, b_wash, b_wash_charge, f_wash, f_wash_charge, baby_set, b_set_charge, meal, meal_charge, remark, society_id,active_status)
                    VALUES                              (@new, dbo.InitCap(@s_name), dbo.InitCap(@s_address_1), dbo.InitCap(@s_address_2), @mobile_no1, @mobile_no2, @cloth_wash, @cloth_wash_charge, @b_wash, @b_wash_charge, @f_wash, @f_wash_charge, @baby_set, @b_set_charge, @meal, @meal_charge, dbo.InitCap(@remark), @society_id,0);
			
					return @new 
				END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.servent_maid_master
                    SET    s_name            = dbo.InitCap(@s_name),
                           s_address_1       = dbo.InitCap(@s_address_1),
                           s_address_2       = dbo.InitCap(@s_address_2),
                           mobile_no1        = @mobile_no1,
                           mobile_no2        = @mobile_no2,
                           cloth_wash        = @cloth_wash,
                           cloth_wash_charge = @cloth_wash_charge,
                           b_wash            = @b_wash,
                           b_wash_charge     = @b_wash_charge,
                           f_wash            = @f_wash,
                           f_wash_charge     = @f_wash_charge,
                           baby_set          = @baby_set,
                           b_set_charge      = @b_set_charge,
                           meal              = @meal,
                           meal_charge       = @meal_charge,
                           remark            = dbo.InitCap(@remark),
                           society_id        = @society_id
                    WHERE  servent_id = @servent_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master
            WHERE  servent_id = @servent_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select_ALL'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master where active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.servent_maid_master set active_status=1
            WHERE  servent_id = @servent_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_delete'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master
            WHERE  servent_id = @servent_id and active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_s_name_exists'
        BEGIN
            IF @servent_id <> 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master
                WHERE  s_name = @s_name and active_status=0
                       AND servent_id <> @servent_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master
                WHERE  s_name = @s_name and active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'gridview_select'
        BEGIN
            IF @para IS NULL
                BEGIN
                    SELECT *
                    FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master where active_status=0;
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    SELECT CONCAT(' * from servent_maid_master', @para);
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_no'
        BEGIN
            IF @servent_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master
                WHERE  mobile_no1 = @mobile_no1 and active_status=0;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.servent_maid_master
                WHERE  mobile_no1 = @mobile_no1
                       AND servent_id = @servent_id and active_status=0
                       AND servent_id <> @servent_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM  servent_maid_master
            WHERE   active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY servent_id DESC;
        END
		IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM  servent_maid_master
            WHERE   active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and( s_name like  @search+'%' or s_address_1 like  @search+'%' or  mobile_no1 like  @search+'%')
            ORDER BY servent_id DESC;
        END
		  IF @operation = 'InsertHelper'
        BEGIN
          
		     exec  @usefull_contact_id= sp_usefull_contact @operation='Update', @p_name=@p_name,@contact_no=@contact_no, @p_type = @p_type_id,@society_id=@society_id -- @ID=@ID OUTPUT;
			 
		    exec sp_usefull_contact @operation ='AssignToFlat' ,@usefull_contact_id=@usefull_contact_id, @flat_id=@flat_id,@society_id=@society_id
		 
        END

		
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_SettleMaintenancePayment]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:04:04 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_SettleMaintenancePayment]
    @ReceiptID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE 
        @FlatID INT,
        @SocietyID NVARCHAR(10),
        @PaidAmount DECIMAL(10,2),
        @Remaining DECIMAL(10,2),
        @BillList NVARCHAR(MAX);

    SELECT 
        @FlatID = flat_id,
        @SocietyID = society_id,
        @PaidAmount = paid_amount,
        @BillList = bill_details
    FROM dbo.receipt
    WHERE receipt_id = @ReceiptID;

    IF @FlatID IS NULL OR @BillList IS NULL
    BEGIN
        RAISERROR('Invalid Receipt ID or Missing Bill Details.', 16, 1);
        RETURN;
    END

    SET @Remaining = @PaidAmount;
    PRINT 'Settlement started for Flat ID: ' + CAST(@FlatID AS NVARCHAR(10));

    DECLARE @BillTable TABLE (BillNo NVARCHAR(50));

    INSERT INTO @BillTable (BillNo)
    SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(value))
    FROM STRING_SPLIT(@BillList, ',');

    -- Cursor now uses TOTAL due (principal + interest) for comparison
    DECLARE @MaintenanceID INT, @Due DECIMAL(10,2), @Interest DECIMAL(10,2), @TotalDue DECIMAL(10,2);

    DECLARE bill_cursor CURSOR FOR
        SELECT maintenance_id, ISNULL(due, 0), ISNULL(tax_interest_amt, 0)
        FROM dbo.maintenance_cal
        WHERE flat_id = @FlatID
          AND CAST(bill_no AS NVARCHAR(50)) IN (SELECT BillNo FROM @BillTable)
        ORDER BY due_date ASC;

    OPEN bill_cursor;
    FETCH NEXT FROM bill_cursor INTO @MaintenanceID, @Due, @Interest;

    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 AND @Remaining > 0
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @Applied DECIMAL(10,2), @InterestApplied DECIMAL(10,2), @PrincipalApplied DECIMAL(10,2);
        SET @TotalDue = @Due + @Interest;

        IF @Remaining >= @TotalDue
        BEGIN
            -- Full settlement (principal + interest both cleared)
            SET @Applied = @TotalDue;
            SET @Remaining = @Remaining - @TotalDue;

         UPDATE dbo.maintenance_cal
SET due = 0,
    advance = 0,
    status = 3
WHERE maintenance_id = @MaintenanceID;


            PRINT 'Bill ID ' + CAST(@MaintenanceID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' fully settled (incl. interest). Amount: ' + CAST(@Applied AS NVARCHAR(20));
        END
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            -- Partial settlement: interest paid off first, then principal
            SET @Applied = @Remaining;

            IF @Applied >= @Interest
            BEGIN
                SET @InterestApplied = @Interest;
                SET @PrincipalApplied = @Applied - @Interest;
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
                SET @InterestApplied = @Applied;
                SET @PrincipalApplied = 0;
            END

            SET @Remaining = 0;

            UPDATE dbo.maintenance_cal
            SET due = due - @PrincipalApplied,
                tax_interest_amt = tax_interest_amt - @InterestApplied,
                status = 5   -- partially paid
            WHERE maintenance_id = @MaintenanceID;

            PRINT 'Bill ID ' + CAST(@MaintenanceID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' partially settled. Amount: ' + CAST(@Applied AS NVARCHAR(20));
        END

        FETCH NEXT FROM bill_cursor INTO @MaintenanceID, @Due, @Interest;
    END

    CLOSE bill_cursor;
    DEALLOCATE bill_cursor;

    IF @Remaining > 0
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.maintenance_cal
        SET advance = ISNULL(advance, 0) + @Remaining
        WHERE flat_id = @FlatID
          AND maintenance_id = (
                SELECT TOP 1 maintenance_id 
                FROM dbo.maintenance_cal 
                WHERE flat_id = @FlatID
                ORDER BY due_date DESC
          );

        PRINT 'Advance of ' + CAST(@Remaining AS NVARCHAR(20)) + ' carried forward.';
    END

    PRINT 'Settlement completed successfully for Receipt ID: ' + CAST(@ReceiptID AS NVARCHAR(10));
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_SettleVendorBills]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:04:21 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_SettleVendorBills]
    @PaymentID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE 
        @VendorID INT,
        @SocietyID NVARCHAR(10),
        @PaidAmount DECIMAL(18,2),
        @Remaining DECIMAL(18,2),
        @BillAmounts NVARCHAR(MAX),
        @BillDetails NVARCHAR(MAX);

    -- 1️⃣ Fetch payment info
    SELECT 
        @VendorID = vendor_id,
        @SocietyID = society_id,
        @PaidAmount = paid_amount,
        @BillDetails = bill_details
    FROM dbo.vendor_bill_payments
    WHERE payment_id = @PaymentID;

    IF @VendorID IS NULL
    BEGIN
        RAISERROR('Invalid Payment ID or Vendor not found.', 16, 1);
        RETURN;
    END

    IF ISNULL(@BillDetails, '') = ''
    BEGIN
        RAISERROR('No bill details found for this payment.', 16, 1);
        RETURN;
    END

    SET @Remaining = @PaidAmount;

    PRINT 'Settlement started for Bill ID: ' + @BillDetails;
    PRINT 'Vendor ID: ' + CAST(@VendorID AS NVARCHAR(10));
    PRINT 'Total Paid Amount: ' + CAST(@PaidAmount AS NVARCHAR(20));

    -- 2️⃣ Get the bill's current due amount
    DECLARE @CurrentDue DECIMAL(18,2);
    
    SELECT @CurrentDue = ISNULL(due, 0)
    FROM dbo.vendor_bills
    WHERE bill_id = CAST(@BillDetails AS INT);

    IF @CurrentDue IS NULL OR @CurrentDue <= 0
    BEGIN
        PRINT 'Bill ID ' + @BillDetails + ' has no due amount or already paid.';
        RETURN;
    END

    PRINT 'Current Due Amount: ' + CAST(@CurrentDue AS NVARCHAR(20));

    -- 3️⃣ Settle the payment
    DECLARE @Applied DECIMAL(18,2);

    IF @PaidAmount >= @CurrentDue
    BEGIN
        -- ✅ Full settlement
        SET @Applied = @CurrentDue;
        SET @Remaining = @PaidAmount - @CurrentDue;

        UPDATE dbo.vendor_bills
        SET due = 0,
            status = 3, -- Paid
            updated_date = GETDATE()
        WHERE bill_id = CAST(@BillDetails AS INT);

        SET @BillAmounts = CAST(@Applied AS NVARCHAR(20));

        PRINT 'Bill ID ' + @BillDetails + ' fully settled. Amount: ' + CAST(@Applied AS NVARCHAR(20));
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        -- ⚠️ Partial settlement
        SET @Applied = @PaidAmount;

        UPDATE dbo.vendor_bills
        SET due = due - @Applied,
            status = 5, -- Partial
            updated_date = GETDATE()
        WHERE bill_id = CAST(@BillDetails AS INT);

        SET @Remaining = 0;
        SET @BillAmounts = CAST(@Applied AS NVARCHAR(20));

        PRINT 'Bill ID ' + @BillDetails + ' partially settled. Amount: ' + CAST(@Applied AS NVARCHAR(20));
    END

    -- 4️⃣ Handle advance amount (if any remains)
    IF @Remaining > 0
    BEGIN
        PRINT 'Advance of ' + CAST(@Remaining AS NVARCHAR(20)) + 
              ' remains unadjusted for Vendor ID: ' + CAST(@VendorID AS NVARCHAR(10));
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        PRINT 'All paid amount has been fully adjusted.';
    END

    -- 5️⃣ Update payment record with bill_paid_amount
    UPDATE vendor_bill_payments 
    SET bill_paid_amount = @BillAmounts 
    WHERE payment_id = @PaymentID;

    PRINT 'Settlement completed successfully for Payment ID: ' + CAST(@PaymentID AS NVARCHAR(10));
END;
GO

CREATE procedure [dbo].[sp_shop_maintenance]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@shop_maint_id int=0,
@mrep_no nvarchar(200)=null,
@m_date smalldatetime=null,
@led_id int=0,
@other_details nvarchar(MAX)=null,
@amt int=0,
@pay_method nvarchar(50)=null,
@cheq_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@cheq_date smalldatetime=null,
@active_status int=0,
@society_id nvarchar(50)=null,
@search  nvarchar(50)=null



AS
BEGIN

    If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	If @shop_maint_id=0
	Begin
	 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = shop_maint_id
                    FROM     dbo.shop_maintenance
                    ORDER BY shop_maint_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END



	     Insert into shop_maintenance(shop_maint_id,society_id,mrep_no,m_date,led_id,other_details,amt,pay_method,cheq_no,cheq_date,active_status)values(@new,@society_id,@mrep_no,@m_date,@led_id,@other_details,@amt,@pay_method,@cheq_no,@cheq_date,0)
		 end
    else

	     Update shop_maintenance set society_id=@society_id,mrep_no=@mrep_no,m_date=@m_date,led_id=@led_id,other_details=@other_details,amt=@amt,pay_method=@pay_method,cheq_no=@cheq_no,cheq_date=@cheq_date where shop_maint_id=@shop_maint_id
	End
     
	 If @operation='Delete'
	Begin
	  
	Update shop_vw set active_status=1 where shop_maint_id=@shop_maint_id

	End
		If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	     select * from shop_vw where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id  ORDER BY shop_maint_id DESC
	   End
	 If @operation='Select'
	Begin
	  
	  Select * from shop_maintenance where shop_maint_id=@shop_maint_id

	End

	If @operation='check_no'
	   Begin
	    If @shop_maint_id=0
		Select * from dbo.shop_maintenance Where mrep_no=@mrep_no and active_status=0
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.shop_maintenance Where mrep_no=@mrep_no AND  shop_maint_id=@shop_maint_id and active_status=0 AND shop_maint_id<>@shop_maint_id
	   End
	   
	   If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	     select * from shop_vw where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id and( led_description like @search+'%' or mrep_no like @search+'%' or cast (m_date as varchar) like @search+'%')  ORDER BY shop_maint_id DESC
	   End

	   If @operation='fill_list'
	   begin
	   SELECT  * FROM  ledger where society_id =@society_id
	   end

END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_society_charges]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:05:07 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_society_charges]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL, 
@charge_id INT=0, 
@amount NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@total_unit int=0
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @charge_id = 0
                BEGIN
                    DECLARE @new AS INT;
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = charge_id
                    FROM     dbo.society_charges
                    ORDER BY charge_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                       OR @new = 0
                        SELECT @new = 1;
                    ELSE
                        SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.society_charges (charge_id,amount, society_id,total_unit,active_status)
                    VALUES                      (@new, @amount, @society_id,@total_unit,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.society_charges
                    SET   
                           amount       = @amount,
                           society_id = @society_id,
						   total_unit=@total_unit
                    WHERE  charge_id = @charge_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT dbo.society_charges.amount,
                   dbo.society_charges.charge_id,
                   dbo.society_charges.society_id
            FROM   dbo.Society_Charges
                   
            WHERE  charge_id = @charge_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
           Update dbo.society_charges set active_status=1
            WHERE  charge_id = @charge_id;
        END
    
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   name,amount,charge_id
            FROM   society_charges inner join society_master on society_charges.society_id=society_master.society_id and dbo.society_charges.active_status=0
          
            ORDER BY charge_id DESC;
        END
      IF @operation = 'society_exist'
        BEGIN
           IF @charge_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.Society_Charges
                WHERE  society_id=@society_id and active_status=0;
                       
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.Society_Charges
                WHERE  society_id=@society_id and active_status=0
                       AND charge_id = @charge_id
                       AND charge_id <> @charge_id;
        END


		if @operation = 'fill_society'
		begin
		Select *  from society_master
		end

   
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_society_charges_monthwise]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:05:23 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO








CREATE procedure [dbo].[sp_society_charges_monthwise]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@mon_charge_id int=0,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@amount DECIMAL(18, 2)=0,
@pending_amount DECIMAL(18, 2)=0,
@date SMALLDATETIME=NULL

AS
BEGIN

   If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @mon_charge_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = mon_charge_id
                    FROM     dbo.MonthwiseCharges
                    ORDER BY mon_charge_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO dbo.MonthwiseCharges(mon_charge_id,society_id,amount,date,active_status,advance)values(@new,@society_id,@amount,getdate(),0,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.MonthwiseCharges set society_id=@society_id,amount=@amount,date=@date where mon_charge_id=@mon_charge_id
		   End
	End

	 If @operation='Delete'
	Begin
	  
	  Update MonthwiseCharges set active_status=1 where mon_charge_id=@mon_charge_id
	  
	  End

		If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.MonthwiseCharges where mon_charge_id=@mon_charge_id
    	End

    IF @operation = 'Charges_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     MonthwiseCharges
            WHERE   active_status=0 and amount!=0
            ORDER BY mon_charge_id DESC;
        END

		   IF @operation = 'Remaining_due'
        BEGIN
	SELECT        ISNULL(SUM(amount), 0) AS due,
                             (SELECT        amount *
                             (SELECT        COUNT(flat_id) AS Expr1
                               FROM            dbo.flat_master
                               WHERE        (society_id = @society_id) AND (active_status = 0)) AS Expr1
FROM            dbo.Society_Charges
WHERE        (society_id = @society_id)) AS amount
FROM            dbo.MonthwiseCharges AS MonthwiseCharges_1
WHERE        (date < CAST(GETDATE() AS date)) AND (society_id = @society_id)
        END

		 --  IF @operation = 'Total_Amount'
   --     BEGIN
		 --select amount *(select count(flat_id)from UserData) as total from society_charges where society_id = @society_id
		 --END
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_society_expense]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:05:41 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_society_expense]
@operation nvarchar(20)=null,
@expense_id int=0,
@user_id int=0,
@invoice_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@date smalldatetime =Null,
@ex_type nvarchar(50)=Null,
@ex_name nvarchar(50)=Null,
@ex_details nvarchar(MAX)=null,
@comments nvarchar(MAX)=null,
@add_maintanance nvarchar(50)=Null,
@amount float=0,
@tax float=0,
@tds float=0,
@f_amount float=0,
@society_id nvarchar(50)=null,
@status int=0,
@regular int=0,
@build_id nvarchar(50)=null,
@search nvarchar(50)=null,
@bill_type int=0

AS
BEGIN
	
		If @operation='Update'
		Begin
			 if @expense_id=0
			   Begin
				DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
						SELECT   TOP (1) @new = expense_id
						FROM     dbo.society_expense
						ORDER BY expense_id DESC;
						IF @new IS NULL
							BEGIN
								SELECT @new = 0;
							END
						ELSE
							BEGIN
								SELECT @new = @new + 1;
							END
			   DECLARE @LastInvoiceNumber AS VARCHAR (20);
				DECLARE @NewInvoiceNumber AS VARCHAR (20);
					declare @num int
				 Select TOP(1)  @NewInvoiceNumber =invoice_no FROM dbo.society_expense  ORDER BY invoice_no DESC
					If @NewInvoiceNumber IS NULL 
						 begin
							 Select @NewInvoiceNumber='EXP0001'
					
						 end
					 Else
						 Begin

				SELECT   @LastInvoiceNumber = invoice_no
				FROM     society_expense
				ORDER BY invoice_no DESC;
				SET @NewInvoiceNumber = CAST (SUBSTRING(@LastInvoiceNumber, 4, 8) AS VARCHAR) + 1;
				SET @NewInvoiceNumber = REPLACE(@LastInvoiceNumber, @NewInvoiceNumber - 1, @NewInvoiceNumber);
			   if   len(@NewInVoiceNumber)>7
				 SET @NewInvoiceNumber = REPLACE(@NewInvoiceNumber,'EXP0','EXP');
				End
				INSERT INTO dbo.society_expense(expense_id,invoice_no,date,ex_type,ex_name,ex_details,comments,amount,tax,tds,f_amount,society_id,status,regular,active_status)values(@new,@NewInvoiceNumber,@date,dbo.InitCap(@ex_type),dbo.InitCap(@ex_name),@ex_details,@comments,@amount,@tax,@tds,@f_amount,@society_id,0, @regular,0)
			  select top 1 expense_id from society_expense order by expense_id desc
			  select @new as expense_id 
			  End
			 Else
			   Begin
			   Update dbo.society_expense set invoice_no=@invoice_no,date=@date,ex_type=@ex_type,ex_name=dbo.InitCap(@ex_name),ex_details=@ex_details,comments=@comments,amount=@amount,tax=@tax,tds=@tds,f_amount=@f_amount,society_id=@society_id,regular=@regular where expense_id=@expense_id
			   End
		End
		If @operation='Select'
		   Begin
	   SELECT* from dbo.society_expense where expense_id=@expense_id
    		End

		If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update dbo.society_expense set active_status=1 where expense_id=@expense_id
    	End
	   If @operation='exfetch'
	   Begin
		 select charges from maintenance_charges where charges_type=1 and society_id=@society_id and  cast(created_at as Date) between  dateadd(dd,-(day(getdate())-1),getdate()) 
	  and dateadd(s,-1,dateadd(mm,datediff(m,0,getdate())+1,0))
  
	 end
		If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	select * from expense_vw where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id ORDER BY expense_id DESC  
    	End
			If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	select * from expense_vw where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and(  ex_details like @search +'%'or ex_name like @search +'%' or cast(f_amount as varchar) like @search +'%' or expense_status like @search +'%' or cast (date as varchar)like @search +'%' ) ORDER BY expense_id DESC  
    	End
		If @operation='invoice_fetch'
	  Begin
	   Select invoice_no from society_expense where society_id= @society_id ORDER BY invoice_no DESC
    	End
			If @operation='add_approver'
	  Begin
	   Select user_id,name,'2' as status,getdate() as date,'add' as type from UserLogin where user_id!=@user_id and active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id
    	End

		If @operation='reg_exp'
	  Begin
	 
	  select * from society_expense_vw where regular=1 and society_id=@society_id
	  
	 --  select * from society_expense_vw where regular=0 and society_id=@society_id
    	End
			If @operation='GetAllExpense'
	  Begin
	 if @user_id=0
	    select distinct invoice_no,date,ex_details,comments,f_amount,society_id,status,  approval_count from society_expense_vw  where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id
   else
		select * from society_expense_vw where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and user_id=@user_id
    	End


		if @operation = 'vendor_fill'
		begin
		Select vendor_id,vendor_name from vendor_master where society_id=@society_id
		end

		if @operation = 'build_fill'
		begin
		Select build_id,(name + w_name) as name from global_society_view where society_id=@society_id
		end

END

GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_society_master]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@society_master_id int=0,
@name nvarchar(250)=null,
@establish_date date=Null,
@registration_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@off_address1 nvarchar(150)=null,
@off_address2 nvarchar(150)=null,
@contact_no1 nvarchar(50)=null,
@email nvarchar(150)=null,
@para nvarchar(100)=Null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@city nvarchar (50)=Null,
@state_id int=0,
@pincode nvarchar(50)=Null,
@tan_no nvarchar(50)=Null,
@gstin_no nvarchar(50)=Null,
@pan_no nvarchar(50)=Null,
@home_no int=0,
@division_id int=0,
@district_id int=0,
@type nvarchar(10)=null,
@village_id nvarchar(50)=Null,
@enrolment_date datetime=Null,
@termination_date datetime=Null,
@house_no int=0,
@house_type nvarchar(50)=Null,
@sq_ft decimal(18,2) = NULL,
@address nvarchar(50)=Null,
@pre_mob int=0,
@status int=0,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@rate_per_sqft decimal = null,
@two_w_rate decimal = null,
@four_w_rate decimal = null,
@auto_bill_generation bit = false,
@bill_gen_date int = 0,
@bill_due_period int  = 0,
@terms text =NULL

AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	
		   Update dbo.society_master set name=dbo.InitCap(@name),establish_date=@establish_date,registration_no=@registration_no,off_address1=dbo.InitCap(@off_address1),off_address2=dbo.InitCap(@off_address2),contact_no1=@contact_no1,
		   email=@email,society_id=@society_id,city=dbo.InitCap(@city),state_id=@state_id,pincode=@pincode,tan_no=@tan_no,
		   gstin_no=@gstin_no,pan_no=@pan_no,home_no=@home_no,division_id=@division_id,district_id=@district_id ,
		   enrolment_date=getdate(),status=0,
		   active_status=0 where society_id=@society_id
		if @type='New'
		begin
		exec sp_terms_condition @operation='Update', @terms=@terms, @society_id=@society_id
		exec sp_account_setting @operation='Insert',  @society_id=@society_id,@rate_per_sqf=@rate_per_sqft,@two_w_rate=@two_w_rate,@four_w_rate=@four_w_rate,
		@auto_bill_generation=@auto_bill_generation,@bill_gen_date=@bill_gen_date,@bill_due_period=@bill_due_period
		end
		
		
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.society_master where society_master_id=@society_master_id         
    	End
		If @operation='TotalFlats'
	   Begin
	     SELECT count(*) as flats from dbo.flat_master where society_id=@society_id  and active_status=0        
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update dbo.society_master set active_status=1 where society_master_id=@society_master_id
    	End

	   If @operation='check_no'
	   Begin
	    If @society_master_id=0
		Select * from dbo.society_master Where registration_no=@registration_no and active_status=0 AND lower(trim(name))=lower(trim(@name))
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.society_master Where registration_no=@registration_no AND active_status=0 and society_master_id=@society_master_id AND society_master_id<>@society_master_id
	   End

	   
	   If @operation='check_id'
	   Begin
	    If @society_master_id=0
		Select * from dbo.society_master Where society_id=@society_id and active_status=0
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.society_master Where society_id=@society_id AND society_master_id=@society_master_id and active_status=0 AND society_master_id<>@society_master_id
	   End

	   If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	   Select * from society_master where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id ORDER BY society_master_id DESC
	   End
	    If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	   Select * from society_master where active_status=0 and society_id=@society_id and( name like @search+'%' or registration_no like @search+'%' or off_address1 like @search+'%' or contact_no1 like @search+'%') ORDER BY society_master_id DESC
	   End
	 If @operation='SearchSociety'
	   Begin
			  SELECT DISTINCT 
        society_name, 
        society_id, 
        build_wing, 
        build_wing_id,
        building_address,
        flat_no,
        flat_id
    FROM customer_flat;
	End	
	 
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_SocietyReceipt]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:06:43 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_SocietyReceipt]
    @operation NVARCHAR(20),           -- Options: INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, SELECT
    @receipt_id INT =0,
    @date DATE = NULL,
    @total_amount DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @bill_no INT = 0,
    @paymode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @paid_amount DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @operation = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
	if @receipt_id = 0 
	BEGIN		
	 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = staff_id
                    FROM     dbo.staff_master
                    ORDER BY staff_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
        INSERT INTO society_receipt
        (receipt_id, date, total_amount, society_id, bill_no, paymode, paid_amount)
        VALUES
        (@new, GETDATE(), @total_amount, @society_id, @new, @paymode, @paid_amount);
		exec [sp_SocietyReceipt] @operation='ClearDue' ,@paid_amount=@paid_amount, @society_id=@society_id
    END
	else

    BEGIN
        UPDATE society_receipt
        SET 
            date = @date,
            total_amount = @total_amount,
            society_id = @society_id,
            bill_no = @bill_no,
            paymode = @paymode,
            paid_amount = @paid_amount
        WHERE receipt_id = @receipt_id;
    END 
	END
    ELSE IF @operation = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM society_receipt
        WHERE receipt_id = @receipt_id;
    END
    ELSE IF @operation = 'SELECT'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * FROM society_receipt
        WHERE receipt_id = @receipt_id;
    END
	 ELSE IF @operation = 'show_history'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * FROM society_receipt where society_id=@society_id
       
    END
    ELSE IF @operation = 'ClearDue'
    BEGIN
       Declare @balance decimal
	declare @charge_id int
		Declare @total_due decimal   
		Declare @due decimal
		Declare @bill_date date
	
		 Declare insert_cur  Cursor 
		 for   select mon_charge_id,amount,date from MonthwiseCharges  WHERE society_id=@society_id   order by mon_charge_id asc
		 OPEN insert_cur   FETCH NEXT FROM insert_cur INTO @charge_id, @due,@bill_date
		   while(@@FETCH_STATUS=0 )
				 BEGIN 
				 Set @balance=@paid_amount-abs(@due)
				  if @balance >0 
					begin
					 update MonthwiseCharges set amount=0 where mon_charge_id=@charge_id
					 set @paid_amount=abs(@balance)
					end
				  else if @balance <= 0
					begin
					 update MonthwiseCharges set amount=abs(@balance) where mon_charge_id=@charge_id
					 break
					end
				FETCH NEXT FROM insert_cur INTO @charge_id, @due,@bill_date
				 END
				 CLOSE insert_cur
				 DEALLOCATE insert_cur
        select @total_due= sum(amount)FROM  dbo.MonthwiseCharges WHERE society_id=@society_id
		
		 if  @total_due=0 
		 begin
		
			 update MonthwiseCharges set advance=@balance where mon_charge_id=@charge_id
			 end
    END
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_square_ft_rate]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:07:01 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE procedure [dbo].[sp_square_ft_rate]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@sq_rate_id int=0,
@rate decimal(18,2)=0,
@applied_date date=null,
@village_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@house_no int=0,
@house_type_id nvarchar(50)=null,
@bill_gen_date date=null,
@due_date date=null,
@active_status int=0,
@status int=0

AS
BEGIN

    If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	If @sq_rate_id=0
	begin
	 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = sq_rate_id
                    FROM     dbo.square_ft_rate
                    ORDER BY sq_rate_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END




	     Insert into square_ft_rate(sq_rate_id,rate,applied_date,village_id,house_type_id,active_status,status,bill_gen_date,due_date)
		 values(@new,@rate,@applied_date,@village_id,@house_type_id,0,0,@bill_gen_date,@due_date)
		
		end
    else
	begin

	     Update square_ft_rate set rate=@rate,applied_date=@applied_date,village_id=@village_id,house_type_id=@house_type_id,bill_gen_date=@bill_gen_date,due_date=@due_date where sq_rate_id=@sq_rate_id
		 End
     end

	 If @operation='Delete'
	Begin
	  
	 Update square_ft_rate set active_status=1 where sq_rate_id=@sq_rate_id 
	
	End

	 If @operation='Select'
	Begin
	  
	  Select * from square_ft_rate where sq_rate_id=@sq_rate_id

	End

     If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
SELECT        dbo.square_ft_rate.sq_rate_id, dbo.square_ft_rate.rate, dbo.house_type.house_type, dbo.square_ft_rate.applied_date
FROM            dbo.square_ft_rate INNER JOIN
                         dbo.house_type ON dbo.square_ft_rate.house_type_id = dbo.house_type.house_type_id where active_status=0 and village_id=@village_id ORDER BY sq_rate_id DESC
	   End

END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_staff_attendance]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:07:18 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_staff_attendance]
    @operation NVARCHAR(100),
    @attendance_id INT = 0,
    @staff_id INT =0,
    @in_date DATE = NULL,
    @in_time TIME = NULL,
    @out_date DATE = NULL,
    @out_time TIME = NULL,
	@status INT = NULL,
	@society_id NVARCHAR(50) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

	--Status 0 for OUT, 1 for IN/Present, 2 for Absent, 3 for Leave
    -- ================= CHECK IN =================
    IF @operation = 'CheckIn'
    BEGIN
        IF EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM StaffAttendance
            WHERE staff_id = @staff_id
              AND out_time IS NULL
        )
        BEGIN
            RAISERROR('Staff already checked in', 16, 1);
            RETURN;
        END

        INSERT INTO StaffAttendance (staff_id, in_date, in_time)
        VALUES (@staff_id, @in_date, @in_time);

        RETURN;
    END

    -- ================= CHECK OUT =================
    IF @operation = 'CheckOut'
    BEGIN
        IF NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM StaffAttendance
            WHERE attendance_id = @attendance_id
              AND staff_id = @staff_id
              AND out_time IS NULL
        )
        BEGIN
            RAISERROR('Invalid attendance for this staff', 16, 1);
            RETURN;
        END

        UPDATE StaffAttendance
        SET out_date = @out_date,
            out_time = @out_time
        WHERE attendance_id = @attendance_id
          AND staff_id = @staff_id;

        RETURN;
    END

    -- ================= SHOW =================
    IF @operation = 'Show'
    BEGIN
        SELECT status
        FROM StaffAttendance
        WHERE (@staff_id = 0 OR staff_id = @staff_id)
        ORDER BY attendance_id DESC;
    END

--status 1 = Active and status 0 = checked out
	IF @operation = 'CheckInOut'
BEGIN
    DECLARE @AttendanceId INT;
    DECLARE @InDate DATE;
    DECLARE @InTime TIME;
    DECLARE @CheckInDateTime DATETIME;
    DECLARE @WorkingSeconds INT;


    /* ==================================
       GET LATEST OPEN CHECK-IN
       ================================== */
    SELECT TOP 1
        @AttendanceId = attendance_id,
        @InDate = in_date,
        @InTime = in_time
    FROM StaffAttendance
    WHERE staff_id = @staff_id
      AND status = 1        -- only open check-in
      AND out_date IS NULL
    ORDER BY in_date DESC, in_time DESC;

    /* =========================
       CASE 1 : CHECK-IN
       ========================= */
    IF @status = 1
    BEGIN
        -- Prevent double check-in
        IF @AttendanceId IS NOT NULL
        BEGIN
            SELECT 
                'ALREADY CHECKED-IN' AS Action,
                NULL AS CheckInDateTime,
                NULL AS CheckOutDateTime,
                NULL AS WorkingHours;
            RETURN;
        END

        INSERT INTO StaffAttendance
        (
            staff_id,
            in_time,
            in_date,
            status
        )
        VALUES
        (
            @staff_id,
            CAST(GETDATE() AS TIME),
            CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE),
            1
        );

        SELECT 
            'CHECK-IN' AS Action,
            GETDATE() AS CheckInDateTime,
            NULL AS CheckOutDateTime,
            NULL AS WorkingHours;
    END

    /* =========================
       CASE 2 : CHECK-OUT
       ========================= */
    ELSE IF @status = 0
    BEGIN
        -- Prevent checkout without check-in
        IF @AttendanceId IS NULL
        BEGIN
            SELECT 
                'NO ACTIVE CHECK-IN FOUND' AS Action,
                NULL AS CheckInDateTime,
                NULL AS CheckOutDateTime,
                NULL AS WorkingHours;
            RETURN;
        END

        SET @CheckInDateTime =
            CAST(@InDate AS DATETIME) + CAST(@InTime AS DATETIME);

        SET @WorkingSeconds =
            DATEDIFF(SECOND, @CheckInDateTime, GETDATE());

        UPDATE StaffAttendance
        SET
            out_time = CAST(GETDATE() AS TIME),
            out_date = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE),
            working_hours = CAST(@WorkingSeconds / 3600.0 AS DECIMAL(10,2)),
            status = 0
        WHERE attendance_id = @AttendanceId;

        SELECT 
            'CHECK-OUT' AS Action,
            @CheckInDateTime AS CheckInDateTime,
            GETDATE() AS CheckOutDateTime,
            CAST(@WorkingSeconds / 3600.0 AS DECIMAL(10,2)) AS WorkingHours;
    END
END
	
	IF @operation = 'GetStaffAttendance'
		BEGIN
			SELECT 
    sr.role,
	sm.role_id,
    sm.staff_id,
    sm.name,
	sa.in_date,
    sm.contact_no,
    sm.society_id,
    sm.active_status,
    sa.status
FROM dbo.Staff_Master sm
INNER JOIN dbo.staff_role sr 
    ON sm.role_id = sr.role_id
LEFT JOIN dbo.StaffAttendance sa
    ON sa.staff_id = sm.staff_id
   AND sa.in_date = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) where sm.role_id != 1 AND sm.society_id=@society_id ;

		END
	

IF @operation = 'Attendance'
BEGIN
    DECLARE 
        @latestId INT,
        @lastInDate DATE,
        @lastStatus INT;

    -- Get latest row
    SELECT TOP 1
        @latestId   = attendance_id,
        @lastInDate = in_date,
        @lastStatus = status
    FROM staffAttendance
    WHERE staff_id = @staff_id
    ORDER BY attendance_id DESC;

    -- If row exists AND not null AND today's date -> UPDATE
    IF @latestId IS NOT NULL
       AND @lastInDate IS NOT NULL
       AND @lastStatus IS NOT NULL
       AND @lastInDate = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
    BEGIN
        UPDATE staffAttendance
        SET status = @status
        WHERE attendance_id = @latestId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        -- Otherwise INSERT a new record
        INSERT INTO staffAttendance (staff_id, in_date,in_time, status, date)
        VALUES (@staff_id, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE),CAST(GETDATE() AS TIME), @status, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE));
    END
END



END


GO


55)USE [society]
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_staff_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:07:38 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

--status:= 1= Present , 2=Absent, 3=Leave

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_staff_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL,
@staff_id INT=0, 
@name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@address NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@contact_no NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, 
@email NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@date_of_join SMALLDATETIME=null,
@society_id NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@build_id INT=0, 
@society_name NVARCHAR(50)=NULL,
@role NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@role_id int=0,
@image nvarchar(500)=NUll,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@id_proof nvarchar(max) = null,
@token nvarchar(max)=null,
@salary  decimal(18,2)=0
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @staff_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = staff_id
                    FROM     dbo.staff_master
                    ORDER BY staff_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;    
                        END
						if @role_id=0
						begin
				 INSERT  INTO dbo.staff_role(role)VALUES(@role);
				 select @role_id=@@Identity 
				end
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.Staff_Master (staff_id, name, address, contact_no, email, date_of_join, society_id,role_id,active_status,image, id_path,salary)
                    VALUES                       (@new,@name, @address, @contact_no, @email, @date_of_join, @society_id, @role_id,0,@image, @id_proof,@salary)
                END
            ELSE
			
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.Staff_Master
                    SET    name         = @name,
                           address      = @address,
                           contact_no   = @contact_no,
                           email        = @email,
                           date_of_join = @date_of_join,
                           society_id   = @society_id,
									image=@image,
							id_path = @id_proof,
                           role_id        = @role_id,
						   salary=@salary
                    WHERE  staff_id = @staff_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM  staff_master 
            WHERE  staff_id=@staff_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update Staff_Master set active_status=1
            WHERE  staff_id = @staff_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     staff_master_vw
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY staff_id DESC;
        END
	IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     staff_master_vw
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and( name like @search+'%' or address like @search+'%' or  contact_no like @search+'%' or email like @search+'%'or cast(date_of_join as varchar) like @search+'%' or role like @search+'%')
            ORDER BY staff_id DESC;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_no'
        BEGIN
            IF @staff_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.Staff_Master
                WHERE  contact_no = @contact_no and active_status=0
                       AND lower(trim(name)) = lower(trim(@name));
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.Staff_Master
                WHERE  contact_no = @contact_no and active_status=0
                       AND Staff_id = @Staff_id
                       AND Staff_id <> @Staff_id;
        END

		--IF @operation = 'Role_Show'
  --      BEGIN
  --          SELECT   *
  --          FROM     staff_role
         
          
  --      END
  IF @operation = 'Role_Show'
BEGIN
    SELECT role_id, role
    FROM staff_role
END
		

		 IF @operation = 'GetStaff'
        BEGIN
           SELECT  *from Staff_Master  where dbo.staff_master.active_status=0 and dbo.staff_master.society_id=@society_id
        END

		if @operation = 'fill_staff'
		begin
		Select distinct role,role_id  from staff_role 
		end

	IF @operation = 'CheckUserByContact'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM Staff_master_vw 
        WHERE active_status = 0 
          AND contact_no = @contact_no;
    END

	IF @operation = 'UpdateGateKeeper'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE Staff_master
        SET name = @name,
            address = @address,
            contact_no = @contact_no,
            email = @email
          
        WHERE staff_id = @staff_id;
    END

		if @operation = 'society_list'
       begin
             Select society_id ,name from society_master
       end

	   	if @operation = 'updateToken'
		BEGIN
			update staff_master set token = @token where staff_id = @staff_id;		
		END 

		if @operation = 'ProfileImage'
		BEGIN
		
				update Staff_Master set image = @image where staff_id = @staff_id;
			
		END
			if @operation = 'FetchStaffData'
		BEGIN
		
				select * from staff_master_vw  where role_id =@role_id and society_id=@society_id;
			
		END

		if @operation ='isStaffExist'
			BEGIN
				select * from Staff_Master where name=@name AND society_id=@society_id;
			END

				if @operation ='UpdateStaffProfile'
			BEGIN
				UPDATE staff_master
		SET image = NULL
		WHERE staff_id= @staff_id;
			END

				if @operation ='UpdateNewToken'
			BEGIN
				UPDATE staff_master
SET token = @token
WHERE staff_id = @staff_id;

			END


			--status:= 1= Present , 2=Absent, 3=Leave
			if @operation = 'GetAttendace'
			BEGIN
				select * from StaffAttendance where staff_id=@staff_id;
			END

END




 
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_suggestion_request_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:07:58 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO






CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_suggestion_request_master]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@sug_id int=0,
@details nvarchar(500)=null,
@society_id nvarchar(10)=null,
@para nvarchar(100)=Null,
@subject nvarchar(250)=null,
@search nvarchar(50)=null
AS
BEGIN
	
	If @operation='Update'
	Begin
	     if @sug_id=0
	       Begin
		    DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = sug_id
                    FROM     dbo.suggestion_request_master
                    ORDER BY sug_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
		    INSERT INTO dbo.suggestion_request_master(sug_id,details,society_id,subject,active_status)values(@new,dbo.InitCap(@details),@society_id,@subject,0)
	       End
         Else
		   Begin
		   Update dbo.suggestion_request_master set details=dbo.InitCap(@details),society_id=@society_id where sug_id=@sug_id
		   End
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.suggestion_request_master where sug_id=@sug_id
    	End
	If @operation='Delete'
	   Begin
	      Update dbo.suggestion_request_master set active_status=1 where sug_id=@sug_id
    	End

    If @operation='check_delete'
	   Begin
          SELECT *  FROM dbo.suggestion_request_master Where sug_id=@sug_id
	   End
	   If @operation='check_name'
	   Begin
	    If @sug_id=0
		Select * from dbo.suggestion_request_master Where lower(trim(details))=lower(trim(@details))
		ElSE
		Select * from dbo.suggestion_request_master Where details=@details AND sug_id=@sug_id AND sug_id<>@sug_id
	   End
	  If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	     select * from dbo.suggestion_request_master where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id  ORDER BY sug_id DESC
    	End
		 If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	     select * from dbo.suggestion_request_master where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id and( subject like @search +'%' or details like @search +'%') ORDER BY sug_id DESC
    	End

END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_terms_condition]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:08:15 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_terms_condition]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL,
@term_id INT=0, 
@terms text =NULL,
@society_id NVARCHAR (50)=NULL
AS
BEGIN
   

		IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     terms
            WHERE active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY term_id DESC;
        END

		IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @term_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = term_id
                    FROM     dbo.terms
                    ORDER BY term_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.terms(term_id,terms,society_id,active_status)
                    VALUES                       (@new,@terms,@society_id,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.terms
                    SET    
					       terms = @terms,
                           society_id = @society_id
                    WHERE  term_id = @term_id;
            END
      END
	  IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   terms
            WHERE active_status=0 and term_id=@term_id;
        END  
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_upload_doc]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:08:38 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_upload_doc]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@file_id INT=0, 
@doc_name nvarchar(20)=null, 
@file_save_path NVARCHAR (500)=NULL, 
@date SMALLDATETIME=NULL, 
 
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@upload_file_id INT=0, 
@file_save NVARCHAR (350)=NULL, 
@society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@Tag NVARCHAR (20)=NULL,
@Description Nvarchar (50)=Null,
@get_doc Nvarchar (20)=null

AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @file_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = file_id
                    FROM     dbo.upload_doc
                    ORDER BY file_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.upload_doc (file_id, file_save_path, date, society_id,active_status,Tag,Description,doc_name )
                    VALUES                     (@new, @file_save_path, getdate(), @society_id,0,@Tag, @Description, @doc_name );
                    SELECT @@IDENTITY AS file_id;
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.upload_doc
                    SET    file_save_path = @file_save_path,
					Tag = @Tag,
					Description = @Description,
					doc_name = @doc_name,
                         
                         
                         
                           society_id     = @society_id
                    WHERE  file_id = @file_id;
                    SELECT @file_id AS file_id;
                END
       End
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
             FROM   dbo.upload_doc
            WHERE  file_id = @file_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
           Update  dbo.upload_doc set active_status=1
            WHERE  file_id = @file_id;
        END

     
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     dbo.upload_doc
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id
            ORDER BY file_id DESC;
        END
		IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     dbo.upload_doc
            WHERE   active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and( doc_name like @search+'%' or doc_name like @search+'%')
            ORDER BY file_id DESC;
        END

				if @operation ='get_doc'
		begin
		select *
		from dbo.upload_doc
		where
		file_id = @file_id
		end
	
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_usefull_contact]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:08:59 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_usefull_contact]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@usefull_contact_id INT=0, 
@p_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,  
@p_type INT=0, 
@org_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@contact_no NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, 
@contact_address NVARCHAR (250)=NULL, 
@address2 NVARCHAR (250)=NULL, 
@email NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@remark NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,
@search nvarchar(200)=null,
@flat_id int=0,
  @helper_id INT=0,
  @type NVARCHAR(50)=null,
    @owner_id INT = 0,
	@maid_id INT = 0,
    @servent_id INT = NULL,

    @review NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @rating NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @member_id INT = NULL,
	@id_path nvarchar(max) =Null
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @usefull_contact_id = 0
			begin
			 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = usefull_contact_id
                    FROM     dbo.usefull_contact
                    ORDER BY usefull_contact_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END


                INSERT  INTO dbo.usefull_contact (usefull_contact_id,p_name, contact_address, p_type, org_name, contact_no, email, remark, address2, society_id,active_status, id_path)
                VALUES                          (@new,dbo.InitCap(@p_name), dbo.InitCap(@contact_address), @p_type, dbo.InitCap(@org_name), @contact_no, @email, dbo.InitCap(@remark), dbo.InitCap(@address2), @society_id,0, @id_path);
				return @new  
		 end
		  ELSE
                UPDATE dbo.usefull_contact
                SET    p_name          = dbo.InitCap(@p_name),
                       contact_address = dbo.InitCap(@contact_address),
                       p_type          = @p_type,
                       org_name        = dbo.InitCap(@org_name),
                       contact_no      = @contact_no,
                       email           = @email,
                       remark          = dbo.InitCap(@remark),
                       address2        = dbo.InitCap(@address2),
                       society_id      = @society_id
                WHERE  usefull_contact_id = @usefull_contact_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   usefull_contact
            WHERE  usefull_contact_id = @usefull_contact_id ;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select_All'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   usefull_contact where active_status=0;
        END
		 IF @operation = 'AddReview'
    BEGIN
	DECLARE @review_id AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @review_id = review_id
                    FROM     dbo.Helperreview
                    ORDER BY review_id DESC;
                    IF @review_id IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @review_id = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @review_id = @review_id + 1;
                        END
        INSERT INTO HelperReview 
            (review_id,usefull_contact_id, owner_id, review, rating, date, mem_id, society_id, active_status)
        VALUES 
            (@review_id,@usefull_contact_id, @owner_id, @review, @rating, GETDATE(), @member_id, @society_id, 0);
    END
	if @operation ='GetCategories'
	Begin 
	select * from person_type where p_type_id not in (4,5,10,11)
	End
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.usefull_contact set active_status=1
            WHERE  usefull_contact_id = @usefull_contact_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'p_name_already_exist'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.usefull_contact
            WHERE  p_name = @p_name
                   AND p_type = @p_type and active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
		if @p_type =0
			begin
					SELECT   *
					FROM     usefull_contact_vw
				WHERE    society_id = @society_id and active_status=0 and(p_name like @search+'%' or org_name  like @search+'%' or contact_no  like @search+'%' or contact_address  like @search+'%' )
				ORDER BY usefull_contact_id DESC;
			END
		else
			begin
					SELECT   *
					FROM     usefull_contact_vw
				WHERE    society_id = @society_id and active_status=0 and p_type=@p_type and(p_name like @search+'%' or org_name  like @search+'%' or contact_no  like @search+'%' or contact_address  like @search+'%' )
				ORDER BY usefull_contact_id DESC;
			END
		End
		if @operation ='WorkDetails'
		Begin
		SELECT 
    u.usefull_contact_id,
    u.org_name,
    r.avg_rating,
    u.p_name,
    u.p_type_name,
    u.contact_no,
    ISNULL(r.review_count, 0) AS review_count,
    ISNULL(w.work_count, 0) AS work_count,
    u.name,
    u.contact_address,
    u.address2
FROM dbo.usefull_contact_vw u
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT 
        COUNT(Unit) AS work_count,
        usefull_contact_id,
        society_id,
        p_type_name
    FROM dbo.helperWorkDetails
    GROUP BY usefull_contact_id, society_id, p_type_name
) w 
    ON w.usefull_contact_id = u.usefull_contact_id
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT 
        COUNT(usefull_contact_id) AS review_count,
        AVG(rating) AS avg_rating,
        usefull_contact_id,
        society_id,
        p_type_name
    FROM dbo.helperInfo
    WHERE active_status = 0
    GROUP BY usefull_contact_id, society_id, p_type_name
) r 
    ON r.usefull_contact_id = u.usefull_contact_id
WHERE 
    u.society_id = @society_id
 AND u.active_status = 0;
End

 IF @operation = 'HelperDetails'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
    FROM usefull_contact_vw
    WHERE society_id = @society_id;
        END
		
	
		IF @operation = 'HelperReview'
        BEGIN
     SELECT 
        review_id,
        usefull_contact_id,
        p_name,
        p_type_name,
        owner,
        owner_type,
        owner_id,
        contact_no,
        review,
        rating,
        CONVERT(VARCHAR(15), [date], 106) AS [date],
        society_id
    FROM helperInfo
    WHERE active_status=0 and usefull_contact_id = @helper_id
   
        END
		
		  IF @operation = 'HelperWorkAt'
        BEGIN
   
     SELECT *
    FROM helperWorkDetails
    WHERE active_status=0 and usefull_contact_id =@helper_id
    
        END
		  IF @operation = 'GetVisitingTypes'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM person_type;
    END
		  IF @operation = 'FamilyMembers'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM owner_extension
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND owner_id = @owner_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'EmergencyContacts'
    BEGIN
        SELECT contact_id AS id, name, contact 
        FROM EmergencyContact
        ORDER BY contact_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'Committee'
    BEGIN
        SELECT u.user_id, ut.UserTypeName AS Unit, u.user_type_id, u.name, u.username, u.contact_no, u.email,
               u.active_status, u.society_id, u.token
        FROM UserLogin u
        INNER JOIN UserType ut ON u.user_type_id = ut.UserTypeId
        WHERE society_id = @society_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'Neighbours'
    BEGIN
        SELECT pre_mob AS contact, name, email, Unit, mask_phone, mask_email
        FROM userData
        WHERE active_status = 0   AND society_id = @society_id;
    END

IF @operation = 'Broadcast'
BEGIN
    SELECT  
	notice_id as record,
        valid_to AS to_date, 
        description, 
        name, 
        n.society_id, 
        nf.notify_status_id,
    ISNULL(nf.seen_status, 0) AS seen_status,
        'Notice' AS notification_type,
        
        CONVERT(varchar, date, 106) AS from_date,
        dbo.GetRelativeTime(n.created_at) AS timestamp
    FROM notice_master AS n 
    LEFT JOIN notify_status AS nf 
        ON n.notice_id = nf.notification_id 
        AND nf.notification_type = 'Notice' 
        AND nf.user_id =  @owner_id
    WHERE n.active_status = 0 
      AND n.society_id = @society_id

    UNION 

    SELECT  
	event_id as record,
        to_date, 
        description, 
        event_name AS name, 
        e.society_id, 
        nf.notify_status_id,
        ISNULL(nf.seen_status, 0) AS seen_status,
        'Event' AS notification_type,
        CONVERT(varchar, from_date, 106) AS from_date,
         dbo.GetRelativeTime(e.created_at) AS timestamp
    FROM event_master AS e 
    LEFT JOIN notify_status AS nf 
        ON e.event_id = nf.notification_id 
        AND nf.notification_type = 'Event'  
        AND nf.user_id = @owner_id
    WHERE e.active_status = 0 
      AND e.society_id = @society_id

    UNION 

    SELECT  
	meet_id as record,
        NULL AS to_date, 
        details AS description, 
        subject AS name, 
        m.society_id, 
        nf.notify_status_id,
        ISNULL(nf.seen_status, 0) AS seen_status,
        'Meeting' AS notification_type,
       
        CONVERT(varchar, meeting_date, 106) AS from_date,
       dbo.GetRelativeTime(m.created_at) AS timestamp
    FROM meeting_master AS m 
    INNER JOIN notify_status AS nf 
        ON m.meet_id = nf.notification_id 
        AND nf.notification_type = 'Meeting' 
        AND nf.user_id = @owner_id
    WHERE m.active_status = 0 
      AND m.society_id = @society_id

    UNION 

    SELECT  
	helpdesk_id as record,
        req_service_date AS to_date, 
        query AS description, 
        'SupportTicket' AS name, 
        h.society_id, 
        nf.notify_status_id,
        ISNULL(nf.seen_status, 0) AS seen_status,
        'Helpdesk' AS notification_type,
       
        CONVERT(varchar, date, 106) AS from_date,
         dbo.GetRelativeTime(h.created_at) AS timestamp
    FROM Helpdesk_vw AS h 
    INNER JOIN notify_status AS nf 
        ON h.helpdesk_id = nf.notification_id 
        AND nf.notification_type = 'Helpdesk' 
        AND nf.user_id = @owner_id
    WHERE h.active_status = 0 
      AND h.society_id = @society_id

	  UNION
	     SELECT  
	visitor_id as record,
        NULL AS to_date, 
        purpose AS description, 
  type AS name, 
        v.society_id, 
        nf.notify_status_id,
        ISNULL(nf.seen_status, 0) AS seen_status,
        'Visitor' AS notification_type,
        CONVERT(varchar, pre_date, 106) AS from_date,
        dbo.GetRelativeTime(v.created_at) AS timestamp
    FROM visitor_master AS v 
    INNER JOIN notify_status AS nf 
        ON v.visitor_id = nf.notification_id 
        AND nf.notification_type = 'Visitor' 
        AND nf.user_id = @owner_id
    WHERE v.active_status = 0 
      AND v.society_id = @society_id

    ORDER BY from_date DESC;


END


    ELSE IF @operation = 'Facilities'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM facilities
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND status = 1 AND isActive=1
          AND society_id = @society_id
        ORDER BY facility_id DESC;
    END

	if @operation ='fill_list'
begin
SELECT * FROM person_type where p_type_id not in(10,11);
end

	if @operation = 'getFamilyHelpers'
		begin
			select * from helperworkdetails where flat_id = @flat_id and active_status=0;
		end

    IF @operation = 'AssignToFlat'
    BEGIN
	 DECLARE @helperWorkId AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @helperWorkId = helper_work_id
                    FROM     dbo.helperWorkAt
                    ORDER BY helper_work_id DESC;
                    IF @helperWorkId IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @helperWorkId = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @helperWorkId = @helperWorkId + 1;
                        END
        INSERT INTO helperWorkAt (helper_work_id,usefull_contact_id, flat_id, society_id,active_status)
        VALUES (@helperWorkId, @usefull_contact_id, @flat_id, @society_id, 0);
    END
	
	if @operation = 'deleteFamilyHelper'
		begin
		Update helperWorkAt set active_status=1 where helper_work_id = @helper_id;
		END

		  IF @operation = 'ComplaintType'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM complaint_type;
    END
END
GO
