/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_UserLogin]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:09:19 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

 
 
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_UserLogin]
@operation NVARCHAR (100)=NULL, @user_id INT=0, @user_type_id INT=0,  @Name NVARCHAR (500)=NULL, @address2 NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, @username NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@password NVARCHAR (200)=NULL, @address1 NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, @contact_no NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, @active_status INT=0,  @email NVARCHAR (100)=NULL,@type NVARCHAR(50)=NULL, 
@join_dt SMALLDATETIME=NULL, @last_dt SMALLDATETIME=NULL, @society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL,@web_token NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL,  @new  NVARCHAR (10)=null,@search nvarchar(50)=null,
                     @num AS INT=0,@village_id NVARCHAR(10)=NULL,@owner_id int=0, @v_name NVARCHAR(100) = NULL, @flat_id int = 0, @photo_path NVARCHAR(500) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @user_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new1 AS int =0;
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new1 = user_id
                    FROM     dbo.UserLogin
                    ORDER BY user_id DESC;
                    IF @new1 IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new1 = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new1 = @new1 + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.UserLogin (user_id,society_id, user_type_id, Name, owner_id,email,contact_no, UserName, Password,  active_status,type,village_id)
                    VALUES                    (@new1,@society_id,@user_type_id, dbo.InitCap(@Name),@owner_id,@email,@contact_no,  @UserName, @Password,0,@type,@village_id);

                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.UserLogin
                    SET    user_type_id  = @user_type_id,
                           Name        = dbo.InitCap(@Name),
                           UserName    = @UserName,
						
                           Password    = case when @Password is not null or @Password !='' then @Password end,
                           email=@email,
						   contact_no=@contact_no,
                           active_status      = @active_status,
						owner_id=@owner_id,
                           type        = @type,
                           society_id  = @society_id,
						   village_id  = @village_id
                    WHERE  user_id = @user_id;
					
                    SELECT @user_id AS user_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Update_Society_ID'
        BEGIN
            IF @user_id IS NOT NULL
                UPDATE dbo.UserLogin
                SET    society_id = @society_id
                WHERE  user_id = @user_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
             SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.user_id, dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id, dbo.UserType.UserTypeName, dbo.UserLogin.name, dbo.UserLogin.username, dbo.UserLogin.password,  
                         dbo.UserLogin.active_status,dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.UserLogin.token,dbo.society_master.name AS Society_name,userlogin.contact_no,userlogin.email,UserLogin.owner_id
FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
 
                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId
						 INNER JOIN
                         dbo.society_master ON dbo.UserLogin.society_id = dbo.society_master.society_id  
           WHERE  user_id = @user_id;
        END
		IF @operation = 'new_society'
        BEGIN
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = society_id
                    FROM     dbo.society_master
                    ORDER BY society_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 'C10001';
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @num = SUBSTRING(@new, 2, 5);
                            SELECT @num = @num + 1;
                            SELECT @new = (SELECT Concat('C', @num));
                        END
             insert into society_master(society_id)values(@new)
			    SELECT   TOP (1) society_master_id
                    FROM     dbo.society_master
                    ORDER BY society_id DESC;
        END
		IF @operation = 'new_village'
        BEGIN
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = village_id
                    FROM     dbo.village_master
                    ORDER BY village_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 'V10001';
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @num = SUBSTRING(@new, 2, 5);
                            SELECT @num = @num + 1;
                            SELECT @new = (SELECT Concat('V', @num));
                        END
             insert into village_master(village_id)values(@new)
			    SELECT   TOP (1) id
                    FROM     dbo.village_master
                    ORDER BY village_id DESC;
        END
--    IF @operation = 'all_Select'
--        BEGIN
--           SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.user_id, dbo.UserType.UserTypeName, dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id, dbo.UserLogin.name, dbo.UserLogin.username, dbo.UserLogin.password, dbo.UserLogin.address1, dbo.UserLogin.address2, 
--                         dbo.UserLogin.contact_no, dbo.UserLogin.email, dbo.UserLogin.active_status, dbo.UserLogin.join_dt, dbo.UserLogin.last_dt, dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.UserLogin.token
--FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
--                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId
--            WHERE  (UserLogin.user_id = @user_id) and active_status=0;
--        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.UserLogin set active_status=1
            WHERE  user_id = @user_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_delete'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.UserLogin
           WHERE  user_id = @user_id and active_status=0;
        END
  IF @operation = 'chk_name'
        BEGIN
            IF @user_id <> 0
                SELECT owner_id
                FROM   dbo.owner_master
                WHERE  name = @Name and pre_mob=@contact_no and  active_status=0
                     
            ELSE
                SELECT email
                FROM   dbo.UserLogin
                WHERE  email = @email and active_status=0;
        END
		
    IF @operation = 'check_email'
        BEGIN
            IF @user_id <> 0
                SELECT email
                FROM   dbo.UserLogin
                WHERE  email = @email and active_status=0
                       AND user_id <> @user_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT email
                FROM   dbo.UserLogin
                WHERE  email = @email and active_status=0;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_UserName'
        BEGIN
            IF @user_id <> 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.UserLogin
                WHERE  UserName = @UserName
                       AND user_id <> @user_id and active_status=0;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.UserLogin
                WHERE  UserName = @UserName and active_status=0;
        END
--    IF @operation = 'Select_all'
--        BEGIN
--           SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.user_id, dbo.UserType.UserTypeName, dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id, dbo.UserLogin.name, dbo.UserLogin.username, dbo.UserLogin.password, dbo.UserLogin.address1, dbo.UserLogin.address2, 
--                         dbo.UserLogin.contact_no, dbo.UserLogin.email, dbo.UserLogin.active_status, dbo.UserLogin.join_dt, dbo.UserLogin.last_dt, dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.UserLogin.token
--FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
--                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId
--          WHERE  user_id = @user_id and active_status=0;
--        END
                             
		 If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	   SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.user_id, dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id, dbo.UserType.UserTypeName, dbo.UserLogin.name, dbo.UserLogin.username, dbo.UserLogin.password,  
                         dbo.UserLogin.active_status,dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.UserLogin.token,dbo.society_master.name AS Society_name,userlogin.contact_no,userlogin.email
FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
 
                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId
						 INNER JOIN
                         dbo.society_master ON dbo.UserLogin.society_id = dbo.society_master.society_id where userlogin.society_id=@society_id and Userlogin.active_status=0
	   End
	   		 If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	   SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.user_id, dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id, dbo.UserType.UserTypeName, dbo.UserLogin.name, dbo.UserLogin.username, dbo.UserLogin.password,  
                         dbo.UserLogin.active_status,dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.UserLogin.token,dbo.society_master.name AS Society_name
FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
 
                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId
						 INNER JOIN
                         dbo.society_master ON dbo.UserLogin.society_id = dbo.society_master.society_id where userlogin.society_id=@society_id and Userlogin.active_status=0 and ( dbo.UserLogin.name like @search+'%' or dbo.UserType.UserTypeName like @search+'%')
	   End
	   IF @operation = 'Insert_Token'
        BEGIN
           Update UserLogin set web_token = @web_token where user_id=@user_id
        END 
		
		IF @operation = 'UpdateProfile'
        BEGIN
           -- Every column here treats a NULL parameter as "not supplied, leave
           -- it alone". That has to hold for ALL of them, not just some:
           -- change-password calls this branch to write only the password, so
           -- any column without the guard is overwritten with NULL. username,
           -- email and contact_no previously lacked it, which wiped the
           -- account's identity — the correct password then had no username to
           -- match against and the account could neither log in nor self-reset.
           --
           -- '' is not the same as NULL: it means "clear this", which is what
           -- the profile editor sends to remove an email or a photo. username
           -- is the exception and preserves on '' too, since a blank username
           -- can never be valid and would lock the account out again.
           Update UserLogin set contact_no = case when @contact_no is null then contact_no else @contact_no end,email = case when @email is null then email else @email end,password= case when @Password is  null or @Password ='' then password else @Password end,username = case when @username is null or @username = '' then username else @username end,Name = case when @Name is null or @Name = '' then Name else dbo.InitCap(@Name) end,photo_path = case when @photo_path is null then photo_path when @photo_path = '' then null else @photo_path end where user_id=@user_id
		  update owner_master set pre_mob = case when @contact_no is null then pre_mob else @contact_no end,email = case when @email is null then email else @email end where owner_id=@owner_id
        END
		IF @operation = 'ResetPass'
        BEGIN
           Update UserLogin set password= case when @Password is  null or @Password ='' then password else @Password end where email=@email
		
        END 
		IF @operation = 'GetProfile'
        BEGIN
             SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.user_id, dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id, dbo.UserType.UserTypeName, dbo.UserLogin.name, dbo.UserLogin.username, dbo.UserLogin.password,  
                         dbo.UserLogin.active_status,dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.UserLogin.token,dbo.society_master.name AS Society_name,userlogin.contact_no,userlogin.email,UserLogin.owner_id,UserLogin.photo_path
FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
 
                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId
						 INNER JOIN
                         dbo.society_master ON dbo.UserLogin.society_id = dbo.society_master.society_id  
           WHERE  user_id = @user_id;
        END 


			if @operation = 'fill_owner'
		begin
		SELECT * FROM owner_master WHERE  login_status=0 and active_status=0 and type = 'owner' AND society_id=@society_id
		end

		if @operation = 'fill_type'
		begin
		Select *  from UserType where type=1
		end 


		    IF @operation = 'GetCommitteeDirectory'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            name,
            contact_no AS contact,
            email,
            society_id
        FROM userlogin
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND society_id = @society_id;
    END

	 --IF @operation = 'GetTokensByUnit'
  --  BEGIN
  --      SELECT * 
  --      FROM userdata
  --      WHERE active_status = 0
  --        AND unit IN (
  --            SELECT unit 
  --            FROM helperWorkDetails
  --            WHERE p_name = @v_name
  --              AND society_id = @society_id
  --        );
  --  END

     IF @operation = 'GetTokensByFlat'
    BEGIN
        SELECT token 
        FROM userdata
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND flat_id = @flat_id and token is not null
          
    END


	if @operation ='getPassword'
		BEGIN
			select password from UserLogin where user_id = @user_id
		END

	if @operation = 'ResetForgotPassword'
		BEGIN
			UPDATE UserLogin set password = @password where email = @email
		END	
		

END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_Vendor_Bill_Payments]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:09:37 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Vendor_Bill_Payments]
    @operation NVARCHAR(20),
    @payment_id INT = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @vendor_id INT = NULL,
    @pay_mode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @cheque_no NVARCHAR(30) = NULL,
    @cheque_date DATE = NULL,
    @bank_name NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @transaction_ref NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @paid_amount DECIMAL(10,2) = NULL,
    @remarks NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @status INT = NULL,
    @created_by NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @bill_details NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @file_path NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @bill_id NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @operation = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @NewReceiptNo NVARCHAR(50);
        DECLARE @NextNum INT;

        -- Generate new sequential receipt number
        SELECT @NextNum = ISNULL(MAX(payment_id), 0) + 1 
        FROM dbo.vendor_bill_payments;

        SET @NewReceiptNo = 'RCPT-' + CAST(YEAR(GETDATE()) AS NVARCHAR(4)) + 
                           '-' + RIGHT('0000' + CAST(@NextNum AS NVARCHAR(4)), 4);

        -- ✅ INSERT PAYMENT RECORD
        INSERT INTO [dbo].[vendor_bill_payments]
        (
            payment_id,
            payment_no,
            society_id,
            vendor_id,
            pay_mode,
            cheque_no,
            cheque_date,
            payment_date,
            bank_name,
            transaction_ref,
            paid_amount,
            remarks,
            status,
            created_by,
            bill_details,
            file_path
        )
        VALUES
        (
            @NextNum,
            @NewReceiptNo,
            @society_id,
            @vendor_id,
            @pay_mode,
            @cheque_no,
            @cheque_date,
            GETDATE(),
            @bank_name, 
            @transaction_ref,
            @paid_amount,
            @remarks,
            ISNULL(@status, 1),
            @created_by,
            @bill_details,
            @file_path 
        );

        -- ✅ SETTLE THE BILL
        EXEC [sp_SettleVendorBills] @PaymentID = @NextNum;

        -- ✅ RETURN THE NEW PAYMENT ID
        SELECT @NextNum AS payment_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE [dbo].[vendor_bill_payments]
        SET
            pay_mode = @pay_mode,
            cheque_no = @cheque_no,
            cheque_date = @cheque_date,
            bank_name = @bank_name,
            transaction_ref = @transaction_ref,
            paid_amount = @paid_amount,
            remarks = @remarks,
            status = @status,
            bill_details = @bill_details,
            file_path = @file_path
        WHERE payment_id = @payment_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM [dbo].[vendor_bill_payments]
        WHERE payment_id = @payment_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'fill_bills'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            vb.bill_id,
            vb.bill_number,
            vb.service_type,
            vb.bill_date,
            vb.due AS total_amount,
            vm.vendor_name,
            bs.bill_status AS status,
            ISNULL(SUM(vp.paid_amount), 0) AS paid_amount,
            (vb.due - ISNULL(SUM(vp.paid_amount), 0)) AS remaining_amount
        FROM vendor_bills vb
        INNER JOIN bill_status bs ON vb.status = bs.status_id
        INNER JOIN vendor_master vm ON vb.vendor_id = vm.vendor_id
        LEFT JOIN vendor_bill_payments vp ON vb.bill_id = vp.bill_details
        WHERE (vb.vendor_id = @vendor_id OR @vendor_id IS NULL)
          AND (vb.bill_id = @bill_id OR @bill_id IS NULL)
          AND vb.status != 4
          AND vb.due != 0
        GROUP BY 
            vb.bill_id,
            vb.bill_number,
            vb.service_type,
            vb.bill_date,
            vb.due,
            vm.vendor_name,
            bs.bill_status;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            payment_id, 
            paid_amount, 
            ISNULL(transaction_ref, cheque_no) AS transaction_ref,
            payment_no, 
            vendor_name,
            bs.bill_status, 
            payment_date, 
            file_path
        FROM vendor_bill_payments AS vp 
        INNER JOIN vendor_master AS vm ON vm.vendor_id = vp.vendor_id 
        INNER JOIN bill_status AS bs ON bs.status_id = status  
        WHERE vp.society_id = @society_id 
        ORDER BY payment_date DESC;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'getreceipt'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM vw_vendor_bill_payment_status
        WHERE bill_id = @bill_id
          AND society_id = @society_id
        ORDER BY payment_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'SELECT'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            payment_id,
            society_id,
            vendor_id,
            pay_mode,
            cheque_no,
            cheque_date,
            payment_date,
            bank_name,
            transaction_ref,
            paid_amount,
            remarks,
            status,
            file_path,
            created_by,
            bill_details
        FROM [dbo].[vendor_bill_payments]
        WHERE 
            (@payment_id IS NULL OR payment_id = @payment_id)
            AND (@vendor_id IS NULL OR vendor_id = @vendor_id)
            AND (@society_id IS NULL OR society_id = @society_id)
        ORDER BY payment_date DESC;
    END
END;
GO

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_vendor_bills]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:09:55 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_vendor_bills]
    @operation NVARCHAR(20),                 -- INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, VIEW, VIEWBYID, APPROVE, REJECT, PAY
    @bill_id INT = 0,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @bill_number NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @bill_date DATE = NULL,
    @vendor_id NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @subtotal DECIMAL(12,2) = 0,
    @tax_amount DECIMAL(12,2) = 0,
    @total_amount DECIMAL(12,2) = 0,
    @status INT = NULL,
    @user_id INT = 0,
    @notes NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @created_by INT = 0,
    @result NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
    @service INT = NULL,
    @desc NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @approval_id INT = 0,
    @salary NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @bill_details NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- INSERT
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        -- ✅ CHECK IF ANY STAFF MEMBER ALREADY HAS SALARY BILL FOR THIS MONTH
        IF @service = 0 -- Staff Payment
        BEGIN
            DECLARE @MonthName NVARCHAR(20);
            DECLARE @YearValue INT;
            DECLARE @DuplicateStaffNames NVARCHAR(MAX) = '';
            DECLARE @DuplicateBillNumbers NVARCHAR(MAX) = '';
            
            SET @MonthName = DATENAME(MONTH, @bill_date);
            SET @YearValue = YEAR(@bill_date);
            
            -- ✅ Check each staff ID in the comma-separated list
            DECLARE @StaffId INT;
            DECLARE @CurrentStaffIds TABLE (staff_id INT);
            
            -- Split the comma-separated staff IDs
            INSERT INTO @CurrentStaffIds (staff_id)
            SELECT CAST(value AS INT)
            FROM STRING_SPLIT(@vendor_id, ',')
            WHERE RTRIM(value) <> '';
            
            -- Check for duplicates for each staff member
            DECLARE staff_cursor CURSOR FOR
            SELECT staff_id FROM @CurrentStaffIds;
            
            OPEN staff_cursor;
            FETCH NEXT FROM staff_cursor INTO @StaffId;
            
            WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
            BEGIN
                -- Check if this staff member already has a bill for this month
                IF EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 
                    FROM vendor_bills vb
                    WHERE vb.society_id = @society_id
                      AND vb.service_type = 0
                      AND YEAR(vb.bill_date) = @YearValue
                      AND MONTH(vb.bill_date) = MONTH(@bill_date)
                      AND vb.status <> 4 -- Exclude rejected bills
                      AND EXISTS (
                          SELECT 1 
                          FROM STRING_SPLIT(vb.vendor_id, ',') 
                          WHERE CAST(value AS INT) = @StaffId
                      )
                )
                BEGIN
                    -- Get staff name and existing bill number
                    DECLARE @StaffName NVARCHAR(100);
                    DECLARE @ExistingBillNo NVARCHAR(50);
                    
                    SELECT TOP 1 
                        @StaffName = sm.name,
                        @ExistingBillNo = vb.bill_number
                    FROM vendor_bills vb
                    INNER JOIN staff_master sm ON sm.staff_id = @StaffId
                    WHERE vb.society_id = @society_id
                      AND vb.service_type = 0
                      AND YEAR(vb.bill_date) = @YearValue
                      AND MONTH(vb.bill_date) = MONTH(@bill_date)
                      AND vb.status <> 4
                      AND EXISTS (
                          SELECT 1 
                          FROM STRING_SPLIT(vb.vendor_id, ',') 
                          WHERE CAST(value AS INT) = @StaffId
                      );
                    
                    -- Add to duplicate list
                    IF @DuplicateStaffNames = ''
                    BEGIN
                        SET @DuplicateStaffNames = @StaffName;
                        SET @DuplicateBillNumbers = @ExistingBillNo;
                    END
                    ELSE
                    BEGIN
                        SET @DuplicateStaffNames = @DuplicateStaffNames + ', ' + @StaffName;
                        SET @DuplicateBillNumbers = @DuplicateBillNumbers + ', ' + @ExistingBillNo;
                    END
                END
                
                FETCH NEXT FROM staff_cursor INTO @StaffId;
            END
            
            CLOSE staff_cursor;
            DEALLOCATE staff_cursor;
            
            -- ✅ If duplicates found, return error
            IF @DuplicateStaffNames <> ''
            BEGIN
                SELECT 
                    bill_id = -1,
                    error_message = 'Salary already paid for ' + @MonthName + ' ' + CAST(@YearValue AS VARCHAR) + 
                                    ' to: ' + @DuplicateStaffNames + 
                                    ' (Bill: ' + @DuplicateBillNumbers + ')',
                    duplicate_staff = @DuplicateStaffNames,
                    existing_bill_numbers = @DuplicateBillNumbers;
                RETURN;
            END
        END

        -- ✅ PROCEED WITH INSERT IF NO DUPLICATE
        DECLARE @NewBillId INT;
        SET @NewBillId = (SELECT ISNULL(MAX(bill_id), 0) + 1 FROM vendor_bills);

        INSERT INTO dbo.vendor_bills
        (
            bill_id,
            society_id,
            bill_number,
            bill_date,
            vendor_id,
            subtotal,
            tax_amount,
            total_amount,
            status,
            notes,
            created_date,
            created_by,
            service_type,
            description,
            due
        )
        VALUES
        (
            @NewBillId,
            @society_id,
            @bill_number,
            @bill_date,
            @vendor_id,
            @subtotal,
            @tax_amount,
            @total_amount,
            ISNULL(@status, 1),
            @notes,
            GETDATE(),
            @created_by,
            @service,
            @desc,
            @total_amount
        );

        -- ✅ Return success with new bill ID
        SELECT 
            bill_id = @NewBillId,
            error_message = NULL,
            duplicate_staff = NULL,
            existing_bill_numbers = NULL;
            
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Get Charges for Maintenance Bill 
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF @operation = 'exfetch'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM maintenance_charges 
        WHERE charges_type = 0 
          AND status = 1 
          AND society_id = @society_id;
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- GetApprover List Except Current User
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF @operation = 'add_approver'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            user_id,
            name,
            '2' AS status,
            GETDATE() AS date,
            'add' AS type 
        FROM UserLogin 
        WHERE user_id != @user_id 
          AND active_status = 0 
          AND society_id = @society_id;
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Fill Vendor List 
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF @operation = 'vendor_fill'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            vendor_id,
            vendor_name,
            gst_no, 
            service_type 
        FROM vendor_master 
        WHERE society_id = @society_id;
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- UPDATE
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.vendor_bills
        SET
            society_id   = @society_id,
            bill_number  = @bill_number,
            bill_date    = @bill_date,
            vendor_id    = @vendor_id,
            subtotal     = @subtotal,
            tax_amount   = @tax_amount,
            total_amount = @total_amount,
            status       = @status,
            notes        = @notes,
            updated_date = GETDATE(),
            service_type = @service
        WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;
        
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Get Bills Items
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'GETBILLITEMS'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM dbo.inventory_master 
        WHERE vendor_id = @vendor_id 
          AND society_id = @society_id;
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- DELETE
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM dbo.vendor_bills 
        WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- VIEW ALL - Grid Show
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'Grid_Show'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            vb.bill_id,
            vb.society_id,
            vb.bill_number,
            vb.bill_date,
            vb.vendor_id,
            names.vendor_name,
            vb.subtotal,
            vb.tax_amount,
            vb.total_amount,
            ISNULL(SUM(TRY_CAST(vp.bill_paid_amount AS DECIMAL(18,2))), 0) AS paid_amount,
            vb.total_amount - ISNULL(SUM(TRY_CAST(vp.bill_paid_amount AS DECIMAL(18,2))), 0) AS remaining_amount,
            CASE 
                WHEN vb.total_amount - ISNULL(SUM(TRY_CAST(vp.bill_paid_amount AS DECIMAL(18,2))), 0) = 0
                    THEN 'Paid'
                WHEN ISNULL(SUM(TRY_CAST(vp.bill_paid_amount AS DECIMAL(18,2))), 0) = 0
                    THEN 'Unpaid'
                ELSE 'Partially Paid'
            END AS payment_status,
            vb.status,
            bs.bill_status,
            vb.notes,
            vb.created_date,
            vb.created_by,
            vb.service_type
        FROM vendor_bills vb
        OUTER APPLY (
            SELECT STRING_AGG(t.name, ', ') AS vendor_name
            FROM (
                -- Vendors
                SELECT vm.vendor_name AS name
                FROM STRING_SPLIT(vb.vendor_id, ',') s
                JOIN vendor_master vm ON vm.vendor_id = TRY_CAST(s.value AS INT)
                WHERE vb.service_type <> 0

                UNION ALL

                -- Staff
                SELECT sm.name
                FROM STRING_SPLIT(vb.vendor_id, ',') s
                JOIN staff_master sm ON sm.staff_id = TRY_CAST(s.value AS INT)
                WHERE vb.service_type = 0
            ) t
        ) names
        INNER JOIN bill_status bs ON bs.status_id = vb.status
        LEFT JOIN vendor_bill_payments vp
            ON vp.society_id = vb.society_id
           AND vp.status = 1
           AND EXISTS (
                SELECT 1
                FROM STRING_SPLIT(vp.bill_details, ',') s
                WHERE TRY_CAST(s.value AS INT) = vb.bill_id
           )
        WHERE vb.society_id = @society_id
        GROUP BY
            vb.bill_id,
            vb.society_id,
            vb.bill_number,
            vb.bill_date,
            vb.vendor_id,
            names.vendor_name,
            vb.subtotal,
            vb.tax_amount,
            vb.total_amount,
            vb.status,
            bs.bill_status,
            vb.notes,
            vb.created_date,
            vb.created_by,
            vb.service_type
        ORDER BY vb.bill_id DESC;

        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- VIEW BY ID
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'VIEWBYID'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            vb.bill_id,
            vb.society_id,
            sm.name AS society_name,
            vb.bill_number,
            vb.bill_date,
            vb.vendor_id,
            vm.vendor_name,
            vb.subtotal,
            vb.tax_amount,
            vb.total_amount,
            vb.status,
            vb.notes,
            vb.created_date,
            vb.updated_date,
            vb.created_by,
            vb.description
        FROM dbo.vendor_bills vb
        INNER JOIN dbo.vendor_master vm ON vb.vendor_id = vm.vendor_id
        INNER JOIN dbo.society_master sm ON vb.society_id = sm.society_id
        WHERE vb.bill_id = @bill_id;

        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- APPROVE
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'APPROVE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.vendor_bills
        SET status = 2, -- Approved
            updated_date = GETDATE()
        WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;

        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- REJECT
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'REJECT'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.vendor_bills
        SET status = 4, -- Rejected
            updated_date = GETDATE()
        WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;

        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- MARK AS PAID
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'PAY'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.vendor_bills
        SET status = 3, -- Paid
            updated_date = GETDATE()
        WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;

        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- SELECT - Get Bill Details
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'SELECT'
    BEGIN
        -- Step 1: Compute payments per bill
        WITH BillPayments AS (
            SELECT 
                vb.bill_id,
                SUM(
                    CAST(
                        TRY_CAST(vp.bill_paid_amount AS DECIMAL(18,2)) 
                        / NULLIF(splits.NumBills, 0) 
                    AS DECIMAL(18,2))
                ) AS paid_amount
            FROM vendor_bills vb
            LEFT JOIN vendor_bill_payments vp
                ON vp.society_id = vb.society_id
               AND vp.status = 1
            CROSS APPLY (
                SELECT COUNT(*) AS NumBills
                FROM dbo.SplitString(vp.bill_details, ',') s
                WHERE TRY_CAST(s.Value AS INT) = vb.bill_id
            ) splits
            WHERE (@bill_id IS NULL OR vb.bill_id = @bill_id)
            GROUP BY vb.bill_id
        ),
        -- Step 2: Get all vendor names concatenated per bill
        VendorNames AS (
            SELECT 
                vb.bill_id,
                STRING_AGG(
                    CASE 
                        WHEN vb.service_type <> 0 THEN vm.vendor_name
                        ELSE sm.name
                    END, 
                    ', '
                ) AS vendor_names,
                MAX(CASE WHEN vb.service_type <> 0 THEN vm.gst_no END) AS gst_no
            FROM vendor_bills vb
            CROSS APPLY (
                SELECT TRY_CAST(Value AS INT) AS vendor_split_id
                FROM dbo.SplitString(vb.vendor_id, ',')
                WHERE TRY_CAST(Value AS INT) IS NOT NULL
            ) AS split_row
            LEFT JOIN vendor_master vm 
                ON vm.vendor_id = split_row.vendor_split_id AND vb.service_type <> 0
            LEFT JOIN staff_master sm 
                ON sm.staff_id = split_row.vendor_split_id AND vb.service_type = 0
            WHERE (@bill_id IS NULL OR vb.bill_id = @bill_id)
            GROUP BY vb.bill_id
        )
        SELECT 
            vb.bill_id,
            vb.society_id,
            vb.bill_number,
            vb.bill_date,
            vb.vendor_id AS original_vendor_ids,
            vn.vendor_names AS vendor_name,
            vn.gst_no,
            vb.subtotal,
            vb.tax_amount,
            vb.total_amount,
            ISNULL(CAST(bp.paid_amount AS DECIMAL(18,2)), 0) AS paid_amount,
            CAST(vb.total_amount - ISNULL(bp.paid_amount, 0) AS DECIMAL(18,2)) AS remaining_amount,
            CASE 
                WHEN vb.total_amount - ISNULL(bp.paid_amount, 0) = 0 THEN 'Paid'
                WHEN ISNULL(bp.paid_amount, 0) = 0 THEN 'Unpaid'
                ELSE 'Partially Paid'
            END AS payment_status,
            vb.status,
            bs.bill_status,
            vb.notes AS description,
            vb.created_date,
            vb.created_by,
            vb.service_type
        FROM vendor_bills vb
        INNER JOIN bill_status bs ON bs.status_id = vb.status
        LEFT JOIN BillPayments bp ON vb.bill_id = bp.bill_id
        LEFT JOIN VendorNames vn ON vn.bill_id = vb.bill_id
        WHERE (@bill_id IS NULL OR vb.bill_id = @bill_id)
        ORDER BY vb.bill_id DESC;

        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- GET_BILL_ITEMS
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'GET_BILL_ITEMS'
    BEGIN
        SELECT * 
        FROM inventory_master 
        WHERE vendor_bill_id = @bill_id;
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- GET_APPROVALS
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'GET_APPROVALS'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            approval_id,
            vendor_bill_approval.user_id,
            bill_id,
            vendor_bill_approval.approval_status,
            approval_date, 
            remarks,
            name,
            'get' AS type
        FROM vendor_bill_approval 
        INNER JOIN UserLogin ON UserLogin.user_id = vendor_bill_approval.user_id
        WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- UPDATE_STATUS
    --------------------------------------------------
    IF UPPER(@operation) = 'UPDATE_STATUS'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE vendor_bill_approval 
        SET approval_status = @status, 
            approval_date = GETDATE(),
            remarks = @notes  
        WHERE approval_id = @approval_id;
        
        SELECT @bill_id = bill_id 
        FROM vendor_bill_approval 
        WHERE approval_id = @approval_id;
        
        -- If rejected, update bill status
        IF @status = 4
        BEGIN
            UPDATE vendor_bills 
            SET status = 4, 
                updated_date = GETDATE() 
            WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;
        END
        
        -- If all approvals are done, update bill to approved
        IF NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 
            FROM vendor_bill_approval 
            WHERE approval_status = 1 
              AND bill_id = @bill_id
        )
        BEGIN
            UPDATE vendor_bills 
            SET status = 2, 
                updated_date = GETDATE() 
            WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;
        END
        
        RETURN;
    END;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- INVALID OPERATION
    --------------------------------------------------
    SELECT 'Invalid operation specified.' AS error_message;
    RETURN;
END;
GO


63)USE [society]
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_vendor_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:10:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_vendor_master]
    @operation NVARCHAR(50),             -- e.g. 'INSERT','UPDATE','DELETE','GET','GET_ALL','SEARCH','CHECK_VENDOR','CHECK_GST','CHECK_EMAIL'
    @vendor_id INT = NULL,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @vendor_name NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
    @contact_person NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @contact_no NVARCHAR(30) = NULL,
    @email NVARCHAR(120) = NULL,
    @service_type NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @gst_no NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @address NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
    @search_text NVARCHAR(200) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @operation = 'INSERT'
    BEGIN
        IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.vendor_master WHERE vendor_name = @vendor_name AND society_id = @society_id AND isActive = 1)
        BEGIN
            SELECT 'Vendor already exists for this society.' AS message, 0 AS status;
            RETURN;
        END

        INSERT INTO dbo.vendor_master (society_id, vendor_name, contact_person, contact_no, email, service_type, gst_no, address)
        VALUES (@society_id, @vendor_name, @contact_person, @contact_no, @email, @service_type, @gst_no, @address);

        SELECT 'Vendor inserted successfully.' AS message, 1 AS status;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'UPDATE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.vendor_master
        SET 
            vendor_name = @vendor_name,
            contact_person = @contact_person,
            contact_no = @contact_no,
            email = @email,
            service_type = @service_type,
            gst_no = @gst_no,
            address = @address
        WHERE vendor_id = @vendor_id;

        SELECT 'Vendor updated successfully.' AS message, 1 AS status;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'DELETE'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dbo.vendor_master
        SET isActive = 0
        WHERE vendor_id = @vendor_id;

        SELECT 'Vendor deleted successfully.' AS message, 1 AS status;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'Select'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM dbo.vendor_master
        WHERE vendor_id = @vendor_id;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM dbo.vendor_master
        WHERE isActive = 1
        ORDER BY created_at DESC;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'SEARCH'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM dbo.vendor_master
        WHERE isActive = 1
        AND (
            vendor_name LIKE '%' + @search_text + '%' OR
            contact_person LIKE '%' + @search_text + '%' OR
            contact_no LIKE '%' + @search_text + '%' OR
            service_type LIKE '%' + @search_text + '%' OR
            gst_no LIKE '%' + @search_text + '%'
        )
        ORDER BY vendor_name;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'CHECK_VENDOR'
    BEGIN
        IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.vendor_master WHERE vendor_name = @vendor_name AND society_id = @society_id AND isActive = 1)
            SELECT 1 AS exists_flag, 'Vendor name already exists' AS message;
        ELSE
            SELECT 0 AS exists_flag, 'Vendor name available' AS message;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'CHECK_GST'
    BEGIN
        IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.vendor_master WHERE gst_no = @gst_no AND society_id = @society_id AND isActive = 1)
            SELECT 1 AS exists_flag, 'GST Number already exists' AS message;
        ELSE
            SELECT 0 AS exists_flag, 'GST Number available' AS message;
    END

    ELSE IF @operation = 'CHECK_EMAIL'
    BEGIN
        IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.vendor_master WHERE email = @email AND society_id = @society_id AND isActive = 1)
            SELECT 1 AS exists_flag, 'Email already exists' AS message;
        ELSE
            SELECT 0 AS exists_flag, 'Email available' AS message;
    END

    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT 'Invalid operation type.' AS message, 0 AS status;
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_village_master]
@operation nvarchar(50)=null,
@village_id nvarchar(50)=null,
@name nvarchar(50)=null,
@contact_no nvarchar(50)=null,
@email nvarchar(150)=null,
@division nvarchar (50)=Null,

@district_id int=0,

@termination_date datetime=Null,

AS
BEGIN

		   active_status=0 where village_id=@village_id
		
		
	End
	If @operation='Select'
	   Begin
	      SELECT * from dbo.village_master where id=@id       
    	End
	--If @operation='Delete'
	--   Begin
	--      Update dbo.society_master set active_status=1 where society_master_id=@society_master_id
 --   	End

	--   If @operation='check_no'
	--   Begin
	--    If @society_master_id=0
	--	Select * from dbo.society_master Where registration_no=@registration_no and active_status=0 AND lower(trim(name))=lower(trim(@name))
	--	ElSE
	--	Select * from dbo.society_master Where registration_no=@registration_no AND active_status=0 and society_master_id=@society_master_id AND society_master_id<>@society_master_id
	--   End

	   
	--   If @operation='check_id'
	--   Begin
	--    If @society_master_id=0
	--	Select * from dbo.society_master Where society_id=@society_id and active_status=0
	--	ElSE
	--	Select * from dbo.society_master Where society_id=@society_id AND society_master_id=@society_master_id and active_status=0 AND society_master_id<>@society_master_id
	--   End

	   If @operation='Grid_Show'
	   Begin
	   --Select * from village_master where active_status=0 and village_id=@village_id ORDER BY society_master_id DESC

FROM            dbo.village_master INNER JOIN
                         dbo.division ON dbo.village_master.district_id = dbo.division.district_id where active_status=0 and village_id=@village_id         
	   End

		 If @operation='check_no'
	   Begin
	    If @id=0
		Select * from dbo.village_master Where registration_no=@registration_no and active_status=0 AND lower(trim(name))=lower(trim(@name))
		ElSE

	   End

FROM dbo.village_master INNER JOIN dbo.division ON dbo.village_master.district_id = dbo.division.district_id
where active_status=0 and village_id='V10010' and name like 'Wayri%'

END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_Village_staff]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:10:56 ******/

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Village_staff]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL,
@staff_id INT=0, 
@staff_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@address NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@contact_no NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, 
@email NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@joined_date SMALLDATETIME=null,
@village_id NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, 
@build_id INT=0, 
@role_id  INT=0,
@role NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@id_path NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
@salary Decimal = NULL

AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @staff_id = 0
                BEGIN
					if @role_id=0
						begin
				 INSERT  INTO dbo.village_staff_role(role)VALUES(@role);
				 select @role_id=@@Identity 
				end
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.Village_staff (staff_name, address, contact_no, email, joined_date, village_id,role_id,is_active, id_path, salary)
                    VALUES                       (@staff_name, @address, @contact_no, @email, @joined_date, @village_id, @role_id,0, @id_path, @salary)
                END
            ELSE
			
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.Village_staff
                    SET    staff_name   = @staff_name,
                           address      = @address,
                           contact_no   = @contact_no,
                           email        = @email,
                           joined_date  = @joined_date,
                           village_id   = @village_id,
						   role_id      = @role_id,
						   id_path		= @id_path,
						   salary		= @salary
                    WHERE  staff_id = @staff_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM  Village_staff 
            WHERE  staff_id=@staff_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update Village_staff set is_active=1
            WHERE  staff_id = @staff_id;
        END

	IF @operation = 'get_staff_role'
		BEGIN
			select * from village_staff_role;
		END
	IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
		BEGIN
			SELECT dbo.village_staff_role.role, dbo.Village_staff.staff_id, dbo.Village_staff.village_id, dbo.Village_staff.staff_name, dbo.Village_staff.contact_no, dbo.Village_staff.email, dbo.Village_staff.is_active, dbo.Village_staff.joined_date, 
                  dbo.Village_staff.address, dbo.Village_staff.id_path, dbo.Village_staff.salary
FROM     dbo.Village_staff INNER JOIN
                  dbo.village_staff_role ON dbo.Village_staff.role_id = dbo.village_staff_role.role_id where village_id = @village_id;
		END
END


GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_Visitor]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:11:17 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Visitor]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@visitor_id INT=0, 
@v_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@type NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@contact_no NVARCHAR (150)=NULL,
@flat_id INT=0,
@build_id Int=0,
@vehicle_no NVARCHAR(50)=NULL,
@preference NVARCHAR(50)=NULL,
@purpose NVARCHAR(50)=NULL, 
@in_date SMALLDATETIME=NUll,
@out_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@in_time SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@pre_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@out_time SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@society_id NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@sql_string NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL,
@image NVARCHAR (MAX)=NULL,
@build INT=0,
@in_user_id INT=0,
@search varchar(250)=null,
@gateOtp int =0,
@user_id int=0,
@Approver_Name  NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
@status int = 0,
@company NVARCHAR(50)=NULL,
@location NVARCHAR(50)=NULL,
@owner_id int =0,
@Approver_id int = 0,
@start_date DATE = NULL,
@end_date DATE = NULL

AS
BEGIN
Set Nocount ON;
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @visitor_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = visitor_id
                    FROM     dbo.visitor_master
                    ORDER BY visitor_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.Visitor_master (visitor_id,society_id, v_name, type, flat_id, in_date, out_date, in_time, out_time,pre_date,build_id,contact_no,purpose,preference,vehicle_no,image,active_status, in_user_id,gateOTp,company,status,location,owner_id)
                    VALUES                         (@new,@society_id, dbo.InitCap(@v_name), @type, @flat_id, cast(@in_date as smalldatetime), @out_date, getdate(), @out_time, @pre_date,@build_id,@contact_no,@purpose,@preference,@vehicle_no,@image,0,@in_user_id, CAST((RAND() * (899999) + 100000) AS INT),@company,@status,@location,@owner_id);
					select gateOtp,visitor_id from visitor_master where visitor_id=@new
					
					
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.Visitor_master 
                    SET    society_id = @society_id,
                           v_name     = dbo.InitCap(@v_name),
                           type       = @type,
                           flat_id       = @flat_id,
                           in_date    = @in_date,
                           out_date   = @out_date,
                           in_time    = @in_time,
                           out_time   = @out_time,
                           build_id   = @build_id,
						   contact_no = @contact_no,
						   purpose    = @purpose,
						   preference = @preference,
						   vehicle_no = @vehicle_no,
						   image= @image,
						   company=@company,
						   location=@location,
						   owner_id=@owner_id
						  
                    WHERE  visitor_id = @visitor_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT *
            FROM   dbo.visitor
            WHERE  visitor_id = @visitor_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.Visitor_master set active_status=1
            WHERE  visitor_id = @visitor_id;
        END

    IF @operation = 'check_no'
        BEGIN
            IF @visitor_id = 0
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.Visitor_master
                WHERE  contact_no = @contact_no and active_status=0 
                       AND lower(trim(v_name)) = lower(trim(@v_name))
                       AND society_id = @society_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.Visitor_master
                WHERE  contact_no = @contact_no and active_status=0
                       AND visitor_id = @visitor_id
                       AND visitor_id <> @visitor_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
	
			SELECT *
			FROM visitor
			WHERE active_status = 0
			  AND society_id = @society_id
			  AND in_date >= DATEADD(DAY, -30, GETDATE())
			ORDER BY visitor_id DESC;
        END
		 IF @operation = 'Search'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     visitor
            WHERE  active_status=0 and society_id = @society_id and ( v_name like @search+'%' or   type like @search+'%' ) and (in_date between @in_date and @out_date) and( out_date between @in_date and @out_date)
            ORDER BY visitor_id DESC;
        END
		 IF @operation = 'today'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *  
        FROM visitor  
        WHERE active_status = 0 
         
          AND flat_id = @flat_id  AND pre_date = DATEADD(dd, 0, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, GETDATE()));
    END
	IF @operation = 'byFlat'
BEGIN
    SELECT *
    FROM visitor_master_vw
    WHERE active_status = 0
      AND flat_id = @flat_id
     -- AND ((is_rented = 1 AND owner_type = 'Rent') OR (is_rented = 0 AND owner_type = 'Owner'));
END


   -- -- Fetch all visitors by flat
   --  IF @operation = 'byFlat'
   -- BEGIN
   --    SELECT   *FROM visitor
   --     WHERE active_status = 0 AND flat_id = 7  and owner_type=(   CASE 
   --     WHEN is_rented = 1 THEN 'Rent'
   --     ELSE 'Owner'
   -- End )        
   
   --End


    -- Fetch visitor details
     IF @operation = 'details'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            Username,
            Approver_Name,
            status,
            v_name,
            CONVERT(VARCHAR, in_date, 106) AS in_date,
            ISNULL(CONVERT(VARCHAR, out_date, 106), 'none') AS out_date,
            in_time,
            ISNULL(out_time, 'none') AS out_time,
            image
        FROM visitor  
        WHERE active_status = 0 
          AND society_id = @society_id
          AND visitor_id = @visitor_id;
    END



	if @operation ='fill_build'
	begin
	Select *  from building_master where society_id =@society_id
	end

	if @operation ='fill_owner'
	begin
	Select unit, flat_id from owner_search_vw where  society_id=@society_id and build_id=@build_id
	end

	
IF @operation = 'GetVisitorList'
    BEGIN
        SELECT *
        FROM visitor_master_vw
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND (
                 in_date =   DATEADD(dd, 0, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, GETDATE()))
              OR pre_date = DATEADD(dd, 0, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, GETDATE()))
              )
          AND society_id = @society_id
        ORDER BY visitor_id DESC;
    END

	IF @operation = 'GetRegularVisitor'
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            contact_no,
            usefull_contact_id AS visitor_id,
            p_name AS v_name,
            CAST(COUNT(unit) AS VARCHAR) AS unit,
            NULL AS image,
            p_type_name AS type,
            0 AS flat_id
        FROM helperWorkDetails
        WHERE society_id = @society_id
        GROUP BY usefull_contact_id, p_name, contact_no, p_type_name;
    END

	 IF @operation = 'VisitorCheckIn'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE visitor_master
        SET in_date = GETDATE(),
            in_time = GETDATE(),
            in_user_id = @user_id,
			status = @status
        WHERE visitor_id = @visitor_id;
    END

    IF @operation = 'VisitorCheckOut'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE visitor_master
        SET out_date = GETDATE(),
            out_time = GETDATE(),
            out_user_id = @in_user_id
        WHERE visitor_id = @visitor_id;
    END

	 IF @operation = 'UpdateApproveStatus'
    BEGIN
        UPDATE Visitor_master
        SET Approver_Name = @Approver_Name,
            status = @status,
            preference = CASE 
                            WHEN @preference IS NOT NULL AND LTRIM(RTRIM(@preference)) <> '' 
                                THEN @preference 
                            ELSE preference 
                         END
        WHERE visitor_id = @visitor_id;
    END

	IF @operation= 'updateStatus'
		BEGIN	
			UPDATE visitor_master
				SET Approval_id = @owner_id, status = @status 
				where visitor_id = @visitor_id
		END

		if @operation = 'VisitorImage'
		BEGIN
		
				update visitor_master set image = @image where visitor_id = @visitor_id;
			
		END
		if @operation = 'VisitorIn'
		BEGIN
		
				update visitor_master set status=1, in_date=cast(GETDATE() AS DATE), in_time=cast(GETDATE() AS TIME) where visitor_id = @visitor_id;
				select token from owner_master where flat_id = (select flat_id from visitor_master where visitor_id = @visitor_id)
		END
		if @operation = 'VisitorOut'
		BEGIN
		
				update visitor_master set status=3, out_date=cast(GETDATE() AS DATE), out_time=cast(GETDATE() AS TIME) where visitor_id = @visitor_id;
			
		END
		

		IF @operation = 'GetCommitteeContact'
			BEGIN
								WITH RankedUsers AS (
					SELECT contact_no, name, user_type_id, society_id,
						   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
							   PARTITION BY user_type_id
							   ORDER BY user_type_id
						   ) AS rn
					FROM userLogin
					WHERE society_id = @society_id
					  AND user_type_id IN (1, 2)
				)
				SELECT contact_no, name, user_type_id, society_id
				FROM RankedUsers
				WHERE rn = 1;

			END
IF @operation = 'GetVisitorHistory'
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @histStart DATE = ISNULL(@start_date, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE));
        DECLARE @histEnd   DATE = ISNULL(@end_date, CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE));

        SELECT *
        FROM visitor_master_vw
        WHERE active_status = 0
          AND (
                 TRY_CONVERT(DATE, in_date, 106) BETWEEN @histStart AND @histEnd
              OR CAST(pre_date AS DATE) BETWEEN @histStart AND @histEnd
              )
          AND society_id = @society_id
        ORDER BY visitor_id DESC;
    END



	END 
	
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_water_tax]
@operation NVARCHAR (50)=NULL,
@water_tax_id int=0,
@village_owner_id int=0,
@village_id nvarchar(10)=Null,
@house_no int=0,
@from_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@to_date SMALLDATETIME=NULL,
@con_type_id int=0,
@water_connection_size int = Null,
@water_tax_amount decimal(18,2)=Null

AS
BEGIN
    
	IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @water_tax_id = 0
                BEGIN
				 DECLARE @new AS int =0;
                  
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = @water_tax_id
                    FROM     dbo.water_tax
                    ORDER BY water_tax_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = 0;
                        END
                    ELSE
                        BEGIN
                            SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                        END
                    
                    INSERT  INTO water_tax(water_tax_id,village_id,house_no,from_date,to_date,con_type_id,water_connection_size,water_tax_amount,active_status)
                    VALUES                  (@new,@village_id,@house_no,@from_date,@to_date,@con_type_id,@water_connection_size,@water_tax_amount,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE water_tax
                    SET    village_id   =  @village_id,
                           house_no     = @house_no,
                           from_date        = @from_date,
						   to_date      = @to_date,
						   con_type_id  = @con_type_id,

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[sp_wing_master]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:11:52 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_wing_master]
@operation NVARCHAR (20)=NULL, @wing_id INT=0, @search nvarchar(200) =null, @w_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL, @build_id INT=0, @para NVARCHAR (100)=NULL, @society_id NVARCHAR (10)=NULL, @build_name NVARCHAR (50)=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    IF @operation = 'Update'
        BEGIN
            IF @wing_id = 0
                BEGIN
                    DECLARE @new AS INT;
                    SELECT   TOP (1) @new = wing_id
                    FROM     dbo.wing_master
                    ORDER BY wing_id DESC;
                    IF @new IS NULL
                       OR @new = 0
                        SELECT @new = 1;
                    ELSE
                        SELECT @new = @new + 1;
                    INSERT  INTO dbo.wing_master (wing_id, w_name, build_id, society_id,active_status)
                    VALUES                      (@new, dbo.InitCap(@w_name), @build_id, @society_id,0);
                END
            ELSE
                BEGIN
                    UPDATE dbo.wing_master
                    SET    w_name     = dbo.InitCap(@w_name),
                           build_id       = @build_id,
                           society_id = @society_id
                    WHERE  wing_id = @wing_id;
                END
        END
    IF @operation = 'Select'
        BEGIN
            SELECT dbo.building_master.build_id,
                   dbo.building_master.name,
                   dbo.wing_master.wing_id,
                   dbo.wing_master.w_name,
                   dbo.wing_master.society_id
            FROM   dbo.building_master
                   INNER JOIN
                   dbo.wing_master
                   ON dbo.building_master.build_id = dbo.wing_master.build_id
            WHERE  wing_id = @wing_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Delete'
        BEGIN
            Update dbo.wing_master set active_status=1
            WHERE  wing_id = @wing_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'check_name'
        BEGIN
            IF @wing_id = 0              
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.wing_master
                WHERE  lower(trim(w_name)) = lower(trim(@w_name)) and active_status=0
                       AND build_id = @build_id
                       AND society_id = @society_id;
            ELSE
                SELECT *
                FROM   dbo.wing_master
                WHERE  w_name = @w_name 
				  AND build_id = @build_id and active_status=0
                       AND wing_id = @wing_id
                       AND wing_id <> @wing_id;
        END
    IF @operation = 'Grid_Show'
        BEGIN
            SELECT   *
            FROM     global_society_view
            WHERE    society_id = @society_id and active_status=0
            ORDER BY wing_id DESC;
        END
		  IF @operation = 'FillList'
        BEGIN
           Select build_id,name from building_master where active_status=0 and society_id= @society_id 
            ORDER BY build_id DESC;
        END
    IF @operation = 'getbuilding'
        BEGIN
            SELECT build_id
            FROM   building_master
            WHERE  name = @build_name
                   AND society_id = @society_id and active_status=0;
        END
				If @operation='Search'
	   Begin
	 
	select * from global_society_view where  active_status=0 AND society_id = @society_id And( name like  @search+'%' or w_name like @search+'%')
		 ORDER BY wing_id DESC
    	End
END

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[usp_GetBillReport]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:12:28 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[usp_GetBillReport]
    @bill_id INT,
    @flat_id INT = 0,
    @society_id NVARCHAR(50) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    -- defensive variables
    DECLARE 
        @flat_count INT = 1,
        @bill_type INT = 0,
        @rate DECIMAL(18,4) = 0,
        @cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,        -- dynamic aggregated CASE columns (name/amount pairs)
        @se_select NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,   -- select list for subquery 'se'
        @sql NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
        @paramDef NVARCHAR(MAX) = N'@bill_id INT, @flat_id INT, @society_id NVARCHAR(50), @rate DECIMAL(18,4), @flat_count INT, @bill_type INT';

    ---------------------------------------------------
    -- 0. If society_id not provided, infer from flat_id
    ---------------------------------------------------
    IF @society_id IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SELECT TOP 1 @society_id = society_id
        FROM flat_master
        WHERE flat_id = @flat_id;
    END

    ---------------------------------------------------
    -- 1. Flat count (active_status = 0 = audit mode)
    ---------------------------------------------------
    SELECT @flat_count = ISNULL(COUNT(DISTINCT flat_id), 1)
    FROM flat_master
    WHERE active_status = 0
      AND society_id = @society_id;

    ---------------------------------------------------
    -- 2. bill_type for this bill
    ---------------------------------------------------
    SELECT TOP 1 @bill_type = ISNULL(bill_type, 0)
    FROM maintenance_cal
    WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;

    ---------------------------------------------------
    -- 3. rate per sqft from audit (safe TOP 1)
    ---------------------------------------------------
    SELECT TOP 1 @rate = ISNULL(a.rate_per_sqfeet, 0)
    FROM account_setting_audit AS a
    INNER JOIN bill_charges AS bc
        ON TRY_CAST(bc.setting_charges_id AS INT) = a.audit_id
    WHERE bc.bill_id = @bill_id
      AND bc.charges_type = @bill_type
      AND bc.society_id = @society_id;

    ---------------------------------------------------
    -- 4. Build dynamic charge columns (name/amount) based on maintenance_charges_audit
    --    We'll compute distinct row numbers (rn) and construct:
    --       MAX(CASE WHEN rn = X THEN charges END) AS col{X+2}_name,
    --       MAX(CASE WHEN rn = X THEN per_flat_amount END) AS col{X+2}_amount
    ---------------------------------------------------

    DECLARE @rns TABLE (rn INT PRIMARY KEY);
    INSERT INTO @rns(rn)
    SELECT DISTINCT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY mca.society_id ORDER BY mca.charges) AS rn
    FROM maintenance_charges_audit mca
    LEFT JOIN bill_charges bc
        ON bc.bill_id = @bill_id
       AND bc.society_id = @society_id
    CROSS APPLY (
        SELECT value
        FROM STRING_SPLIT(ISNULL(bc.bill_charges_id, ''), ',')
    ) AS ss
    WHERE TRY_CAST(ss.value AS INT) IS NOT NULL
      AND mca.audit_id = TRY_CAST(ss.value AS INT)
      AND mca.status = 1
      AND mca.charges_type = @bill_type
      AND mca.society_id = @society_id;

    DECLARE @max_rn INT = (SELECT ISNULL(MAX(rn), 0) FROM @rns);
    DECLARE @i INT = 1;

    IF @max_rn = 0
    BEGIN
        -- default placeholder: at least one dynamic pair (col3_name, col3_amount)
        SET @se_select = N'NULL AS col3_name, NULL AS col3_amount';
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @se_select = N'';
        WHILE @i <= @max_rn
        BEGIN
            -- the dynamic columns are offset by 2 (col1 and col2 reserved in main select)
            DECLARE @colIndex INT = @i + 2;
            IF LEN(@se_select) > 0
                SET @se_select = @se_select + N', ';

            SET @se_select = @se_select 
                + N'MAX(CASE WHEN rn = ' + CAST(@i AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N' THEN charges END) AS col' 
                + CAST(@colIndex AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N'_name, '
                + N'MAX(CASE WHEN rn = ' + CAST(@i AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N' THEN per_flat_amount END) AS col' 
                + CAST(@colIndex AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N'_amount';

            SET @i = @i + 1;
        END
    END

    ---------------------------------------------------
    -- 5. Build the main dynamic SQL (parameterized)
    --    We'll pass @bill_id, @flat_id, @society_id, @rate, @flat_count, @bill_type as params
    ---------------------------------------------------

    SET @sql = N'
    SELECT 
        DATEPART(mm, mc.m_date) AS month,
        DATENAME(mm, DATEADD(mm, DATEPART(mm, mc.m_date), -1)) AS month_name,
        DATEPART(yyyy, mc.m_date) AS year,
        ow.name AS owner_name,
        ow.flat_no,
        ow.w_name,
        mc.bill_no,
        mc.total_amount,
        ow.flat_id,
        rec.receivable_amt,
        ow.sq_ft,
        mc.amt_forward,
        mc.tax_interest_amt,
        ow.Unit,
        ow.type,
        ow.pre_mob,
        ow.bed,
        ow.email,
        mc.advance,
        t.terms,
        mc.due,
        ow.build_id,
        ow.owner_id,
        mc.gen_date,
        mc.due_date,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN ''Regular'' ELSE ''Add-on'' END AS bill_type,

        -- reserved columns
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN ''Maintenance Charges'' END AS col1_name,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN (ow.sq_ft * @rate) END AS col1_amount,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 AND parking.parking_amount IS NOT NULL THEN ''Parking Charges'' END AS col2_name,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN parking.parking_amount END AS col2_amount,

        -- dynamic audit columns (name + per_flat_amount)
        se.*,

        ow.build_name,
        ow.society_name,
        bm.address1,
        bm.address2,
        bm.print_name,
        bm.registration_no,
        mc.bill_id
    FROM maintenance_cal mc
    INNER JOIN owner_search_vw ow ON mc.flat_id = ow.flat_id
    INNER JOIN building_master bm ON ow.build_id = bm.build_id
    LEFT JOIN terms t ON ow.society_id = t.society_id

    -- parking computed per-flat (left join)
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT 
            v.flat_id, 
            SUM(
                CASE 
                    WHEN v.vehicle_type = 0 THEN ISNULL(a.two_wheeler_rate, 0)
                    WHEN v.vehicle_type = 1 THEN ISNULL(a.four_wheeler_rate, 0)
                    ELSE 0
                END
            ) AS parking_amount
        FROM dbo.Vehicle v
        LEFT JOIN dbo.account_setting_audit a
            ON v.society_id = a.society_id
        WHERE v.society_id = @society_id
          AND ISNULL(v.park_place_id, 0) <> 0
        GROUP BY v.flat_id
    ) AS parking ON parking.flat_id = mc.flat_id

    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT flat_id, SUM(ISNULL(total_amount,0)) AS receivable_amt
        FROM maintenance_cal
        WHERE m_date < DATEADD(month, DATEDIFF(month, 0, GETDATE()), 0)
        GROUP BY flat_id
    ) rec ON rec.flat_id = ow.flat_id

    LEFT JOIN (
        -- se: pivoted maintenance charges from audit, grouped by society
        SELECT society_id,  + @se_select + 
        FROM (
            SELECT 
                mca.society_id,
                mca.charges,
                CAST(mca.amount / NULLIF(@flat_count, 0) AS DECIMAL(18,2)) AS per_flat_amount,
                ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY mca.society_id ORDER BY mca.charges) AS rn
            FROM maintenance_charges_audit mca
            LEFT JOIN bill_charges bc
                ON bc.bill_id = @bill_id
               AND bc.society_id = @society_id
            CROSS APPLY (
                SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(ISNULL(bc.bill_charges_id, ''), '','')
            ) ss
            WHERE TRY_CAST(ss.value AS INT) IS NOT NULL
              AND mca.audit_id = TRY_CAST(ss.value AS INT)
              AND mca.status = 1
              AND mca.charges_type = @bill_type
              AND mca.society_id = @society_id
        ) x
        GROUP BY society_id
    ) se ON se.society_id = ow.society_id

    WHERE ow.type = ''Owner''
      AND mc.bill_id = @bill_id
      AND (
          (@flat_id = 0 AND mc.society_id = @society_id)
          OR (@flat_id <> 0 AND mc.flat_id = @flat_id)
      )';

    ---------------------------------------------------
    -- 6. Execute parameterized dynamic SQL
    ---------------------------------------------------
    EXEC sp_executesql 
        @sql,
        @paramDef,
        @bill_id = @bill_id,
        @flat_id = @flat_id,
        @society_id = @society_id,
        @rate = @rate,
        @flat_count = @flat_count,
        @bill_type = @bill_type;
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[validateuser]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:12:47 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO






CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[validateuser] 
	@operation nvarchar(50)=NULL, 
	@username varchar(250)=NULL,
	@password varchar(50)=NULL,
	@UserLoginId int=0,
	@otp_no nvarchar(50)=NULL,
	@society_id nvarchar(10)=null
AS
BEGIN


 if @operation='login'
    Begin
SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.user_id, dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id, dbo.UserType.UserTypeName, dbo.UserLogin.name, dbo.UserLogin.username, dbo.UserLogin.password, 
                         dbo.UserLogin.active_status,  dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.UserLogin.token, dbo.society_master.name AS Society_name, 
                         dbo.UserLogin.photo_path, dbo.UserLogin.village_id, dbo.village_master.name AS village_name, case when UserLogin.type=1 then 'Society' else 'Village' end as type ,USerLogin.password,owner_id
FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId LEFT OUTER JOIN
                         dbo.village_master ON dbo.UserLogin.village_id = dbo.village_master.village_id LEFT OUTER JOIN
                         dbo.society_master ON dbo.UserLogin.society_id = dbo.society_master.society_id
						 	WHERE     (UserLogin.UserName = @username) and UserLogin.active_status=0				
   End
   End

   IF @operation ='getData'
   Begin
    SELECT        dbo.UserLogin.Name, dbo.UserLogin.UserName, dbo.UserLogin.Password, dbo.UserLogin.society_id, dbo.society_master.name AS Society_name, dbo.UserLogin.active_status,token
FROM            dbo.UserLogin INNER JOIN
                         dbo.UserType ON dbo.UserLogin.user_type_id = dbo.UserType.UserTypeId INNER JOIN
                         dbo.society_master ON dbo.UserLogin.society_id = dbo.society_master.society_id
						 WHERE    dbo.UserLogin.society_id=@society_id and dbo.UserLogin.active_status=0 and dbo.society_master.active_status=0
						
   End
GO

GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[view_bill]    Script Date: 03-08-2026 17:13:05 ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[view_bill]
   @society_id NVARCHAR(10)=null,
   @bill_id INT=2,
   @flat_id INT = 7
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE 
        @cols NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
        @sql NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
        @flat_count INT,
        @bill_type INT;

    -- 1️⃣ Count flats for per-flat division
	if @society_id is null
	  begin
	  select @society_id=society_id from flat_master where flat_id=@flat_id
	  end
    SELECT @flat_count = COUNT(DISTINCT flat_id)
    FROM owner_search_vw
    WHERE flat_status = 1 
      AND type = 'Owner'
      AND society_id = @society_id;
	 
	 
   -- IF ISNULL(@flat_count, 0) = 0 SET @flat_count = 1;

    -- 2️⃣ Get bill type
    SELECT @bill_type = bill_type 
    FROM maintenance_cal 
    WHERE bill_id = @bill_id;

    -- 3️⃣ Build dynamic columns for charges
    SELECT @cols = STRING_AGG(
        CONCAT(
            'MAX(CASE WHEN rn = ', rn, ' THEN charges END) AS col', rn + 1, '_name, ',
            'MAX(CASE WHEN rn = ', rn, ' THEN per_flat_amount END) AS col', rn + 1, '_amount'
        ), ', '
    )
    FROM (
        SELECT DISTINCT rn
        FROM (
            SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY society_id ORDER BY charges) AS rn
            FROM dbo.maintenance_charges
            WHERE status = 1 AND charges_type = @bill_type
        ) s
    ) AS x;

    -- 4️⃣ If no dynamic columns, set placeholders
    IF @cols IS NULL OR LTRIM(RTRIM(@cols)) = ''
        SET @cols = 'NULL AS col2_name, NULL AS col2_amount';

    -- 5️⃣ Build main SQL
    SET @sql = N'
    SELECT 
        DATEPART(mm, mc.m_date) AS month,
        DATENAME(mm, DATEADD(mm, DATEPART(mm, mc.m_date), -1)) AS month_name,
        DATEPART(yyyy, mc.m_date) AS year,
        ow.name AS owner_name,
        ow.flat_no,
        ow.w_name,
        mc.bill_no,
        mc.total_amount,
        ow.flat_id,
        rec.receivable_amt,
        ow.sq_ft,
        mc.amt_forward,
        mc.tax_interest_amt,
        ow.Unit,
        ow.type,
        ow.pre_mob,
        ow.bed,
        ow.email,
        mc.advance,
        t.terms,
        mc.due,
        ow.build_id,
        ow.owner_id,
        mc.gen_date,
        mc.due_date,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN ''Regular'' ELSE ''Add-on'' END AS bill_type,

        -- Fixed per-sqft maintenance (col1)
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN ''Maintenance Charges'' END AS col1_name,
        CASE WHEN mc.bill_type = 1 THEN (ow.sq_ft * 2) END AS col1_amount,

        -- Dynamic expense columns (col2_name, col2_amount, etc.)
        se.*,

        ow.build_name,
        ow.society_name,
        bm.address1,
        bm.address2,
        bm.print_name,
        bm.registration_no,
        mc.bill_id
    FROM dbo.maintenance_cal AS mc
    INNER JOIN dbo.owner_search_vw AS ow ON mc.flat_id = ow.flat_id
    INNER JOIN dbo.building_master AS bm ON ow.build_id = bm.build_id
    INNER JOIN dbo.terms AS t ON ow.society_id = t.society_id

    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT flat_id, SUM(ISNULL(total_amount, 0)) AS receivable_amt
        FROM dbo.maintenance_cal
        WHERE m_date < DATEADD(month, DATEDIFF(month, 0, GETDATE()), 0)
        GROUP BY flat_id
    ) AS rec ON rec.flat_id = ow.flat_id

    -- 🔹 Dynamic pivot of maintenance charges (per-flat division applied here)
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT society_id, ' + @cols + '
        FROM (
            SELECT 
                society_id,
                charges,
                CAST(amount / NULLIF(' + CAST(@flat_count AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ', 0) AS DECIMAL(10,2)) AS per_flat_amount,
                ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY society_id ORDER BY charges) AS rn
            FROM dbo.maintenance_charges
            WHERE status = 1 AND charges_type = ' + CAST(@bill_type AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '
        ) AS e
        GROUP BY society_id
    ) AS se ON se.society_id = ow.society_id

    WHERE ow.type = ''Owner''
      AND mc.bill_id = ' + CAST(@bill_id AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '
	


      AND (
          (' + CAST(@flat_id AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' = 0 AND mc.society_id = ''' + ISNULL(@society_id, '') + ''')
          OR (' + CAST(@flat_id AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' <> 0 AND mc.flat_id = ' + CAST(@flat_id AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ')
      );';

    -- 6️⃣ Execute dynamically
    EXEC sp_executesql @sql;
END;
GO
